### [1] [Polarization of the Drosophila oocyte by active cortical exchange](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10916)
*Mohammadhossein Firouznia,Wen Lu,Margot Lakonishok,Reza Farhadifar,Vladimir I. Gelfand,Michael J. Shelley*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 果蝇卵母细胞中oskar mRNA的后极定位源于皮层交换与胞质扩散的耦合，而非单纯细胞骨架运输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前-后极性建立依赖oskar mRNA后极定位，但细胞质运输、皮层结合/解离与微管密度梯度如何协同产生稳定的后极"帽状"分布尚不清楚。

Method: 结合定量显微成像、光转换实验、实验扰动与生物物理建模，其中模型考虑了oskar-RNP的自我吸引（去混合倾向）。

Result: 后极定位由位置依赖的皮层交换耦合胞质扩散产生；决定定位模式的关键参数为oskar总量及皮层解离/结合速率比（交换平衡常数）。野生型体积分数下，测得交换常数使oskar定位于后极皮层而不发生去混合；若平衡被破坏（如提高解离速率），oskar会不可逆地去混合形成胞质液滴，与扰动实验一致。

Conclusion: 在oskar-RNP自吸引背景下，体相扩散与皮层交换可将细胞骨架不对称性转化为稳健的发育极性。

Abstract: Anterior-posterior polarization in the Drosophila oocyte requires posterior localization of oskar mRNA. oskar mRNA is produced in the anteriorly situated nurse cells, moved into the oocyte, and assembled into oskar-Staufen ribonucleoprotein particles (RNPs). These RNPs then move through the oocyte and interact with the cortex, where myosin-V binds them to the actin network and kinesin-1 removes them along cortically anchored microtubules. This cortical tug-of-war takes place against an anterior-to-posterior decrease in microtubule density. How the interplay between cytoplasmic transport and cortical binding and unbinding produces a stable posterior cap is unclear. Here, we combine quantitative microscopy, photoconversion experiments, experimental perturbations, and biophysical modeling to show that posterior localization emerges from position-dependent cortical exchange coupled to cytoplasmic diffusion. Our model, which accounts for the tendency of RNPs to demix, suggests that two key parameters that determine the observed localization pattern are the total amount of oskar and the ratio of cortical unbinding to binding rates (the exchange equilibrium constant). Given oskar volume fractions in wild-type, the measured exchange constant is such that oskar localizes to the posterior cortex without demixing. If the balance is broken, say by increasing unbinding rates, oskar irreversibly demixes into cytoplasmic droplets. This is consistent with observations from our experimental perturbations. Our results show how bulk diffusion and cortical exchange, in the face of oskar RNPs' self-attraction, convert a cytoskeletal asymmetry into robust developmental polarity.

</details>

Kirian,Jochen Küpper,Erik G. Marklund,Petra Fromme,Michael Meyer,Filipe R. N. C. Maia*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该研究利用欧洲XFEL，结合电荷还原电喷雾电离与氦气交换降低背景，从32788张单颗粒衍射图重建出1-MDa光系统I三聚体的3D电子密度，分辨率3.8 nm（受探测器几何限制）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XFEL有望实现单生物分子、病毒和细胞的超快成像，但小颗粒弱散射易被残余气体背景淹没，此前仅适用于病毒、细胞器等强散射样品。研究希望突破背景限制，实现对膜蛋白复合物的真空成像。

Method: 采用电荷还原电喷雾电离将PS I三聚体气溶胶化并注入欧洲XFEL光束，通过部分氦气交换将背景散射降低80%；采集随机取向单三聚体的32788张衍射图，并进行3D电子密度重建。

Result: 重建得到3.8 nm分辨率的3D电子密度；密度呈盘状，约22 nm宽、10 nm厚，与去垢剂胶束中PS I三聚体尺寸相符，也与真空中分子动力学模拟预测的紧缩构象及原生质谱观察一致。

Conclusion: 结果表明膜蛋白复合物可在真空中用X射线激光成像，是迈向单大分子超快衍射成像的重要一步。

Abstract: The prospect of imaging single biomolecules, viruses and cells with intense, ultrashort X-ray pulses has driven the development of X-ray free-electron lasers (XFELs). However, the weak scattering from small particles is easily swamped by background from residual gas, which has so far limited applications to strongly scattering targets such as viruses, cell organelles and cells. Here we report a three-dimensional (3D) reconstruction of an isolated 1-MDa membrane-protein complex, photosystem I (PS I), from single-particle diffraction data. PS I trimers were aerosolised by charge-reduction electrospray ionisation and injected into the European XFEL beam, with partial helium gas exchange reducing background scattering by 80%. From 32 788 diffraction patterns of single trimers in random orientations, we reconstructed the 3D electron density to a resolution of 3.8 nm, limited by the detector geometry. The disc-shaped density, about 22 nm across and 10 nm thick, matches the size of a PS I trimer in a detergent micelle and is consistent with the compaction predicted by molecular dynamics simulations of the complex in vacuo and observed in native mass spectrometry. These results show that membrane-protein complexes can be imaged in vacuo with X-ray lasers, an important step towards ultrafast diffractive imaging of single macromolecules.

</details>

Marklund,Nadia Zatsepin,Andrew V. Martin,Saša Bajt,Roger D. Kornberg,David Baker,Jochen Küpper,Henry N. Chapman*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 本研究利用X射线自由电子激光（XFEL），首次对单个生物分子复合物——巨型血红蛋白erythrocruorin（Ery）实现了单粒子成像：二维衍射图样分类可达约15 Å分辨率，三维重建约20 Å，倒易空间合并强度超过20 Å，分辨率差异主要归因于气相压缩导致的样品异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XFEL的强脉冲可突破辐射损伤限制，理论上可实现无需冷冻固定、具备飞秒至毫秒时间分辨的生物分子单粒子成像。然而该构想此前仅在大病毒颗粒上得到验证，尚未在更小的单分子复合物上实现，主要受限于X射线聚焦、样品递送、衍射探测与数据分析等环节的水平。

Method: 在数十年X射线束聚焦、粒子递送、衍射探测与先进分析算法改进的基础上，以巨型血红蛋白复合物erythrocruorin（Ery）为样本，利用XFEL脉冲进行单粒子衍射成像；对二维衍射图样进行分类重构，并进行三维图像重建与倒易空间强度合并分析。

Result: 二维衍射图样分类可重构至约15 Å分辨率；三维图像分辨率约为20 Å；倒易空间中的三维合并强度延伸超过20 Å。三维分辨率低于二维分类分辨率，可能源于气相压缩引起的复合物结构异质性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将XFEL生物单粒子成像流程从大病毒颗粒推进到单个分子复合物层面。若提高数据通量，该方法可用于质谱研究中揭示生物分子的原位结构细节；而样品递送技术的改进有望实现天然态生物单粒子的超快快照成像，突破辐射损伤限制。

Abstract: The extremely intense pulses of X-ray free-electron lasers (XFELs) have enabled imaging of radiation-sensitive samples, such as macromolecular microcrystals, beyond radiation damage limits. These sources have the potential to deliver biomolecular single-particle imaging, similar to cryo-electron microscopy but without the need for cryo-fixation and with temporal resolution from femtoseconds to milliseconds. While this possibility was recognized before XFELs were built, the biological single-particle imaging work-flow has previously only been demonstrated on large virus particles. Based on decades of improvements in X-ray beam focusing, particle delivery, diffraction detection, and advanced analysis, here we demonstrate imaging of a single molecular complex, the giant-hemoglobin erythrocruorin (Ery) with X-ray laser pulses. Two-dimensional classes of diffraction patterns could be reconstructed to 15 Angstrom resolution, and 3D images to approximately 20 Angstrom, while the 3D merged intensity in reciprocal space extended beyond 20 Angstrom. The resolution discrepancy is likely due to heterogeneity caused by gas-phase compaction of the complexes. With increased throughput, this approach could be used to reveal in-situ structural details during mass spectrometry studies of biomolecules, while improvements in sample delivery may provide ultrafast snapshot imaging of biological single-particles in their native-state beyond the limitations of radiation damage.

</details>

### [4] [Evidence-Gated Plasticity and Context-Dependent Consolidation for Multi-Goal Learning in Spiking Neural Networks](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.06.25.734613v2)
*Neymotin, S. A.,Hazan, H.,Unal, G.,Earl, C.,Anwar, H.,Franaszczuk, P.,Boothe, D.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 该研究提出证据门控可塑性（EGP）框架，利用多时间尺度可塑性与情境特异性信用分配，实现脉冲神经网络中的多目标交错学习；其目标-情境扩展版本（Target-context EGP）在学习速度与目标选择性上更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物启发的脉冲神经网络虽能提供自适应行为的机制框架，但当不同目标的突触更新相互干扰时，多目标学习变得困难。作者想验证多时间尺度可塑性与情境特异性信用分配能否改善基于内嗅-海马回路的多目标交错导航学习。

Method: 提出证据门控可塑性（EGP）：由奖励调制的STDP生成瞬时突触提议，再由需求加权的行为证据调节这些提议向持久权重的缓慢巩固过程。全局EGP跨目标汇聚所有提议与证据；目标-情境EGP（Target-context EGP）则引入目标特异的提议与证据存储，并对巩固施加额外的需求依赖缩放。在成对脉冲网络上比较两种算法，在关闭可塑性条件下测试学习到的权重，并考察上游表征与巩固各组分。

Result: 全局EGP可支持多目标学习与持久导航。目标-情境EGP在40对网络中提升早期奖励（含独立指定的30对重复实验）。在8对网络训练2000个回合中，目标-情境EGP保持奖励优势、提高目标接触成功率，并在计入失败回合时缩短到达目标的期望时间。固定权重评估显示两者均具备持久导航能力，其中8个网络中有7个偏好目标-情境EGP。组分实验复现了目标-情境的奖励优势，并识别出"情境特异巩固的额外需求依赖缩放"是唯一在调整后仍具统计支持、可改善奖励的组分。

Conclusion: EGP支持脉冲神经网络中的持久多目标学习，其目标-情境扩展在该交错导航任务中改善了习得速度与条件性目标选择性。该框架为在其他多情境学习问题中检验情境敏感、证据调制的可塑性提供了基础。

Abstract: Background / Introduction: Biologically inspired spiking neural networks offer a mechanistic framework for adaptive behavior, yet learning multiple goals remains challenging when synaptic updates for different targets interfere. We investigated whether multi-timescale plasticity and context-specific credit assignment improve interleaved multi-goal learning in a spiking navigation system inspired by entorhinal-hippocampal circuitry. Methods: We introduce evidence-gated plasticity (EGP), in which reward-modulated spike-timing-dependent plasticity (STDP) generates transient synaptic proposals and need-weighted behavioral evidence modulates their slower consolidation into durable weights. Global EGP pools these proposals and the corresponding evidence across goals. Target-context EGP extends this framework through goal-specific proposal and evidence stores and additional need-dependent scaling of consolidation. We compared the algorithms across paired spiking networks, tested learned weights with plasticity disabled, and examined upstream representations and consolidation components. Results: Global EGP supported multi-goal learning and durable navigation. Target-context EGP improved early reward across 40 paired networks, including an independently specified 30-pair replication. Across eight paired networks trained for 2000 episodes, target-context EGP maintained a reward advantage, increased target-contact success, and reduced expected time to reach the target when unsuccessful blocks were included. Independent fixed-weight evaluation demonstrated durable navigation in both algorithms, with seven of eight networks favoring target-context EGP. A component experiment reproduced the target-context reward advantage and identified additional need-dependent scaling of context-specific consolidation as the only component with adjusted statistical support for improved reward. Conclusions: EGP supports durable multi-goal learning in spiking neural networks, and its target-context extension improves acquisition and conditional target selectivity in this interleaved navigation task. The framework offers a basis for testing context-sensitive, evidence-modulated plasticity in other multi-context learning problems.

</details>


### [5] [Hunger reconfigures a reward learning circuit into a memory competent mode](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.09.17.752172v2)
*Park, A.,Senapati, B.,Mancini, N.,Zhang, Y.,Choi, J.,Cone, I.,Treiber, C. D.,Costa, R. P.,Fenk, L. M.,Bidaye, S. S.,Waddell, S.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 饥饿通过肽能信号抑制多巴胺能基线活动，使奖赏诱发的大振幅脉冲成为可能，从而促进学习；进食后持续的大振幅脉冲将记忆网络切换到优先巩固的模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内部状态（如饥饿）会重塑神经回路活动以支持觅食行为，神经调质可调控神经元的生理特性，但这种灵活性如何调控记忆网络尚不清楚。本文旨在揭示饥饿如何重构果蝇多巴胺能食物奖赏回路，进而影响学习与记忆巩固。

Method: 以果蝇为模型，研究多巴胺能食物奖赏回路在饥饿与饱食状态下的两种生理模式；通过肽能信号、糖摄取、多巴胺能神经元放电模式（基线活动、大振幅脉冲）及突触后输出神经元活动（紧张性去相关模式 vs 爆发性相关模式）的分析，结合行为学学习与记忆测试。

Result: 饥饿通过肽能信号抑制多巴胺能基线活动，使奖赏诱发的大振幅多巴胺能脉冲得以强化学习；该脉冲模式可由糖摄取触发并在进食后持续，反映饱食状态。持续的大振幅脉冲强化学习并使记忆网络转向优先巩固新近获得记忆的模式。突触后输出神经元从对多巴胺敏感的紧张性、去相关模式，转变为爆发性、相关且多巴胺调制被遮蔽的模式。

Conclusion: 营养剥夺将记忆回路重构为具备学习能力、对多巴胺敏感的模式，而持续强化性多巴胺随后将其切换为驱动记忆巩固的饱食模式。这些过程通过生理模式的转变，将状态依赖的奖赏信号与随后的记忆稳定机制在机制上交织在一起。

Abstract: Internal states such as hunger dynamically reshape activity across circuits to support resource seeking. Neuromodulation provides a means of controlling such physiological properties of neurons, but how this flexibility regulates memory networks remains unclear. Here, we describe how hunger reconfigures a dopaminergic food-reward circuit between two physiological modes that support memory formation, in Drosophila. Starvation suppresses baseline dopaminergic activity through peptidergic signalling, enabling reward-evoked, large-amplitude dopamine neuron spikes to reinforce learning. This spiking mode can be engaged by sugar consumption and persists beyond feeding, reflecting the fly's satiety state. Persistent dopaminergic large-amplitude spiking reinforces learning and transitions the memory network into a mode that prioritizes consolidation of recently acquired memories. The transition from hunger to satiation is also reflected in the activity of postsynaptic output neurons that shift from a tonic, decorrelated mode that is responsive to dopamine into a bursting, correlated mode in which further dopaminergic modulation is occluded. Therefore, nutrient deprivation reconfigures the memory circuit into a learning competent dopamine-receptive mode, which persistent reinforcing dopamine then switches into a satiated mode driving memory consolidation. Together these processes mechanistically intertwine state-dependent reward-signalling with subsequent memory stabilization through transitions in physiological mode.

</details>


### [6] [Inhibitory budget matching constrains homeostatic plasticity in competitive spiking networks](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.09.27.754763v2)
*Musacchio, F.,Fuhrmann, M.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 研究在竞争性STDP脉冲网络中，用多种抑制可塑性规则替代固定侧抑制，发现稳定塑形竞争需同时满足合理工作区间与"抑制预算匹配"，预算匹配可使局部抑制权重自适应而不破坏表征学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮层抑制性突触具有可塑性并参与兴奋-抑制平衡，但Diehl-Cook等竞争性STDP网络通常采用固定侧抑制。目前尚不清楚在何种条件下具有生物学依据的抑制可塑性可替代固定竞争而不损害表征学习。

Method: 在MNIST上训练竞争性STDP网络，比较固定抑制与三类塑性规则：Vogels式抑制性STDP、慢速稳态抑制、以及守恒每个兴奋神经元总抑制输入的预算约束慢稳态规则；通过固定抑制工作点图选取参考区间（g=2.0, wIE=10.0），并绘制参数图与预算扫描进行比较。

Result: 固定抑制运行仅56%完成训练，高增益强抑制导致活动失控；参考区间实现完全稳定且分类精度84.3±0.6%。该区间内预算匹配（BI=3990）的约束规则全部稳定，精度83.5±0.6%，放电率与抑制电导统计接近固定抑制；预算失配（BI=7980）全部失控。参数图显示Vogels式iSTDP稳定性90%、慢稳态抑制96%；预算约束稳定性强烈依赖预算值。

Conclusion: 竞争性STDP网络中的抑制可塑性不能仅由局部稳态更新决定，稳定塑形竞争依赖工作区间与固定总竞争强度的抑制预算。预算匹配将抑制权重的局部再分配与总抑制驱动变化分离，避免塑性使网络偏离稳定竞争态，可作为以可塑、稳态调控竞争替代固定抑制的设计原则。

Abstract: Competitive spiking networks can form sensory representations through local synaptic plasticity and lateral inhibition. A canonical example is the Diehl-Cook architecture, in which Poisson-encoded sensory inputs drive excitatory neurons through spike-timing-dependent plasticity (STDP), while a separate inhibitory population imposes competition between excitatory units. In most implementations this competitive circuit is fixed, although inhibitory synapses are plastic in cortical circuits and contribute to excitation-inhibition balance. It therefore remains unclear under which conditions biologically motivated inhibitory plasticity can replace fixed lateral competition without disrupting representation learning. Here we study this question in a competitive STDP network trained on MNIST. We compare fixed inhibition with a Vogels-style inhibitory STDP rule, slow homeostatic inhibition, and a budget-constrained slow homeostatic rule that conserves the total inhibitory input received by each excitatory neuron. A fixed-inhibition operating-point map showed that stability depends on the dynamical regime: 56% of tested fixed-inhibition runs completed training, whereas stronger inhibition at higher input gain produced runaway activity. The reference regime g = 2.0, wIE = 10.0, selected for subsequent comparisons, combined complete stability across replicate initializations with high classification performance (84.3 {+/-} 0.6% after 30,000 training examples). In this regime, the budget-constrained rule was stable in all replicate runs when its inhibitory budget matched the fixed-inhibition reference (BI = 3990), reaching 83.5 {+/-} 0.6% accuracy with firing-rate and inhibitory-conductance statistics close to fixed inhibition, whereas a mismatched budget (BI = 7980) produced runaway activity in every replicate. Parameter maps showed that Vogels-derived iSTDP was broadly but not uniformly stable (90% runs completed), while slow homeostatic inhibition was even more broadly stable (96%). Budget-constrained stability depended strongly on the conserved budget, i.e., low and matched budgets were stable or nearly stable, intermediate budgets were mixed, and BI = 7980 failed in all tested runs. These results show that inhibitory plasticity in competitive STDP networks is not governed by a local homeostatic update alone. This identifies inhibitory budget matching as a practical design principle for replacing fixed inhibition with plastic, homeostatically regulated competition in STDP-based spiking networks.

We assessed the protein expression of a top DE candidate, the calcium-binding protein Calbindin (Calb1), across transplant conditions and developmental stages. ### [8] [Environmental dynamics shape human learning: change points versus random walks](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.1101/2025.11.26.690700v2)
*Foucault, C.,Weber, L. A.,Hunt, L. T.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应学习需要结合环境动态来解释预测误差，但环境不仅在"何时变化"上不同，还在"如何变化"上不同：有的过程逐渐漂移，有的则突然变化。这引出一个核心问题——人类能否根据环境的生成结构来调整学习。

Method: 采用预测推断任务开展两项行为实验，比较两种典型非平稳环境：突变变化点与渐变随机游走；构建统一贝叶斯框架，并进行交叉验证的模型比较，考察人类表观学习率随预测误差幅度变化的动态特征，以及"桨宽"与"桨位"如何协同调整以追踪潜在方差与均值。

Result: 交叉验证的模型比较显示，与环境匹配的模型能解释几乎所有参与者的行为；表观学习率呈现动态特定特征，即在变化点环境下随预测误差幅度上升得更陡；参与者在两种环境中都通过与潜在均值联合的机制在线追踪变化的潜在方差（环境随机性），表现为桨宽与桨位的协调调整；在变化点环境中，桨宽调整还能进一步区分方差变化与均值变化。

Conclusion: 人类能够根据潜在状态"如何变化"以及"哪些属性在变化"来调整学习，研究为跨不同环境动态的自适应学习提供了统一的计算解释。

Abstract: Adaptive learning requires interpreting prediction errors in light of environmental dynamics. However, environments may differ not only in when changes occur, but also in how they occur: some nonstationary processes drift gradually, whereas others change abruptly. This raises the question of whether humans can adapt their learning to the generative structure of their environment. Here, we compared learning in two canonical nonstationary environments: abrupt change points versus gradual random walks. Across two behavioral experiments using a predictive inference task and a unified Bayesian framework, we show that participants adjust their learning to the dynamics of their environment. Cross-validated model comparison showed that the environment-matched model best explained behavior in nearly all participants. Human apparent learning rates also showed the predicted dynamics-specific signature, increasing more sharply with prediction-error magnitude under change points compared to random walks. Participants tracked the changing latent variance (environmental stochasticity) online in both environments, jointly with the latent mean, through coordinated adjustments of paddle width and location. In the change-point environment, their width adjustments further distinguished changes in variance from changes in mean. Together, these results show that humans adapt their learning to both how latent states change and which properties are changing. They establish a unified computational account of adaptive learning across different environmental dynamics.

</details>

Abstract: The mammalian neocortex is a complex structure with many interacting regions and cell types. Multiple efforts have recently generated large datasets from multiple cortical regions to determine generalizing principles of cortical organization and activity. One such generalizing principle is the concept of a cortical hierarchy - low-dimensional macroscopic gradients of biological properties across cortical areas and a hierarchy of timescales for information processing. Such gradients in properties along a cortical hierarchy may explain how different cortical regions serve different computational roles (i.e. sensory processing versus working memory). We set out to investigate such principles using biologically realistic simulations. Starting from our previously published model of the mouse primary visual cortex (VISp) with updated connectivity reflecting recent large-scale connectomics and synaptic physiology datasets, we generated multiple versions of the cortical circuit model with increased connectivity as measured along the cortical hierarchy. We then measure the resulting neural activity in response to a simulated single-cell photostimulation paradigm and thalamocortical inputs. We found that overall circuit stability increased across the cortical hierarchy, due to both increases in connection density and increases in the relative proportion of SST cells. The results of this study link cortical cell types to population-level activity, generating insight into the function of local circuits across cortex.

Recent single-cell transcriptomic datasets have identified several potential genes that may regulate cellular diversity, but the underlying mechanisms have not been investigated. For example, the transcription factor Meis2 is selectively expressed in the GABAergic subclass of ACs. In this study, we focused on how Meis2 regulates the development of GABAergic ACs. We hypothesized that Meis2 is required for specification and survival of GABAergic ACs. To test this, we used the Meis2 conditional knockout mouse (Meis2-CKO) where Meis2 was deleted from either the entire retina or specifically from AC precursor cells. The deletion of Meis2 led to a ~60% reduction in total GABAergic ACs in the retina. This loss of GABAergic ACs occurred only after postnatal day 5 (P5), suggesting Meis2 is not required for early specification but may be required for survival. To further investigate cell death and survival, we quantified cleaved caspase-3 positive cells in the retina at several developmental timepoints. In Meis2-CKO mice, we found an increase in cleaved caspase-3 immunolabeling at P5 and P6 timepoints coinciding with the reduction of GABAergic ACs. Further analysis of GABAergic AC subtypes demonstrated a subtype-specific cell death in Meis2-CKO retinas. For example, the total number of dopaminergic (TH) ACs were significantly reduced, whereas starburst (ChAT) ACs remained unchanged. These findings suggest that Meis2 is important for the survival of a subset of GABAergic AC subtypes in the mammalian retina. In addition, Meis2 may control the expression of specific survival factors that prevent apoptosis during developmental cell death in the retina.

Abstract: The ventral subiculum (vSub), a major output node of the hippocampal formation, has been implicated in diverse neurological disorders. Disruption of excitatory-inhibitory balance is a common feature of neurological disorders, with altered GABAergic signaling representing a critical component. More recently, the vSub has gained increasing attention as a component of neural circuits underlying emotional and motivational regulation, through its extensive connections with regions implicated in mental health disorders such as addiction and anxiety. Our previous work in models of alcohol use disorder and comorbid anxiety identified an important role for GABAergic inhibition in regulating vSub excitability, prompting us to investigate the functional consequences of recruiting the broader local GABAergic population. Although previous studies have examined individual interneuron subtypes, the network consequences of activating the global population of local GABAergic neurons remain poorly understood. To address this question, we expressed channelrhodopsin using an mDlx-dependent strategy to target GABAergic neurons in the ventral hippocampus and performed in vivo electrophysiological recordings from vSub neurons in anesthetized rats. Repeated activation of this GABAergic population at 20 Hz, a stimulation frequency commonly used in behavioral optogenetic studies, produced robust suppression of pyramidal neuron firing during stimulation. This direct inhibition was accompanied by network-dependent increases in post-stimulus activity that varied as a function of baseline activity, sex, and trial number. Complementary ex vivo electrophysiological experiments further supported network-dependent effects and revealed short-term synaptic plasticity that evolved across repeated stimulation trials, reflecting contributions from both pre- and postsynaptic mechanisms. Together, these findings demonstrate that recruitment of the broader GABAergic population produces effects extending beyond direct inhibition and can engage dynamic network responses that depend on stimulation history and circuit state. These findings highlight the importance of stimulation frequency, timing, and network context when interpreting the functional consequences of GABAergic circuit manipulation.

*Arioli, M.,Federici, A.,Silvestri, V.,Santini, M.,Colombo, L.,Gianni, M. L.,Bottari, D.,Macchi Cassia, V.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 该研究考察41名健康足月新生儿（出生24–72小时）在两种时间结构不同的听觉刺激下非营养性吸吮（NNS）的变化，通过幅度平方相干分析发现新生儿吸吮行为能有选择地与刺激特定的时间规律对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 感觉运动同步被认为是随发育逐渐出现的能力，但其起源尚不清楚。作者希望探究新生儿在出生后最初几天是否已能调节吸吮行为并与听觉刺激的时间结构同步。

Method: 41名健康足月新生儿（24–72小时龄）暴露于两段各3分钟的听觉刺激流：一段为时间规律稳定的高结构流，一段为时间变异性更大的高变异流；同时连续记录非营养性吸吮（NNS）。评估NNS频率与压力的变化，并用幅度平方相干（magnitude-squared coherence）在各刺激流特征频率上量化NNS与声音的耦合。

Result: 结构化刺激促进更有序的NNS（吸吮爆发更多）并增强吸吮力度（第一分钟内吸吮峰值速率较变异流短暂升高，吸吮压力较静默条件更大）。相干分析显示，无论结构化还是高变异刺激，相干性均在各自特征频率处选择性升高。

Conclusion: 新生儿在出生数小时内，NNS行为即可有选择地与刺激特定的时间规律对齐，即便这些规律可预测性弱且缺乏主导周期性。这种早期提取时间结构并协调运动行为的能力，可能为后续感觉运动同步的发展奠定基础。

Abstract: Sensorimotor synchronization, the ability to align motor behaviour with the temporal structure of external stimuli, is thought to emerge gradually during development, but its origins remain unclear. Here, we investigated whether newborns can adapt their sucking behaviour and synchronise it to the temporal structure of auditory stimuli within the first days of life. Forty-one healthy full-term newborns (24-72 hours old) were exposed to two three-minute auditory streams differing in temporal structure: a highly structured stream with a stable temporal pattern and a highly variant stream with greater temporal variability, while their non-nutritive sucking (NNS) was continuously recorded. We assessed changes in NNS frequency and pressure and quantified NNS-sound coupling using magnitude-squared coherence at frequencies distinctive to each auditory stream. Structured stimulation promoted a more organised NNS, with more suck bursts than the variant stream, and increased sucking vigour, with a transiently higher suck peak rate during the first minute relative to the variant stream and greater suck pressure relative to silence. Coherence analyses revealed that coherence was selectively higher at the frequencies distinctive to each auditory stream, both for the structured and the highly variant stimulus. These findings show that, within hours of birth, NNS behaviour can selectively align with stimulus-specific temporal regularities, even when these are weakly predictable and lack a dominant periodicity. This early capacity to extract temporal structure and coordinate motor behaviour may provide a foundation for the subsequent development of sensorimotor synchronisation.

</details>


### [15] [Global statistical constraint and local partner-sensitive inference support dynamic, open-ended verbal coordination](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.10.03.756413v1)
*Zhang, T.,Shaw, D. J.,Zhou, S.,Zhu, L.,Lu, C.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 该研究结合预测性深度语言模型与认知计算建模，提出开放式言语交流中的对齐源于"全局语义约束+伙伴敏感局部调整"的计算分工，并用fNIRS超扫描实验与跨模型、跨语言验证加以支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效的言语交流需要双方通过精确的言语使用对齐彼此的交际意图，但这一过程在动态、开放式交流中如何实现尚不清楚。作者希望厘清交流中统计性（上下文先验）与语用性（伙伴推断）计算的各自作用及其神经基础。

Method: 让30组被试完成动态、开放式的协作词语生成任务，同时进行fNIRS超扫描；用主动推断启发的更新规则构建统计—语用混合模型，形式化局部调整；以深度语言模型提供上下文选择先验，比较多种模型对词汇对齐的解释力；分析个体内与伙伴间的假设空间与局部选择不确定性变化，并做神经相关分析；在多个语言模型与独立捷克语数据集上做泛化验证。

Result: 被试的词汇选择趋于收敛，词汇对齐最佳地由融合上下文选择先验与交互特异性局部调整的混合模型解释。交互并非单纯降低不确定性：个体内全局语义搜索的假设空间逐步收窄，而局部备选项之间的不确定性上升；伙伴间全局假设空间与局部选择均愈发对齐，其中局部选择优先级的对齐最能预测后续词汇对齐。局部调整的贡献在精确言语交流中强于控制任务。神经层面，颞顶与颞前区分别支持伙伴相关推断与语义预测误差，思考期的活动预测说话期与自我更新相关的额下活动；自身假设景观表征于左顶下—缘上回皮层。

Conclusion: 开放式交流中的对齐源于统计计算与语用计算的互补分工：全局上下文约束塑造语义搜索空间，伙伴敏感的局部调整调节选择优先级，二者共同构成动态假设景观；该框架可推广至不同语言模型与语言，为理解人类及人—AI交流提供了理论工具。

Abstract: Effective verbal communication requires individuals to align their communicative intentions through the precise use of utterances, yet how this is achieved in dynamic, open-ended communication remains unclear. Combining predictive deep language models (DLMs) with cognitive computational modeling, we tested a computational division of labor between context-sensitive global constraint on the utterance space and partner-sensitive local adjustment of selection priorities. We formalized local adjustment using an active inference inspired updating rule within a statistical-pragmatic hybrid model. Thirty dyads performed a dynamic, open-ended collaborative word-generation task during functional near infrared spectroscopy (fNIRS) hyperscanning. Interlocutors converged in their lexical choices, and lexical alignment was best explained by a hybrid model integrating contextual selection priors with interaction-specific local adjustment among the models tested. This hybrid process formed a dynamic hypothesis landscape comprising a global semantic search region and local selection priorities. Critically, interaction did not simply reduce uncertainty: within individuals, the hypothesis space of global semantic search progressively narrowed while local uncertainty among plausible alternatives increased; between partners, both global hypothesis space and local selection became increasingly aligned, with alignment of local selection priorities most strongly predicting subsequent lexical alignment. The contribution of local adjustment was stronger during precise verbal communication than in the control tasks. Neural analyses further identified correlates of the local adjustment process: temporoparietal and anterior temporal regions supported partner-related inference and semantic prediction error, respectively, and their activity during thinking predicted subsequent inferior frontal activity associated with self-updating during speaking. The participant's own hypothesis landscape was also represented in the left inferior parietal-supramarginal cortex. The main findings generalized across language models and an independent Czech dataset. Together, these findings reveal that alignment in open-ended communication emerges from complementary statistical and pragmatic computations, providing a framework for understanding human and human-AI communication.

</details>


### [16] [The anterior insular cortex mediates higher-order threat learning and memory](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.10.09.757763v1)
*Namkung, H.,Mukherjee, B.,de Chabot, J.,Lockhart, S.,Guttman, L.,Isehak, I.,Clark, A.,Saito, A.,Miura, I.,Ishizuka, K.,Kwon, H.,Sawa, A.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 前岛叶皮层（AIC）第5层投射至基底外侧杏仁核的兴奋性锥体神经元，是高级威胁学习/记忆所必需的，并对恐惧记忆的形成与表达实施双向调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 了解潜在威胁对生存至关重要，其功能缺陷是多种神经精神疾病的基础。人类脑成像提示前岛叶皮层（AIC）是威胁学习的关键神经相关物，但其机制性作用尚不清楚。

Method: 采用多模态系统神经科学方法，对AIC兴奋性神经元（尤其是投射至基底外侧杏仁核的第5层锥体神经元）进行功能解析，并比较不同威胁学习范式（预测线索与厌恶事件存在时间间隔、预测信息嵌入弥散情境，以及基本的、时间连续的威胁学习）中的作用。

Result: AIC兴奋性神经元、特别是投射至基底外侧杏仁核的第5层锥体神经元，是高级威胁学习/记忆所必需的：当预测线索与厌恶事件在时间上分离，或预测信息嵌入弥散情境时特异性地需要AIC；而对基本的、时间连续的威胁学习AIC并非必需。此外，AIC虽促进记忆形成，却在提取阶段以情境依赖的方式限制恐惧表达水平，这种双向控制由不同AIC神经元集群的招募所介导。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了AIC对高级威胁学习/记忆进行双向调控的新型环路机制。

Abstract: Learning about potential threats is indispensable for survival, and its deficits underlie numerous neuropsychiatric disorders. Human brain imaging suggests the anterior insular cortex (AIC) as a key neural correlate of threat learning, yet its mechanistic role remains unclear. Leveraging multimodal systems neuroscience approaches, we demonstrate that AIC excitatory neurons, specifically layer 5 pyramidal neurons projecting to the basolateral amygdala, are essential for higher-order threat learning/memory. They are specifically required when a predictor is temporally separated from an aversive event or when the predictive information is embedded within a diffuse context. In contrast, the AIC is dispensable for elementary, temporally contiguous threat learning. Surprisingly, while the AIC promotes memory formation, it also constrains the level of fear expression during retrieval in a context-dependent manner. This bidirectional control is mediated by the recruitment of distinct AIC neuronal ensembles. Together, we identify a novel circuit mechanism for bidirectional regulation of higher-order threat learning/memory.

</details>


### [17] [Disrupting the Right Inferior Frontal Gyrus Increases Social Brain Activity During Emotion Recognition](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.10.08.757563v1)
*Kucuk, E.,Gofton, H.,Smith, C.,Kaiser, D.,Pitcher, D.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 结合TMS与fMRI发现，短暂干扰右侧额下回（IFG）会增强社会脑网络多个远端节点（双侧颞极、楔前叶、内侧前额叶）的神经活动，表明IFG自上而下控制机制的破坏会改变社会脑网络的远端加工。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经影像研究已识别出支持情绪识别与社会认知的专门化社会脑网络，神经心理学患者研究也表明该网络中负责自上而下控制的右侧IFG受损会损害面部表情识别，但右侧IFG的短暂干扰如何影响更广泛的社会脑网络尚不清楚。

Method: 将被试（n=20）的经颅磁刺激（TMS）与功能磁共振成像（fMRI）结合；在两次扫描中完成延迟匹配样本情绪识别任务，目标表情（高兴或恐惧）前呈现一致或不一致的词线索；每次扫描前对右侧IFG或顶点对照位点施加theta-burst TMS（会话顺序在被试间平衡）；进行组水平全脑分析。

Result: 对IFG施加TMS使社会脑网络多个区域的神经活动增强，包括双侧颞极、楔前叶和内侧前额叶皮层（mPFC）。

Conclusion: 定位于IFG的自上而下控制机制被干扰后，会改变社会脑网络远端节点的神经加工，这为理解人们如何识别与理解他人情绪提供了更广泛的背景。

Abstract: Neuroimaging studies have identified a specialised social brain network that supports emotion recognition and social cognition. This network performs a range of functions that include facial expression recognition and inferring the mental states of others. Studies of neuropsychological patients report that damage to the right inferior frontal gyrus (IFG), the top-down control area in this network, impairs facial expression recognition. To investigate how transient disruption of the right IFG affects the wider social brain we combined transcranial magnetic stimulation (TMS) with functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI). Participants (n=20) were scanned across two sessions while performing a delayed match-to-sample emotion recognition task in which happy or fearful expressions were preceded by a congruent or incongruent word cue. Prior to each scan, thetaburst TMS was delivered over the right IFG or over the vertex control site (session order was balanced across participants). A group whole-brain analysis revealed that TMS to the IFG increased neural activity in multiple regions of the social brain network. These included the bilateral temporal pole, precuneus and medial prefrontal cortex (mPFC). This pattern of results shows that the disruption of top-down control mechanisms localised in the IFG alters the neural processing in remote nodes of the social brain network. Our findings are discussed within the wider context of how we recognise and understand the emotions of other people.

</details>

### [18] [A Closer Look at Agentic BBO: Benchmarking LLM Agents for Black-Box Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12183)
*Ming Chen,Rong-Xi Tan,Ke Xue,Yu-Jie Zhou,Taiye Lu,Zhi-Xuan Gao,Peng Xie,Zijun Shen,Chen Lu,Haopu Shang,Chao Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出跨域基准 AgenticBBO-Bench，统一评估 LLM 智能体在黑盒优化中的表现，并分析工具、任务信息与 LLM 角色三因素影响，最后给出五任务前沿挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒优化中目标评估昂贵且有限，LLM 智能体结合任务语义、计算与优化工具展现出潜力，但已有研究使用不同任务域与系统配置，结果难以比较，单个设计选择的效果也无法隔离。

Method: 构建 AgenticBBO-Bench 跨域基准，覆盖合成函数、超参数优化、数据库调优、芯片设计与分子设计，采用统一的有限预算评估协议；实验比较智能体式 BBO 与直接 LLM 方法及最佳数值优化器；并系统研究优化工具、任务信息与先验知识、搜索中 LLM 角色三因素；在 Codex 智能体框架下评估七个 LLM。

Result: 智能体式 BBO 在五个域上的族平均得分均高于直接 LLM 方法，并在四个域上超过最佳数值优化器；额外数值工具并不稳定提升性能；任务语义普遍有用而更具体的先验较不可靠；数值优化器可有效吸收智能体搜索轨迹带来的增益；GPT-6 Astra 与 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 位于性能-成本帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: AgenticBBO-Bench 提供了可比较、可隔离设计因素的统一评估平台，表明智能体式 BBO 具有跨域优势，但工具与先验的增益需谨慎对待，后续可基于该基准推进智能体黑盒优化研究。

Abstract: Black-box optimization (BBO) arises in many scientific and engineering problems where objective evaluations are expensive and limited. Recent large language model (LLM) agents offer a new way to approach BBO by combining task semantics, computation, optimization tools, and feedback-driven decision making, showing great potential due to the integration with mathematically rigorous tools. However, existing agentic BBO studies use different task domains and system configurations, making their results difficult to compare and the effects of individual design choices hard to isolate. We therefore introduce AgenticBBO-Bench, a cross-domain benchmark for agentic BBO spanning synthetic functions, hyperparameter optimization, database tuning, chip design, and molecular design under a unified finite-budget evaluation protocol. In our experiments, agentic BBO achieves higher family-averaged scores than direct LLM-based methods in all five domains and outperforms the best numerical optimizers in four. We further study three factors shaping agent performance: optimization tools, task information and prior knowledge, and the role of the LLM during search. Our results show that additional numerical tools do not consistently improve performance, task semantics are broadly useful while more specific priors are less reliable, and numerical optimizers can effectively absorb gains from search trajectories established by the agent. Finally, we introduce a five-task frontier challenge within AgenticBBO-Bench and evaluate seven LLMs under the Codex agent harness, where GPT-6 Astra and DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash lie on the Pareto frontier of performance and cost among the evaluated models. Our code is available at https://github.com/lamda-bbo/agentic-bbo.

</details>


### [19] [Freeze the Decoder, Heal the Encoder: Parameter-Efficient Adaptation for SVD-Based KV-Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10552)
*Yufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在共享学习率下比较不同可训练参数量PEFT方案会产生假优势；改为逐臂调参后，"仅训练encoder"的SVD KV-cache压缩愈合方案与替代方案真实持平，但省3倍参数量与优化器状态内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复盘/评测常用"单一共享学习率"比较多臂微调方案。当各臂可训练参数量差异大时，共享学习率会同时压低大参数量臂的均值并抬高其方差，从而制造出最小参数量臂"多随机种子显著占优"的假象。作者旨在揭示并纠正这一混淆。

Method: 以基于SVD的事后KV-cache压缩为具体场景：将已预训练模型的key/value权重分解为下投影（encoder）与上投影（decoder），得到低秩（类MLA）缓存，再用短期微调（healing）恢复截断导致的精度损失。对比三种healing臂：冻结decoder仅训练encoder、仅训练decoder、两者都训练。关键做法是对每一臂分别调优学习率（per-arm LR tuning），每配置3个随机种子。主实验在视觉语言模型Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct上（单一压缩比），并在纯文本测试床、两个backbone上复现。

Result: 共享学习率下，"仅healing encoder"看似明显优于healing decoder或两者；逐臂调参后该表面优势消失，encoder-only与替代方案持平，同时实测确实节省3倍可训练参数量与3倍优化器状态内存。

Conclusion: encoder-only healing是低内存的即插即用配方，可用于训练期改装低秩KV-cache压缩；同时，"共享学习率陷阱"是一项警示性结论：当被比较的微调臂可训练参数量不同时，必须逐臂调参，否则结论可能不成立。

Abstract: Comparing parameter-efficient fine-tuning recipes under a single, shared learning rate is a common but flawed practice: when the arms being compared have very different trainable-parameter counts, a shared rate can simultaneously depress the larger arms' means and inflate their variance, manufacturing a large, seemingly multi-seed-significant advantage for the smallest arm that is not a real effect. We document this confound in a concrete setting: post-hoc SVD-based KV-cache compression, where an already-pretrained model is converted to a low-rank (multi-head-latent-attention-style) cache by factorizing its key/value weights into a down-projection ("encoder") and an up-projection ("decoder"), after which a short fine-tune ("healing") recovers the accuracy lost to truncation. Under a shared learning rate, freezing the decoder and healing only the encoder looks like a clear win over healing the decoder or both factors; once every arm is given its own tuned learning rate, that apparent advantage disappears, and encoder-only healing instead reaches parity with the alternatives, at a real, measured saving of 3x fewer trainable parameters and 3x less optimizer-state memory. We verify this parity with per-arm learning-rate tuning and three seeds per configuration on a vision-language model (Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct), at the one compression ratio this protocol covers, and replicate it on a text-only testbed across two backbones. Encoder-only healing is therefore a lower-memory drop-in recipe for retrofitting low-rank KV-cache compression at training time, and the shared-learning-rate pitfall we document and correct is a cautionary result for comparing any fine-tuning recipes whose arms differ in trainable-parameter count.

</details>


### [20] [SPERA: Spherical Prior EEG Foundation Model with Geometry- and Frequency-Aware Latent Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10571)
*Minsu Kim,Ye-Sung Kim,Hyeseong Jeon,Wooseok Hyung,Joshua Lee,Chang-Hwan Im*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPERA 是一个基于联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）的脑电基础模型，在潜空间中预测而非重建信号，引入 Legendre 多项式空间先验编码电极几何，配合因子化时空注意力与关系谱正则化，在大规模预训练后于九项下游任务取得最高平均平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG 可无创测量神经活动，但受试者、设备与电极蒙太奇（montage）高度异质，构建通用 EEG 模型困难。现有 EEG 基础模型多依赖在观测信号上的重建目标，而观测信号同时包含神经与非神经成分，导致学习目标被非神经成分干扰，难以学到可迁移的神经表征。

Method: 提出 SPERA（Spherical Prior EEG Representation Architecture），采用 JEPA 在潜空间进行预测。其核心组件包括：(1) Legendre 多项式空间先验，嵌入注意力机制以编码多变的头皮电极几何；(2) 因子化的时间注意力与空间注意力，并与周期性全注意力块交错；(3) 关系谱正则化器，使潜空间相似性结构与谱视图对齐。预训练数据规模约 80,000 小时 EEG，覆盖 106 个数据集、29,048 名受试者。

Result: 在涵盖临床、认知与脑机接口（BCI）应用的九项下游任务上，SPERA 取得最高平均平衡准确率；在线性探测（linear probing）下表现出强参数效率，并在不同记录条件下保持稳健性。

Conclusion: SPERA 有望成为面向多样化 EEG 分析的通用骨干模型，其潜空间预测范式与空间先验设计为异构 EEG 建模提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) provides a non-invasive measure of ongoing neural activity, but building general-purpose EEG models remains challenging due to the heterogeneity of subjects, devices, and electrode montages. Existing EEG foundation models predominantly rely on reconstruction-based objectives defined on the observed signal, which contains both neural and non-neural components. We introduce SPERA (Spherical Prior EEG Representation Architecture), an EEG foundation model that adopts the joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) to predict in latent space. SPERA introduces a Legendre-polynomial spatial prior, incorporated into attention to encode varying scalp electrode geometries. Two further components adapt the model to EEG: factorized temporal and spatial attention interleaved with periodic full-attention blocks, and a relational spectral regularizer aligning latent similarity structure with spectral views. Pretrained on approximately 80,000 hours of EEG from 29,048 subjects across 106 datasets, SPERA achieves the highest average balanced accuracy across nine downstream tasks spanning clinical, cognitive, and BCI applications. SPERA further exhibits strong parameter efficiency under linear probing and robustness across varying recording conditions, suggesting its potential as a general-purpose backbone for diverse EEG analyses.

</details>


### [21] [Coverage, Not Difficulty, Sets How Much Synthetic Data an Activation Probe Needs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10594)
*Ankush Checkervarty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究激活探针（activation probe）在监测语言模型时所需的合成样本量问题。通过对三种监测概念（高风险情境、有害回复、不遵循指令）在多个评估分布和探针模型上的学习曲线分析，发现所需样本量由"覆盖度"而非"单类难度"决定，且不同概念间样本迁移程度差异显著，导致"广度优于深度"的建议需按概念区分对待。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活探针通常在合成对话上训练，但训练一个探针究竟需要多少样本尚不明确。先前工作建议将生成预算花在"广度"（更多种类的数据）而非"深度"（每类更多样本）上，但这一建议是否对所有监测概念普遍适用，缺乏系统性验证。作者希望厘清：探针所需样本量由什么因素决定，以及不同监测概念间是否存在系统性差异。

Method: 作者对三种监测概念（高风险情境、对人有害的回复、不遵循用户指令）追踪学习曲线，样本量从10到590个合成样本；在14个留出评估分布和4个探针模型上进行实验，并变换生成器LLM和提示细节。将概念所需的"深度"定义为拟合曲线的半增益规模（half-gain size，即获得一半增益所需样本数）。此外使用来自dev集的真实样本进行验证，并分析概念与分布、生成器、探针模型、提示细节对半增益规模方差的贡献。

Result: 在Gemma-3-27B-IT上，高风险和有害概念的探针从80个样本起就接近其平台期（差距仅百分之几），指令探针则需要数倍样本，该顺序在三个更小的探针模型及真实样本上均成立。概念和分布解释了42-45%的半增益规模方差，而生成器、探针模型和提示细节合计不到10%。决定半增益规模的是覆盖度而非单类难度：每个评估分布对应的"种类"中位半增益规模在三种概念下均为7-11个同类合成样本。概念间的差异主要来自样本跨类迁移程度：高风险下几乎完全迁移，有害次之，指令最低。

Conclusion: "广度优于深度"的建议应按概念区别对待：在指令监测下，各类之间几乎不迁移，需要覆盖许多种类；而在高风险监测下，一类样本即可覆盖其余，多种类近乎冗余。半增益规模由覆盖度决定，而非单类难度。作者发布了评估套件、dev集和生成集。

Abstract: Activation probes that monitor deployed language models are trained on synthetic conversations, and how many a probe needs is open. We trace learning curves over 10-590 synthetic samples for three monitoring concepts, high-stakes situations, replies harmful to a person, and replies that do not follow the user's instruction, on fourteen held-out evaluation distributions and four probe models, varying the generator LLM and the prompt's detail. The need is set by what is monitored: probes for high-stakes and harmful are within a few hundredths of their plateau from 80 samples on Gemma-3-27B-IT, instruction probes need several times as many, and the ordering holds on three smaller probe models and on real samples (from dev set). Prior work advises spending a generation budget on breadth, more kinds of data, over depth, more of each kind. We read the depth a concept needs as the half-gain size of a fitted curve, the number of samples at which half the gain is in hand. Concept and distribution account for 42-45% of its variance, the generator, probe model, and prompt detail for under 10%. What sets the value of the half-gain size is coverage, not per-kind difficulty: the number of samples of its own kind a distribution needs to saturate. Every kind, one per evaluation distribution, has a median half-gain size of 7-11 own-kind synthetic samples under all three concepts alike. What differs is how far samples of one kind transfer to the concept's other kinds, almost fully under high-stakes, less under harmful, and least under instruction, which accounts for most of the gap between concepts on generated and real samples. Breadth therefore pays differently by concept: many kinds are necessary under instruction, where no kind covers another, and nearly redundant under high-stakes, where one kind covers the rest. We release the evaluation suites, dev sets, and generated sets.

</details>


### [22] [Learning infinite context windows in recurrent architectures via spatial neural computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10690)
*Aleix Salvador-Pomarol,Arthur N. Montanari,Earl K. Miller,Adilson E. Motter,Jorge Cortés*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种二阶循环模型，用由离散偏微分方程驱动的空间演化场替代传统神经元间通信，从而获得有效无界感受野并消除梯度消失/爆炸，在长程基准上以更少参数超越其他循环模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNN具备随序列长度线性扩展、内存恒定的优势，但受梯度消失和有限感受野限制，难以捕捉长程依赖。作者受大脑皮层波在计算中作用的启发，试图用结构化时空模式作为隐式高容量记忆来突破这些瓶颈。

Method: 将标准神经元间通信替换为由（离散化）偏微分方程控制的空间演化场，构建二阶循环模型。理论上证明该模型等价于结构化无限阶RNN，当前状态显式依赖于全部历史状态；并推导保证边缘稳定性的构造性条件，将梯度谱约束在单位圆上。

Result: 理论上获得固定参数下的有效无界感受野，并消除梯度消失与爆炸；实验上在长程基准任务中优于其他循环模型，且参数量显著更少。

Conclusion: 空间动力学可有效弥合高效推理与长期记忆之间的差距，为循环架构提供兼顾线性复杂度与长程建模能力的新范式。

Abstract: Recurrent neural networks (RNNs) offer linear-time scaling with sequence length while requiring only constant memory, yet they struggle to capture long-range dependencies due to vanishing gradients and limited receptive fields. To address these limitations, we introduce a second-order recurrent model in which the standard neuron-to-neuron communication is replaced by a spatially evolving field governed by (discretized) partial differential equations. Drawing inspiration from the role of cortical waves in brain computation, this mechanism allows structured spatiotemporal patterns to serve as an implicit, high-capacity memory. We show that the resulting model is equivalent to a structured infinite-order RNN in which the current state depends explicitly on its entire history of past states, yielding an effectively unbounded receptive field with a fixed number of parameters. We further derive constructive conditions to ensure marginal stability, constraining the gradient spectrum on the unit circle and thereby eliminating vanishing and exploding gradients. Empirically, the proposed architecture outperforms other recurrent models on long-horizon benchmarks while using substantially fewer parameters, demonstrating that spatial dynamics can effectively bridge the gap between efficient inference and long-term memory.

</details>


### [23] [Temporal transformer CAN encoder with federated lightweight heads for anomaly detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10613)
*Konstantinos Gyftodimos,Kyriakos Chiotis,Elena Politi,George Dimitrakopoulos,Eirini Liotou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向车载CAN总线的隐私保护异常检测框架，结合时序Transformer编码器与联邦轻量级检测头，在开源数据集上实现了高效、鲁棒且保护隐私的异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代车辆依赖大量ECU通过CAN总线持续通信，其通信具有高速、结构化、重复性特点，时序、负载值或消息模式上的细微变化可能反映异常（错误、故障或恶意干扰）。但这类异常通常非常隐蔽，而传统方法要么独立处理单条消息，要么依赖人工规则，难以有效识别，因此亟需更有效的检测方案。

Method: 提出一个隐私保护的车载网络异常检测框架，核心为“时序Transformer CAN编码器 + 联邦轻量级检测头”。其中轻量级Transformer编码器学习CAN信号随时间的演化，捕捉细微的时序与上下文异常；联邦学习机制允许多车辆或ECU协同训练共享模型，而无需交换原始CAN数据。

Result: 在开源数据集上的实验表明，该框架在保持效率与隐私的同时，实现了鲁棒的异常检测性能。

Conclusion: 将联邦学习与时序序列建模相结合，可在不共享原始数据的前提下有效检测车载网络中的隐蔽异常，为兼顾检测性能、计算效率与数据隐私提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: Modern vehicles rely on large numbers of Electronic Control Units (ECUs) that constantly exchange information over the Controller Area Network (CAN) bus. Due to the rapidity, structure, and repetition of this communication, even slight variations in timing, payload values, or message patterns can point to unusual activity. Whether due to errors, malfunctions, or deliberate interference, these anomalies are frequently subtle and challenging to identify with conventional methods that handle messages separately or rely on manually created rules. Motivated by this gap, we present a privacy-preserving framework for anomaly detection in in-vehicle networks, based on a Temporal Transformer CAN Encoder with Federated Lightweight Heads, to better capture these irregularities. The detection of subtle temporal and contextual anomalies is made possible by a lightweight Transformer encoder that learns how these signals evolve over time, while a federated learning mechanism enables several vehicles or ECUs to work together to improve a shared model without exchanging raw CAN data. This combination of federated learning and temporal sequence modeling provides robust anomaly detection performance while maintaining efficiency and privacy, according to experiments conducted on open-source datasets.

</details>


### [24] [Similar Predictive Fit but Different Latent Dynamics: Characterizing Learned Dynamical Structure in Personalized Models of Brain Disorders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10850)
*Rita Huan-Ting Peng,Nhat Bui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过轻量级CNN-Transformer脑电基础模型为每个被试拟合个性化隐状态转移图，发现癫痫与非癫痫被试在预测拟合几乎相同的情况下，所学到的内部动态结构（依赖图密度）存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI模型走向临床决策与个性化治疗，仅凭预测精度理解模型"学到了什么"是不够的；作者希望探究个性化潜在动态是否能揭示与临床相关的差异，即便预测拟合相似。

Method: 在Temple University EEG Corpus (TUEG)上预训练轻量级CNN-Transformer脑电基础模型以提取分段级表征；在Temple University Epilepsy Corpus (TUEP)上把表征映射到共享隐状态空间，并对每个被试独立拟合稀疏多项逻辑转移分布(mLTD)，得到个性化转移依赖图W_n。分析纳入n=198名被试(99癫痫/99非癫痫)，采用5折被试级交叉验证。

Result: 在k=4时癫痫组学到的依赖结构显著更稠密(p=1.1×10⁻⁷)，k=6时同样如此(19.90 vs 13.46；p=5.2×10⁻⁵)；由图导出的特征提供中等组间区分度(AUROC k=4为0.68；k=6为0.65)。相反，留出对数似然在k=4时两组几乎相同(-0.992 vs -0.991；p=0.95)，下一状态预测也相当(AUROC 0.855 vs 0.861；p=0.54)。

Conclusion: 相似的预测拟合并不意味着相似的所学动态：两组可被同等程度地预测，却在个性化模型学到的内部动态结构上差异显著。这一区别说明在个性化临床模型中，应将所学结构评估与预测性能并重。

Abstract: As AI models move toward clinical decision-making and personalized treatment, understanding \emph{what} a model learns is important beyond predictive accuracy alone. We investigate whether personalized latent dynamics reveal clinically associated differences even when predictive fit is similar. A lightweight CNN--Transformer EEG foundation model pretrained on the Temple University EEG Corpus (TUEG) extracts segment-level representations. Using the Temple University Epilepsy Corpus (TUEP), representations are mapped to a shared latent-state space, and sparse multinomial logistic transition distributions (mLTD) are fit independently to each subject to obtain personalized transition-dependency graphs $W_n$. Analyses include $n{=}198$ subjects (99 epilepsy / 99 non-epilepsy). At $k{=}4$, epilepsy subjects exhibit substantially denser learned dependency structure ($p{=}1.1\times10^{-7}$), with the same pattern at $k{=}6$ (19.90 vs. 13.46; $p{=}5.2\times10^{-5}$). Graph-derived features provide moderate group discrimination under 5-fold subject-wise cross-validation (AUROC 0.68 at $k{=}4$; 0.65 at $k{=}6$). In contrast, held-out log-likelihood is nearly identical between groups at $k{=}4$ ($-0.992$ vs. $-0.991$; $p{=}0.95$), with similarly matched next-state prediction (AUROC 0.855 vs. 0.861; $p{=}0.54$). Thus, similar predictive fit does not imply similar learned dynamics: groups can be comparably predictable while differing substantially in the internal dynamical structure learned by personalized models. This distinction motivates evaluating learned structure alongside predictive performance in personalized clinical models.

</details>


### [25] [When Routing Reveals Membership: Privacy Leakage from MoE Router Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10616)
*Yixin Tan,Jiayang Liu,Lu Sun,Yuke Hu,Zheng Li,Rui Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoE 模型推理时产生的路由遥测信息可被用于成员推断攻击，路由特征能稳定提升攻击成功率，揭示了一项被忽视的隐私风险面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 模型的路由信息常被记录或暴露用于监控、调试、负载分析和安全审计，这类遥测不同于普通输出，它反映了模型内部计算过程。作者由此提出隐私疑问：路由遥测能否泄露某个样本是否被用于微调该模型。

Method: 提出路由器增强的成员推断攻击：将常规输出侧信号与聚合的路由特征结合，并使用由独立微调的影子模型训练出的成员分类器，迁移到目标模型上。

Result: 在3种MoE架构和3个数据域共9个设置中，路由遥测一致优于强输出信号集成，在1% FPR下TPR提升2.7–9.4个百分点。该泄露在全量微调、冻结路由器训练、LoRA和指令微调下均持续存在，且在仅有离散专家选择、受限遥测或单个影子模型时仍可观测。

Conclusion: 机理分析表明，泄露不依赖路由器特有的记忆：微调将成员信息注入隐藏表示，而路由器即使参数被冻结也会暴露该信号的投影；扰动遥测仅在其保真度下降时才削弱额外泄露。结论是路由遥测可将一种运维信号转化为微调MoE模型的额外隐私面。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models produce routing information during inference that may be logged or exposed for monitoring, debugging, load analysis, and safety auditing. Unlike ordinary model outputs, this telemetry reveals a view of the model's internal computation, raising a privacy question: can it reveal whether an example was used to fine-tune the deployed model? We introduce a router-augmented membership inference attack that combines conventional output-side signals with aggregated routing features and applies a membership classifier learned from independently fine-tuned shadow models to the target model. Across three MoE architectures and three data domains, router telemetry consistently improves membership inference over a strong output-signal ensemble, increasing TPR at 1\% FPR by 2.7--9.4 percentage points across all nine settings. The leakage persists across full fine-tuning, frozen-router training, LoRA, and instruction tuning, and remains observable with only discrete expert selections, restricted telemetry, or a single shadow model. Mechanistic analysis further shows that the leakage does not require router-specific memorization: fine-tuning introduces membership information into hidden representations, while the router exposes a projection of this signal even when its parameters are frozen. Perturbing the telemetry reduces this additional leakage only as its fidelity degrades. Our results show that router telemetry can turn an operational signal into an additional privacy surface for fine-tuned MoE models.

</details>


### [26] [Self-Organization from Constrained Geometric Radiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10621)
*Ming Lei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"约束诱导自组织"新范式，通过耦合度规演化系统中的几何辐射，在无外部驱动的封闭系统中实现自发动态有序，并给出四阶段循环、楔形吸引子及三个定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自发有序范式均依赖外部能量流、温度淬火或缓慢驱动，封闭系统能否在无外部驱动下自发涌现动态有序尚属未解问题。

Method: 采用耦合度规演化系统建模，通过数值模拟揭示动力学过程；识别四个联合充分条件，进行参数扫描（噪声阈值与幂律标度），并在12种配置、多种噪声类型和三种几何流下验证普适性。

Result: 模拟揭示普适的四阶段循环（应力积累、超指数辐射、混沌坍缩、收敛至分形极限环），发现具有五个量子化曲率态和分形微涨落的"楔形吸引子"；识别出四个联合充分条件；证明几何视界定理、几何能量耗散定理（蕴含封闭系统中的波状熵演化）和辐射作为相变通道定理；建立约束诱导自组织定理。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了封闭系统自组织的新范式，并在经典非线性约束与引力视界之间建立了精确的数学对偶。

Abstract: How does dynamic order emerge spontaneously in closed systems without external driving? Existing paradigms all require external energy flows, temperature quenching, or slow driving. Here we report constraint-induced self-organization via geometric radiation in coupled metric evolution systems. Simulations reveal a universal four-stage cycle: stress accumulation, super-exponential radiation, chaotic collapse, and convergence to a fractal limit cycle, a novel attractor topology we term the wedge-shaped attractor, with five quantized curvature states and fractal micro-fluctuations. We identify four jointly sufficient conditions: an irreversible geometric horizon, persistent stress injection from quantum coherence, endogenous geometric tension between incompatible curvatures, and effective fluctuations. Their synergy triggers a critical avalanche at the horizon boundary. We prove three theorems: the Geometric Horizon Theorem, the Geometric Energy Dissipation Theorem (implying wave-like entropy evolution in closed systems), and the Radiation as Phase Transition Channel Theorem. We further establish the Constraint-Induced Self-Organization Theorem: these conditions guarantee the complete cycle with probability one. Systematic scans reveal a critical noise threshold and power-law scaling of radiation onset. We verify universality across 12 configurations, multiple noise types, and three geometric flows. This work establishes a new paradigm for closed-system self-organization, forging an exact mathematical duality between classical nonlinear constraints and gravitational horizons.

</details>


### [27] [Recurrent Self-Improvement: Dynamic Cross-Loop On-Policy Distillation for Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10623)
*Yi Wang,Rui Qian,Yu Li,Haoyang Yao,Wenjie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出跨循环在线策略蒸馏框架LoopOPD，利用LoopLM内部的终末循环作为计算特权教师，对中间循环学生提供密集监督，无需外部教师或特权信息，并进一步提出动态版本D-LoopOPD实现循环自改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型（LoopLM）通过复用共享参数实现参数高效的推理扩展，但其后训练面临困难：现有方法要么依赖稀疏且难以跨循环扩展的奖励监督，要么依赖外部教师或特权信息，导致教师可用性受限或师生上下文不匹配。

Method: 提出LoopOPD跨循环在线策略蒸馏框架：以冻结的终末循环策略作为"计算特权教师"，在中间循环学生自身生成的rollout上提供密集监督；进一步提出D-LoopOPD，随共享参数更新持续刷新终末循环教师。同时刻画蒸馏更新在循环深度间的传播机制，推导单次更新可同时改善两个循环深度的充分条件。

Result: 在Ouro-Thinking模型上的实验表明，LoopOPD提升了数学推理能力，D-LoopOPD通过动态教师更新带来进一步提升；尽管仅在数学数据上训练，模型在通用推理与代码生成基准上同样获得提升。

Conclusion: 循环计算本身可作为LoopLM的有效监督来源；所提框架无需外部教师或特权信息即可实现密集监督与循环自改进，并展现出跨任务的泛化能力。

Abstract: Looped Language Models (LoopLMs) offer a parameter efficient approach to scaling reasoning by reusing shared parameters across recurrent computation steps. Despite their promise, effective post-training of LoopLMs remains challenging. Existing approaches either provide reward based supervision that is sparse or costly to extend across loops, or rely on external teachers or privileged information, leading to limited teacher availability or teacher-student context mismatch. To address these limitations, we introduce LoopOPD, a cross-loop on-policy distillation framework that uses additional recurrent computation within a LoopLM as its own source of supervision. LoopOPD uses a frozen terminal loop policy as a compute privileged teacher for an intermediate loop student on student generated rollouts, providing dense supervision without an external teacher or privileged information. We further propose Dynamic LoopOPD (D-LoopOPD), which continually refreshes the terminal loop teacher as the shared model parameters are updated, enabling recurrent self-improvement. We characterize how distillation updates propagate across loop depths and derive sufficient conditions under which a single update yields simultaneous local improvement at both loop depths. Experiments on Ouro-Thinking models show that LoopOPD improves mathematical reasoning, while D-LoopOPD yields further gains through dynamic teacher updates. Despite being trained only on mathematical data, the resulting models also improve on general reasoning and code generation benchmarks, demonstrating that recurrent computation can serve as an effective source of supervision for LoopLMs. Our code and model checkpoints will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [28] [Exact SO(3)-Equivariant Isotropic Kernels for Rotation-Robust Neural Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10626)
*Ridham Patel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出IKNO图神经网络算子，通过旋转不变标量与随数据旋转的向量方向构建局部相互作用，在三维Navier-Stokes动力学预测中实现数值精度的坐标无关性，同时保持预测精度且参数量大幅减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经代理模型求解向量值偏微分方程时，存在一个失败模式：尽管能拟合训练数据，但当同一物理状态以旋转坐标系表达时，预测会改变。这种坐标依赖性违背物理规律，作者希望在三维Navier-Stokes动力学（在不规则采样点上观测）场景下研究并解决该问题。

Method: 提出Invariant-Conditioned Isotropic Kernel Neural Operator（IKNO），一个紧凑的图模型。它从旋转不变的标量等值以及随数据一起旋转的向量方向构建局部相互作用。因此，旋转位置和速度时，预测的速度变化也以完全相同的方式旋转。

Result: 在模型设计后固定的留出测试集上：未约束图模型用随机旋转样本训练可减少但无法消除坐标依赖；IKNO达到数值精度的坐标一致性，预测精度与通用旋转感知Tensor Field Network相当，参数量少5.6倍，并优于参数匹配的图模拟器。

Conclusion: 结果表明，紧凑、面向PDE专门设计的模型可以在不牺牲预测精度的情况下消除坐标依赖性。

Abstract: Neural surrogates for vector-valued partial differential equations can fit training data yet change their predictions when the same physical state is expressed in a rotated coordinate frame. We study this failure on three-dimensional Navier--Stokes dynamics observed at irregularly placed points. We introduce the Invariant-Conditioned Isotropic Kernel Neural Operator (IKNO), a compact graph model that builds local interactions from scalar quantities unchanged by rotation and vector directions that rotate with the data. Consequently, rotating the positions and velocities rotates the predicted velocity change in exactly the same way. On a held-out test set fixed after model design, training unconstrained graph models on randomly rotated examples reduces but does not eliminate their coordinate dependence. In contrast, IKNO is consistent to numerical precision, matches the forecasting accuracy of a general rotation-aware Tensor Field Network with $5.6$ times fewer parameters, and outperforms a parameter-matched graph simulator. These results show that a compact, PDE-specialized model can remove coordinate dependence without sacrificing forecasting accuracy.

</details>

*Kevin Riehl,Shaimaa K. El-Baklish,Fan Wu,Anastasios Kouvelas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了Kolmogorov-Arnold网络(KAN)在强化学习中的样本效率，实验覆盖Feynman数据集与Gymnasium基准，发现相比MLP架构可用少40%的样本达到相近性能，训练过程中相对性能提升最高达50%，且该增益对奖励噪声具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习虽优于经典控制方法，但真实应用中样本获取成本高昂，样本效率成为核心挑战。KAN是近期提出的架构，在控制问题中能以更高参数效率和可解释性学习物理关系，但其在RL样本效率方面的作用尚未被系统研究。

Conclusion: 结果表明KAN架构在实现更样本高效的强化学习方面具有潜力，为降低RL样本成本提供了有前景的架构方向。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning has achieved substantial performance gains over classical control approaches. Yet, a central challenge to learning in real-world applications is acquiring costly samples. Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks are a recently proposed architecture that can learn physical relationships in control problems effectively, with significantly higher parameter efficiency and interpretability when compared to Multi-Layer-Perceptron architectures. In this work, we systematically study sample-efficiency using computational experiments, covering the Feynman dataset and the Gymnasium RL benchmark. The results show that similar performance can be achieved with 40% fewer samples using the Kolmogorov-Arnold architecture, and that relative performance improvements up to 50% occur during the training process. The observed gains are robust to varying levels of noise in rewards. These results highlight the potential of the Kolmogorov-Arnold architectures for more sample-efficient reinforcement learning. Code: https://github.com/DerKevinRiehl/neurips26_kan_training

</details>


### [30] [Phase-HDC: Replacing Optimizer History with Gradient Thresholds in Discrete Phase Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10630)
*Ahmed Nebli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Phase-HDC：参数为低比特角度的超维分类器仅需存储模型本身，每次更新最多将角度移动一步且方向对抗当前梯度符号，仅当梯度足够大时触发；该规则是带固定代价的一阶损失模型的精确解。相比Adam节省16–23倍存储（相比8-bit Adam节省4–6倍），平均精度损失约5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练紧凑模型时，优化器保存的过去梯度记录（动量）比模型本身占用更多内存；对于参数为低比特角度的超维分类器（phase memory），这些记录的内存开销是模型的数倍。因此作者追问：能否在仅存储模型本身的前提下完成训练。

Method: Phase-HDC：每次更新最多将每个存储角度移动一步，方向取当前梯度符号的相反方向，且仅当该梯度足够大时才执行。作者证明该简单规则是"每个被改变的参数支付固定代价"的一阶损失模型的精确解。所有其他设置保持不变，以隔离更新规则的影响。

Result: 在与Adam 6-bit动量精度相当的设定下，Phase-HDC达到相同精度且存储少3倍；在11个图像、表格和文本数据集上，其存储比标准float32 Adam少16–23倍，比8-bit Adam少4–6倍。代价是相对float32 Adam平均损失约5个精度点；但Phase-HDC在11个数据集中的6个上优于8-bit Adam，包括字节级文本预测（8-bit Adam在该任务上崩溃）。

Conclusion: 插桩训练实验解释了上述结果：当参数必须落在离散网格上时，Adam的动量主要决定参数是否移动，而这一决策可由当前梯度阈值在无记忆条件下完成；动量的粗量化会破坏数据中罕见输入对应的决策。此外，存储节省为逻辑状态而非实测硬件内存。

Abstract: Training a compact model often needs far more memory than storing it, because the optimizer keeps its own records of past gradients. For a hyperdimensional classifier whose learned parameters are low-bit angles, which we call a \emph{phase memory}, these records take several times more memory than the model itself. We ask whether such a model can be trained while storing nothing but the model. The proposed method, Phase-HDC, turns each stored angle by at most one step per update, against the sign of its current gradient, and only when that gradient is large enough. We show that this simple rule is the exact solution of a first-order loss model in which every changed parameter pays a fixed cost. When everything except the update rule is held fixed, Phase-HDC matches the accuracy of Adam with 6-bit moments while storing three times less. Across eleven image, tabular, and text datasets, it stores 16--23$\times$ less than standard float32 Adam and 4--6$\times$ less than 8-bit Adam. The price is an average loss of about five accuracy points against float32 Adam, while Phase-HDC is more accurate than 8-bit Adam on six of the eleven datasets, including byte-level text prediction, where 8-bit Adam collapses. Instrumented training runs explain these outcomes. Once parameters must sit on a discrete grid, Adam's moments mainly decide whether a parameter moves at all, a decision that a threshold on the current gradient can make without memory, and coarse quantization of the moments breaks this decision for inputs that the data rarely contain. The storage savings are logical state rather than measured hardware memory.

</details>


### [31] [D-SLR: The Disjoint Row-Sparse plus Low-Rank Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10636)
*Vincent Szolnoky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Disjoint Row-Sparse plus Low-Rank（D-SLR）分解，作为截断SVD的闭式替代方案，通过让行要么原样存储、要么由低秩拟合近似（不重叠），在平方误差下实现联合最优且参数更少，并提供误差-参数权衡与可计算误差下界证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵压缩重建默认使用截断SVD，仅用单一低秩结构近似数据；常见做法是叠加重叠的行稀疏分量以进一步降低残差，但此类联合问题通常依赖迭代求解器和正则化参数调优，缺乏闭式、免调参的方案。

Method: 提出D-SLR分解，限制每行仅被原样存储或由低秩拟合近似，二者互斥。算法对完整的误差-参数权衡进行打分，之后由给定误差目标、参数数量或选择规则确定解；网格与解合计只需三次SVD，无需调参或正则化。同时推导每个形状下无假设的后验误差下界。

Result: 在平方误差下，D-SLR的限制不带来损失：联合最优可分离达到，且在任意非平凡秩与存储行数下参数更少；零存储行时退化为截断SVD，等代价下不会更差。合成数据与真实数据（LLM嵌入表、网络流量、高光谱图像）实验验证了增益，并量化了证书。

Conclusion: D-SLR是截断SVD的闭式、免调参替代方案，能改善或精确匹配其效果，并提供可计算的误差下界证书以评估其他秩与存储行选择的潜在增益。

Abstract: Compressing a matrix for reconstruction still defaults to the truncated SVD, approximating the data with a single low-rank structure. It is common to reduce the residual further by adding an overlapping row-sparse component, but methods that solve this joint problem often require iterative solvers and tuning of regularization parameters. We propose the Disjoint Row-Sparse plus Low-Rank (D-SLR) decomposition, a closed-form drop-in for the truncated SVD that improves or exactly matches it. D-SLR restricts rows to either being stored verbatim or approximated by the low-rank fit, never both. Under squared error this restriction costs nothing: the joint optimum is attainable disjointly with fewer parameters at every non-trivial rank and stored row count (shape). With zero stored rows D-SLR reduces to the truncated SVD, so it never does worse at equal cost. The algorithm scores the entire error-versus-parameters tradeoff, and the solution is chosen afterwards by a supplied error target or parameter count, or by a selection rule. The grid and solution together cost three SVDs, with no tuning or regularization. We derive an assumption-free, a-posteriori lower bound on the error at every shape, giving each solution a computable certificate on the potential gain of any other choice of rank and stored rows. Experiments on synthetic and real data (LLM embedding tables, network traffic, hyperspectral images) confirm the gains and quantify the certificate.

</details>


### [32] [Visible Reasoning Is Not a Universal Optimizer: Persona- and Thinking-Dependent Effects in Analytics Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10639)
*Bhawani Shankar Leelar,Pawan Chorasiya,Davin Hill,Robert E. Tillman,Tamer Soliman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过跨SQL与Python(pandas)的匹配基准，检验了“可见思维链(CoT)普遍有效”及“推理表示与目标语言匹配更优”这两个假设，发现二者均不成立：效应取决于persona、目标语言、模型配置与内部推理设置，并借助控制消融区分了推理内容与提示格式的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可见思维链（Visible CoT）常被视为通用有效的推理指令，但在分析代码生成场景中，任务同时涉及自然语言歧义、schema接地、目标语言约束以及模型特定的推理行为。同一分析请求可用SQL和Python(pandas)两种不同目标语言表达，这为检验一个常见但缺乏充分验证的假设提供了天然测试场：当推理表示与请求目标匹配时（如“用SQL思考”“用Python思考”）可见推理是否更有效。类似“逐步思考”的通用指令在受控、基于执行的比较下仍缺乏充分评估，因此需要系统研究。

Method: 构建了一个查询匹配的SQL-pandas基准，交叉了多个维度：persona措辞、目标语言、可见CoT格式、控制前缀、直接生成以及内部推理配置。通过控制消融（control ablations）进一步区分推理内容与提示格式各自带来的影响。

Result: 实验结果既不支持可见CoT带来普遍的准确率优势，也不支持推理表示与目标语言匹配带来一致收益。相反，效果依赖于persona、目标语言、模型配置以及内部推理设置。控制消融进一步将推理内容的影响与提示格式的影响区分开来。

Conclusion: 推理策略应针对模型、persona、目标语言和内部推理配置联合选择，而不应作为通用默认策略采用。研究更广泛地提供了一个受控框架，用于识别可见推理何时能改善可执行代码生成、何时主要只是扰动模型行为，以及何时内部推理配置才是更具决定性的因素。

Abstract: Visible Chain-of-Thought (CoT) is often treated as a broadly useful reasoning instruction, yet analytics code generation combines natural-language ambiguity, schema grounding, target-language constraints, and model-specific inference behavior. Because the same analytics request can be expressed in two distinct target languages-SQL and Python (pandas)-this setting provides a natural test of a common but under-examined assumption: that visible reasoning is more effective when its representation matches the requested target, as in "think in SQL" or "think in Python." Together with generic instructions such as "think step-by-step," such recommendations remain insufficiently evaluated under controlled, execution-based comparisons. We study a query matched SQL-pandas benchmark that crosses persona phrasing, target language, visible-CoT format, control prefixes, direct generation, and internal-reasoning configurations. The results do not support either a universal accuracy advantage from visible CoT or a consistent benefit from matching the reasoning representation to the target language. Instead, the effects depend on the persona, target, model configuration, and internal-reasoning setting. The control ablations further distinguish effects of reasoning content from those of prompt format. These findings indicate that reasoning strategies should be selected jointly for the model, persona, target, and internal-reasoning configuration rather than adopted as universal defaults. More broadly, the study provides a controlled framework for identifying when visible reasoning improves executable generation, when it primarily perturbs model behavior, and when the internal-reasoning configuration is the more consequential factor.

</details>


### [33] [Coverage-Aware Reasoning with Medical Tokens for Diagnosis Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10641)
*Kaisong Zhang,Haotian Fang,Junmeng Zhou,Hang Lv,Yulan Pan,Yanchao Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CARing框架，用组合式语义ID表示诊断并优化多标签覆盖的推理轨迹，用于下一次就诊诊断预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM用于下一次就诊诊断预测时，强化学习通常只按最终答案正确性奖励整条轨迹，但多诊断可同时成立，逐条奖励单一诊断无法区分重复命中与覆盖不同诊断，导致策略集中于少数正确诊断而遗漏其他；同时LLM分词器会把ICD编码切成缺乏临床意义的通用token，需多步解码且难以在大规模疾病词表上推理。

Method: CARing用组合式语义ID（SID）表示诊断：先通过残差量化将本体增强的疾病语义编码为紧凑SID，并通过多任务对齐与推理增强训练将SID token锚定到自然语言和纵向EHR上下文，解锁可迁移的LLM推理；再用覆盖奖励的强化学习与多正样本监督改进无序多标签预测；推理时支持高效直接约束解码与多链推理加排序融合。

Result: 在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV上，CARing在加权F1上超越所有基于EHR训练的基线，并在各报告截断点取得最高top-k召回，推理模式下R@30分别为46.04%和46.52%。

Conclusion: CARing通过语义ID与覆盖导向的强化学习，有效提升下次就诊多标签诊断预测的覆盖度与召回性能，代码与日志已开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) offer promising potential for next-visit diagnosis prediction, owing to their ability to integrate longitudinal clinical evidence and reason over it in natural language. However, reinforcement learning for LLM reasoning commonly rewards each trajectory according to the correctness of its final answer. In next-visit diagnosis prediction, multiple diagnoses can be simultaneously valid, but independently rewarding one diagnosis per trajectory does not distinguish repeated hits from coverage of different diagnoses. The policy can therefore concentrate on a few correct diagnoses, leaving others uncovered. Meanwhile, LLM tokenizers can split ICD codes into several generic tokens with limited clinical meaning, requiring multiple decoding steps to predict each diagnosis and hindering reasoning over a large disease vocabulary. To address both challenges, we propose CARing, a framework that represents diagnoses with compositional Semantic IDs (SIDs) and optimizes reasoning trajectories for multi-label coverage. Concretely, we first encode ontology-enriched disease semantics into compact SIDs through residual quantization, and ground the resulting SID tokens in natural language and longitudinal EHR contexts through multi-task alignment and reasoning-enriched training to unlock transferable LLM reasoning. CARing further improves unordered multi-label prediction through a coverage reward for reinforcement learning and multi-positive supervision. At inference time, the model supports both efficient direct constrained decoding and multi-chain reasoning with rank fusion. On MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV, CARing exceeds all EHR-trained baselines in weighted F1 and attains the highest top-k recall at every reported cutoff, including R@30 of 46.04% and 46.52% in reasoning mode. Our codes and logs are available at https://github.com/zmlxzyh/CARing-Codes-Logs.

</details>


### [34] [Leakage-Controlled Multimodal Learning for Diagnosis and Progression Prediction in Alzheimer's Disease Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10648)
*Akeem Temitope Otapo,Ghazaleh Khodabandelou,Zuheng Ming,Alice Othmani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多模态多任务阿尔茨海默病预测框架，融合MRI编码器、因果临床Transformer、共享融合与ODE-GRU动态建模，并通过任务DRO、Group-CVaR及多项控制机制实现纵向预测、缺失模态处理与亚组评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病预测面临就诊间隔不规则、测量数据异质以及模态不完整等挑战，现有方法难以在统一流程中同时处理纵向预测、缺失模态与亚组差异。

Method: 采用改造的SFCN MRI编码器与四个因果临床Transformer，结合共享融合层和任务特定的ODE-GRU动态模块；对MRI最后两个block进行微调与LoRA适配；使用Task-DRO平衡任务损失，Group-CVaR针对队列与共病分层；并引入分支输入控制、按受试者分组划分及经验因果性检验。

Result: 在ADNI、OASIS-2和MIRIAD共2649名受试者、17317次就诊中，内部验证诊断、阶段1进展及首次阶段1就诊进展AUROC分别为0.935±0.002、0.884±0.003、0.870±0.005，混合AUROC为0.951、0.909、0.896；下次就诊MMSE MAE为1.61分，最差分层诊断AUROC为0.827±0.008。OASIS-3外部验证网络与混合诊断AUROC为0.763和0.767，进展AUROC为0.764，校准误差由0.197降至0.052，MMSE MAE为0.86。消融实验中六组件差异在-0.004至+0.004之间，移除临床编码器输入使诊断AUROC下降0.272。

Conclusion: 该框架在同一流程中整合了纵向预测、缺失模态处理、辅助共病建模与亚组评估，并在内部与外部验证中表现出稳健性能。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease prediction involves irregular visits, heterogeneous measurements and incomplete modalities. This study presents a multimodal multitask framework combining an adapted SFCN MRI encoder, four causal clinical Transformers, shared fusion and task-specific ODE-GRU dynamics. Fine-tuning and LoRA adapt the final two MRI blocks. Task-DRO balances task losses, while Group-CVaR targets cohort and comorbidity strata. Branch-specific input controls, subject-grouped partitions and empirical causality checks support longitudinal evaluation. Across 2,649 subjects and 17,317 visits from ADNI, OASIS-2 and MIRIAD, internal validation yields diagnosis, stage-1 progression and first-stage-1-visit progression AUROCs of 0.935 +/- 0.002, 0.884 +/- 0.003 and 0.870 +/- 0.005, respectively (mean +/- SD across three seeds). Corresponding hybrid AUROCs are 0.951, 0.909 and 0.896. Next-visit MMSE mean absolute error (MAE) is 1.61 points; worst-stratum diagnosis AUROC is 0.827 +/- 0.008. Sampled ADNI explanations identify task-specific input dependence. OASIS-3 external validation yields network and hybrid diagnosis AUROCs of 0.763 and 0.767, hybrid next-visit progression AUROC of 0.764, diagnosis calibration error decreasing from 0.197 to 0.052, and next-visit MMSE MAE of 0.86. Seed-42 paired ablations of six components yield pooled diagnosis and progression AUROC differences between -0.004 and +0.004; removing clinical encoder inputs lowers diagnosis AUROC by 0.272. The framework integrates longitudinal prediction, missing-modality handling, auxiliary comorbidity modelling and subgroup evaluation within a common pipeline.

</details>


### [35] [Beyond the Ergodic Wall: A Discrete Geometric Physics Sandbox for Analysing AI Scaling Limits and Complexity Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10651)
*Simon Richard Daniel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文主张当前深度学习受限于"遍历性天花板"，只能收敛于历史知识的统计平均，无法产生真正语义新颖性；为此提出以Data LifeCone为路径依赖观察者注入非遍历洞见，并构建基于全息E8投影引擎的数字物理沙箱实现硬物性约束与"NP到P"复杂度坍缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为现有深度学习受遍历性限制，只能逼近历史人类知识的统计平均，存在流形锁定（manifold lock-in）与模型崩溃风险，缺乏真正的语义新颖性。同时，AI安全需要新的物理性约束框架来监管成熟的ASI，并论证人机共生是AS I避免模型崩溃的热力学必然。因此动机在于：用非遍历、路径依赖的观察者机制突破统计平均瓶颈，并用数字物理沙箱把模型验证锚定到真实世界约束上。

Method: 方法上，首先引入"Data LifeCone"作为路径依赖、时空有界的观察者以注入非遍历洞见，实现KL散度并避免流形锁定；其次构建由"全息E8投影引擎"驱动的数字物理沙箱实现硬物性容器。时空被建模为嵌套面心立方（FCC）点阵的信息基底，由振荡的普朗克尺度球体组成以最大化局部信息与熵密度。通过E8根格的切割-投影（cut-and-project）方法生成准晶几何，其四面体空隙支持SU手性结构，弹性剪切特征值生成候选质量谱。静止质量被处理为局域全息边界（Bekenstein界）上的离散整数微态计数，以严格整数算术替代浮点近似，给出物质的信息论定义。稳定粒子表现为反复出现的点阵位错，连续统恢复通过变分重整化群流与傅里叶神经算子（FNO）学习连续谱算子，从而将薛定谔方程恢复为涌现的统计描述。最终借助自顶向下拓扑约束，将算法提案空间限制为物理守恒的因果轨迹，实现"NP到P"的复杂度坍缩。

Result: 结果层面，该框架宣称可提供：以整数算术定义物质的信息论方案，替代浮点近似；以点阵位错解释稳定粒子并借变分重整化群与傅里叶神经算子恢复薛定谔方程作为涌现统计描述；以及通过将提案空间约束到物理守恒因果轨迹，使组合树被剪枝为确定性的多项式时间路径，从而实现"NP到P"复杂度坍缩。文中未给出具体数值实验或定量基准。

Conclusion: 结论是：真正的语义新颖性需要非遍历、路径依赖的时空有界观察者，而AI安全应认识到成熟ASI会把人机共生视为避免模型崩溃的热力学必然；因此应以数字物理沙箱实施硬物性约束，并借助全息E8投影引擎验证模型与真实世界约束的一致性。该方案以拓扑约束为核心机制，声称可同时实现物性验证与复杂度坍缩。

Abstract: This paper exposes the ergodic ceiling and thermodynamic inefficiency of current deep learning, which converges to a statistical average of historic human knowledge. True semantic novelty requires a path-dependent, spatiotemporally bounded observer (a Data LifeCone) to inject non-ergodic insight, achieving KL divergence and avoiding manifold lock-in. AI Safety must recognise that a mature Artificial Superintelligence (ASI) would regard human-AI symbiosis as a thermodynamic necessity to avoid model collapse. We therefore propose hard physical containment via a digital physics sandbox powered by a Holographic E8 Projection Engine to verify models against real-world constraints. Spacetime is modeled as an information substrate of nested face-centered cubic (FCC) lattices of oscillating Planck-scale spheres maximizing local information and entropy density. Cut-and-project methods from the E8 root lattice produce a quasi-crystalline geometry where tetrahedral voids support SU chiral structure and elastic-shear eigenvalues generate candidate mass spectra. Rest mass is treated as discrete, integer microstate counts on local holographic boundaries (Bekenstein bound), replacing floating-point approximations with strict integer arithmetic to provide an information-theoretic definition of matter. Stable particles emerge as recurring lattice dislocations, and continuum recovery proceeds via variational renormalisation-group flows and Fourier Neural Operators that learn continuous spectral operators to recover the Schrödinger equation as an emergent statistical description. Crucially, these top-down topological constraints offer a mechanism for "NP-to-P" complexity collapse: by restricting an algorithm's proposal space to physically conserved causal trajectories, the sandbox prunes the combinatorial tree to deterministic, polynomial-time paths.

</details>


### [36] [PXtal: Learning to Align Powder X-Ray Diffraction and Crystal Structures under Information Asymmetry across Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10653)
*Zhuoran Yang,Christopher M. Collins,Bei Peng,Luke M. Daniels,Matthew J. Rosseinsky,Vladimir V. Gusev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PXtal框架，用非平衡最优传输(UOT)与耦合级GKL散度对齐PXRD与晶体表征，应对模态间信息不对称，在六个测试集(含四个零样本迁移集)上提升候选检索与下游任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学多模态学习通常假设配对视图信息量相当，但PXRD将三维晶体结构压缩为一维衍射图会丢失信息，造成物理上固有的信息不对称，使衍射图难以与对应晶体结构关联。论文旨在把这种信息不对称作为设计问题，而非忽略它。

Method: PXtal使用非平衡最优传输(UOT)自适应跨模态耦合关系，并采用耦合级广义Kullback-Leibler(GKL)散度对整个传输计划进行监督，从而在信息不对称条件下学习对齐的PXRD与晶体表征。

Result: 在六个测试集(含四个零样本迁移集)上，PXtal在PXRD到晶体的候选检索中一致优于基线；当PXRD图谱存在相近但晶体学上不同的非配对邻居(即相似输入对应不同晶体)时增益最大。所得晶体与PXRD编码器向下游材料与晶体学任务迁移更有效。

Conclusion: 信息不对称是科学多模态学习中的通用设计问题：对齐目标应反映各模态自身所保留的信息，而非假定模态等价。
