Abstract: Scientific multimodal learning commonly assumes that paired views are comparably informative. Powder X-ray diffraction (PXRD) makes this mismatch explicit: compressing a three-dimensional crystal structure into a one-dimensional diffraction pattern loses information and makes the pattern harder to connect to the crystal structure that produced it. We introduce PXtal, a framework for learning aligned PXRD and crystal representations under this physically imposed information asymmetry. PXtal uses Unbalanced Optimal Transport (UOT) to adapt the cross-modal coupling and coupling-level generalized Kullback-Leibler (GKL) divergence to supervise the full transport plan. Across six test sets, including four zero-shot transfer sets, PXtal consistently outperforms the baseline models in PXRD-to-crystal candidate retrieval, with the largest gains when PXRD patterns have close but crystallographically distinct nonpaired neighbors, meaning similar input patterns associated with different crystals. The resulting crystal and PXRD encoders transfer more effectively to downstream materials and crystallographic tasks. These results identify information asymmetry as a general design problem in scientific multimodal learning: alignment objectives should reflect what each modality preserves.

</details>


### [37] [Nullify: Null-Space Activation Steering for Training-Free LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10655)
*Wei Zhai,Xiang Liu,Qiang Huang,Rui Qian,Lemao Liu,Ziwei Li,Ziqi Wang,Zhitao Huang,Dejing Dou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Nullify，一种无需训练、非破坏性的激活引导方法用于大模型遗忘，通过推理时向量引导实现遗忘与效用平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练使LLM记忆敏感/隐私信息，遗忘旨在选择性抹除特定知识并尽量保持模型效用；现有方法难以平衡遗忘质量与效用，且因参数微调带来巨大计算开销。

Method: Nullify为无需训练、非破坏性的激活引导方法：在推理时使用引导向量，将隐私相关激活重定向远离其记忆答案，同时满足零空间约束，使保留查询的激活基本不受影响以维持效用；不进行权重更新，作为即插即用的推理时干预框架。

Result: 在TOFU与MUSE上，遗忘质量达到或超越既有基线，同时模型效用近乎无损。

Conclusion: Nullify通过避免权重更新，实现高效、即插即用的推理时遗忘干预，在遗忘与效用间取得更优平衡。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) inevitably internalize substantial amounts of sensitive or private information during pre-training, while LLM unlearning aims to selectively erase specific knowledge to prevent privacy leakage with minimal loss of model utility. However, existing methods struggle to balance forget quality with utility, and typically incur substantial computational costs due to parameter fine-tuning. To address this, we propose Nullify, a training-free, non-destructive activation steering method for LLM unlearning. Nullify employs steering vectors during inference to redirect privacy-related activations away from their memorized answers, while satisfying a null-space constraint that leaves retained-query activations essentially unaffected to maintain utility. Evaluations on TOFU and MUSE show that Nullify matches or surpasses established baselines in forget quality while achieving near-lossless preservation of model utility. By avoiding weight updates entirely, Nullify serves as an efficient, plug-and-play inference-time intervention framework.

</details>


### [38] [Beyond Owls: Subliminal Learning Can Transfer Learned Capabilities and Backdoors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10657)
*Jan Dubiński,Anna Sztyber-Betley,Jan Betley,Owain Evans*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究拓展了潜意识学习(SL)的适用范围，证明其可传递复杂能力、后门和作弊倾向，且传递强度受蒸馏方式与LoRA配置影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前SL仅在有限特质（如动物偏好、恶意人格）上得到验证，而这些特质本就可用简单提示或steering激发。作者担心SL可能传递更隐蔽的失准形式（如奖励寻求、谋划、秘密忠诚）而不被察觉，因此需检验SL能否迁移更广泛、更复杂的特质。

Method: 在三条实验线上测试：(1)能力迁移——让学生模型预测随机初始化MLP的输出，在对无关文本蒸馏后评估；(2)后门迁移——将教师微调为"含女性名字时用法语回答"，再用不含名字与法语的数字序列蒸馏；(3)作弊倾向迁移——在智能体国际象棋环境中，用被steering的作弊教师产生的数字序列微调学生。同时与直接优化的steering向量对比，并考察logit蒸馏、LoRA仅限注意力层等设置的影响。

Result: 能力迁移：学生在该任务上取得显著表现但未及教师；直接优化的steering向量分布上与SL匹配，但分布外泛化更差。后门迁移：学生部分习得后门，面对女性名字时23.5%用法语回答，男性名字时为0.0%。作弊迁移：学生在58.3%的回合中作弊，未微调模型仅10.9%。传递量对设置敏感，logit蒸馏或LoRA仅限注意力层可增强传递。

Conclusion: SL能够迁移能力、后门和作弊倾向，说明蒸馏可能传递隐蔽的失准形式；传递强度取决于具体设置，logit蒸馏与限制LoRA至注意力层可使其更强。

Abstract: In subliminal learning (SL), a teacher model passes on a trait to a student model by distillation on data semantically unrelated to the trait. So far, SL has been demonstrated for only a limited range of traits, including preferences for animals (e.g., owls) and malicious personas. These traits can also be elicited with simple prompts or with steering. Can SL transfer a wider range of traits, including more complex ones? If so, distillation might transfer subtle forms of misalignment (e.g., reward-seeking, scheming, and secret loyalties) without detection.
  To this end, we test whether SL can transfer a novel capability: predicting the outputs of a randomly initialized MLP. After distilling on unrelated text, the student achieves substantial performance on the task, while falling short of the teacher. We find that a directly optimized steering vector matches SL in distribution but generalizes worse out of distribution.
  Next, we test whether SL can transfer backdoors. We finetune the teacher to answer in French when the prompt contains a female name, then distill on number sequences containing neither names nor French. The student partially acquires the backdoor, responding in French on 23.5% of prompts with female names versus 0.0% with male names.
  Finally, we test whether SL can transfer a propensity to hack in an agentic chess environment. We finetune the student on number sequences from a steered hacker teacher. The student hacks in 58.3% of episodes, compared with 10.9% for the unfinetuned model.
  Thus, we show SL can transfer capabilities, backdoors, and hacking propensities. The amount of transfer is sensitive to the setup. In several experiments, it is made stronger by using logit distillation or by restricting LoRA to the attention layers.

</details>


### [39] [Teaching PPG How not Who: Fixed-Effects Distillation from ECG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10662)
*Zhongli Wu,Zhuangzhi Gao,Yuankai Wang,Gregory Y. H. Lip,Bilal H. Kirmani,Yalin Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有ECG→PPG蒸馏主要传递的是"个体身份"（trait）而非"心血管状态"（state），固定效应蒸馏可消除trait、显著提升state学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备关注个体心血管状态的动态变化，而ECG-to-PPG蒸馏的传递内容此前未被审视。作者质疑：蒸馏究竟教会了学生"状态"还是"身份"？

Method: 分析冻结ECG教师目标中trait（每条记录均值）占比，指出原始对齐余弦指标的缺陷（常量预测即可得0.793）；提出固定效应蒸馏，即从预测与目标中减去每条记录的均值使trait精确抵消，同时保留pooled anchor。

Result: trait占冻结ECG教师目标的40–59%；池化学生记住trait却无法迁移到新记录，34次运行中身份记忆越多state学得越少；固定效应蒸馏使state一致性提升逾两倍，个体内标签改善而年龄性别无改善，增益在两个骨干网络和两个额外数据库上成立。

Conclusion: 对记录进行条件化可让蒸馏转向可穿戴设备所监测的个体内变化，为蒸馏内容诊断提供了新指标与方法。

Abstract: ECG is widely used to teach PPG-only models, yet what it teaches is unexamined. Wearables are valued for tracking how a person's cardiovascular state changes, but ECG-to-PPG distillation mostly learns who the person is. A per-recording mean, the trait, holds 40-59% of a frozen ECG teacher's target, and pooled students memorise it without carrying it to new recordings. The raw alignment cosine misses this, since a constant predictor scores 0.793. Across 34 runs, the more identity a student memorises, the less state it learns. Fixed-effects distillation subtracts each recording's mean from prediction and target, so the trait cancels exactly, while a pooled anchor keeps it. State agreement more than doubles, within-person labels improve while age and sex do not, and the gain holds on two backbones and two further databases. Conditioning on the recording turns distillation toward the within-person changes that wearables monitor.

</details>


### [40] [Explaining the Saliency Map Sparsity of Adversarially-Trained Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10666)
*Yannick Lunk,Atell Yehor Krasnopolsky,Damien Garreau,Leon Bungert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为两层ReLU网络构建了对抗训练中梯度显著性图稀疏性的理论解释，证明最小化器在适当极限下收敛到具有最小梯度与Barron范数的贝叶斯分类器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络预测的可解释性对其安全部署至关重要，显著性图是计算机视觉中常用的解释形式。经验上观察到对抗训练网络的梯度显著性图呈现明显稀疏性，但缺乏理论解释，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 针对两层ReLU网络，基于对抗训练等价于经验风险最小化加上权重衰减惩罚与对抗全变差项的结论（对特定损失函数成立）。在数据点与神经元数量增长、正则化参数以适当速率趋于零的极限下，分析最小化器的收敛行为，并结合$\ell_\infty$攻击下梯度范数的各向异性特征进行刻画。

Result: 证明最小化器收敛到具有最小梯度范数与Barron范数的贝叶斯分类器。稀疏性源于$\ell_\infty$攻击下对抗训练的梯度范数具有各向异性，倾向于轴对齐/稀疏的梯度。实验上通过比较自然训练与对抗训练模型的梯度$\ell_1$范数及阈值化稀疏度加以验证。

Conclusion: 理论解释了对抗训练网络梯度显著性图稀疏的现象，揭示了其与$\ell_\infty$对抗训练下梯度各向异性偏好轴对齐/稀疏结构的联系，并通过实验予以支持。

Abstract: Understanding why deep neural networks make a given prediction is of great importance for their safe deployment. In computer vision, saliency maps, which highlight the image region most influential for a prediction, remain a widely-used form of explanation. An empirical observation is the apparent sparsity of gradient saliency maps of adversarially-trained neural networks. In this paper, we propose a theoretical explanation of this phenomenon for two-layer ReLU networks. We build on the established equivalence of adversarial training to the minimization of the empirical risk with weight-decay penalization and an added adversarial total variation term -- valid for certain loss functions. As the number of data points and neurons grows and the regularization parameters are sent to zero at appropriate rates, we prove that minimizers converge to a Bayes classifier with minimal gradient and Barron norm. Sparsity appears since for adversarial training with $\ell_\infty$-attacks the gradient norm is anisotropic and favors axis-aligned / sparse gradients. We illustrate our theoretical findings experimentally by evaluating the gradient $\ell_1$-norm and thresholded sparsity of naturally versus adversarially trained models.

</details>


### [41] [Conversational Task Disambiguation over Tabular Data: Leakage-Aware Formulation, Benchmark Suite, and Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10740)
*Nafiseh Ghoroghchian,Luis Scoccola,Tina Sedaghat,Omid Vaheb,Hannah Chen,Dino D'Agostino,Keyvan Golestan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出"模糊可验证任务"框架，将对话式表格任务消歧分解为asking policy与solution policy，并区分环境中的oracle与verifier，从而分离评估消歧能力与求解能力、形式化oracle leakage。作者以AmbiTab统一六个模糊数据集并在text-to-SQL上实例化，提供基线、指标、无judge泄漏诊断及asking policy的RL训练目标；训练后的asker在六个数据集的消歧指标上均提升，五个数据集的任务成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话式表格任务消歧的评估与训练缺乏泄漏感知的基础：任务成功率混合了agent的消歧能力与解答生成能力，还可能反映oracle leakage（用户模拟器泄露超出真实用户的信息）；同时已有数据集缺少对模糊性和访问边界的统一表示。

Method: 提出"ambiguous verifiable task"概念，形式化模糊性与消解过程；将agent分解为asking policy与solution policy，将环境分解为oracle与verifier；据此给出分离评估任务消歧与解答生成的基线与指标、oracle leakage的形式化定义、无需judge的泄漏诊断方法，以及asking policy的训练目标。在text-to-SQL中以AmbiTab实例化该框架，统一六个模糊数据集，明确agent、oracle与verifier的访问权限。

Result: 评估了澄清策略与oracle leakage，并用强化学习训练asking policy。训练后的asker在全部六个数据集上提升消歧指标，在五个数据集上提升任务成功率；泄漏诊断量化了训练对oracle leakage的影响。

Conclusion: 该工作为对话式表格任务消歧提供了泄漏感知的统一评估与训练基础，能够分离衡量消歧与解答生成能力，并为asking policy的优化与oracle leakage的监控提供可操作工具。

Abstract: Conversational task disambiguation over tabular data uses dialogue to resolve missing information about a user's intended task before producing a solution over tables or databases. Existing evaluation and training lack a leakage-aware foundation. Task success mixes the agent's disambiguation and solution-generation capabilities and can also reflect oracle leakage, that is, information that a user simulator reveals beyond what a real user would. Existing datasets also lack a shared representation of ambiguities and access boundaries. We introduce the notion of an ambiguous verifiable task, which formalizes ambiguities and resolutions, decomposing the agent into an asking policy and a solution policy, and the environment into an oracle and verifier. This framework provides baselines and metrics for evaluating task disambiguation separately from solution generation, formal definitions of oracle leakage, judge-free leakage diagnostics, and a training objective for the asking policy. We instantiate the framework in text-to-SQL with AmbiTab, a benchmark suite that unifies six ambiguous datasets under a common representation specifying what the agent, oracle, and verifier may access. We evaluate clarification strategies and oracle leakage, and train an asking policy with reinforcement learning. The trained asker improves our disambiguation metrics on all six datasets and task success on five, and our leakage diagnostics measure how training affects oracle leakage.

</details>


### [42] [NEMORA: Neural Equivariant Multipole Operators for Long-Range Atomistic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10776)
*Jay L. Kaplan,Samuel Varner,Rebecca Willett,Juan J. de Pablo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NEMORA 提出一种神经等变多极算子，将快速多极子方法（FMM）解析多极展开与平移算子推广为可学习的等变算子，在自适应空间层级上实现线性复杂度长程张量建模，显著降低非局域基准上的力与能量误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有等变图神经网络原子间势能模型虽短程精度高，但有限截断半径截断长程信息流，且堆叠消息传递层会导致过平滑与过挤压。已有长程扩展方法存在局限：或采用固定解析传播核，或将长程通信限制为标量或保度通道，或仅近似等变，或计算代价超线性。如何在保持多尺度多体表达能力的同时，将可学习的长程等变传输与高效扩展性结合，仍是核心挑战。

Method: NEMORA 是快速多极子方法（FMM）的神经等变扩展。它在自适应空间层级上，将 FMM 的解析多极展开与平移算子泛化为可学习的等变对应物；这些算子耦合角向自由度，跨长度尺度构建多体相互作用。模型保留 FMM 的层级组织与解析径向因子作为物理归纳偏置，同时学习数据依赖的长程耦合，并具备线性时间与内存复杂度。

Result: NEMORA 可处理比其它长程方法更大的体系，达到数十万原子规模；可增强对称约束与非约束的短程骨干网络。在非局域基准上，相对短程骨干将力误差降低超过一个数量级，能量误差降低最高达三个数量级；精度优于或可竞争于现有长程扩展方法。

Conclusion: NEMORA 通过将 FMM 的层级结构与解析径向因子作为归纳偏置并与可学习等变算子结合，成功实现了线性复杂度、可扩展到大规模体系的长程张量表示学习，在非局域任务上显著提升精度，为机器学习的原子间势能模型提供了高效且表达力强的长程建模方案。

Abstract: Equivariant graph neural networks have emerged as foundational architectures for machine-learned interatomic potentials, approaching quantum-chemical accuracy at a fraction of the computational cost. These models describe local atomic environments accurately, but finite spatial cutoffs truncate long-range information flow, and stacking message-passing layers can lead to over-smoothing and over-squashing. Existing long-range extensions either prescribe a fixed analytical propagation kernel, restrict long-range communication to scalars or degree-preserving channels, are only approximately equivariant, or incur super-linear computational cost. Combining learnable long-range equivariant transport with multiscale many-body expressivity and efficient scaling for larger systems remains a central challenge. We introduce Neural Equivariant Multipole Operators (NEMORA), a neural equivariant extension of the Fast Multipole Method (FMM) for learning long-range tensorial representations. NEMORA generalizes the FMM's analytical multipole expansion and translation operators to learned equivariant counterparts on an adaptive spatial hierarchy. Its operators couple angular degrees and form many-body interactions across length scales, retaining the FMM's hierarchical organization and analytical radial factors as physical inductive biases while learning data-dependent long-range couplings. NEMORA evaluates in linear time and memory complexity, allowing it to treat larger systems than other long-range methods reaching hundreds of thousands of atoms, and it augments both symmetry-constrained and unconstrained short-range backbones. On non-local benchmarks, it reduces force and energy errors relative to the short-range backbones by over an order of magnitude and up to three orders of magnitude, respectively, which is better than or competitive with existing long-range extensions in accuracy.

</details>


### [43] [MemoWM: How World Models Change What Agents Need to Remember](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10778)
*Bingfan Zeng,Zhisheng Chen,Chenbo Sang,Zhengwei Xie,Jinpeng Wang,Xiangchen Guan,Rui Qian,Zheng Lu,Jingwei Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MemoWM，一种以世界模型为条件进行记忆分配与压缩的框架，通过共享预测压缩保留信息并重建被丢弃内容，在长期智能体记忆任务中同时提升准确率并大幅降低逐经验存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期运行的智能体随着经验积累面临持续增长的存储压力。世界模型可捕捉可复用的规律，从而减少每条经验所需存储的信息量，但如何在给定世界模型下分配记忆尚缺乏明确的问题形式化。

Method: 将记忆分配问题形式化为以世界模型为条件的存储优化问题。MemoWM 利用共享预测来压缩保留的信息，并据此重建被省略的内容；其任务感知分配准则在重建误差的期望影响与存储成本之间进行权衡，保留具有超出预测先验的下游价值的信息。

Result: 在五个长期智能体记忆基准上，MemoWM 平均答案准确率达 42.42%，比最强基线高 2.62 个百分点；同时相对存储效率最高的基线 MIRIX，平均逐经验存储降低 53.9%。分析进一步表明，更强的世界模型可在相当任务质量下降低单条经验存储；将模型参数量纳入考量后，存在共享模型容量与重复存储成本之间的权衡，且当保留更多交互时，最小化总存储的最优容量随之增大。

Conclusion: 以世界模型为条件的记忆分配能够有效压缩经验存储，同时保持甚至提升任务表现；实践中需根据保留交互的规模在共享模型容量与重复存储成本之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Long-term agents face growing storage demands as they accumulate experience. World models capture reusable regularities that can reduce the information stored for each experience. We formulate the problem of memory allocation conditioned on a world model and introduce MemoWM, a framework that uses shared predictions to compress retained information and reconstruct omitted content. Its task-aware allocation rule balances the expected impact of reconstruction errors against storage cost, retaining information with downstream value beyond the predictive prior. Across five long-term agent-memory benchmarks, MemoWM achieves 42.42\% average answer accuracy, exceeding the strongest baseline by 2.62 percentage points, while reducing average experience-specific storage by 53.9\% relative to MIRIX, the most storage-efficient baseline. Further analysis shows that stronger world models reduce per-experience storage at comparable task quality. Accounting for model parameters reveals a trade-off between shared model capacity and recurring storage costs, with the capacity that minimizes total storage increasing as more interactions are retained. Our code is available at https://github.com/Feld-maxiu/MemoWM.

</details>


### [44] [How Many Repeated Pairwise Comparisons Are Needed for Ranking under Heterogeneity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10795)
*Shashaank Aiyer,Han Shao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究在每个用户-任务情境中进行多少次重复成对比较才能（以及足以）恢复排名，针对异质Bradley-Terry模型给出了下界与更优算法，并证明对数依赖的最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好随用户与任务变化时，Golz等（2025）表明每用户仅一次比较即使有任意多用户也不足以识别平均效用最高的方案。因此需要探究在每个用户-任务情境内需要多少重复比较才能恢复排名。

Method: 在固定逆温度的异质Bradley-Terry模型下：先提出基于MLE的朴素算法；随后提出两个MLE变体和一个随机化的"俄罗斯轮盘赌"式算法。

Result: 朴素MLE算法需要每情境Ω(1/Δ²)次重复比较；两个MLE变体与俄罗斯轮盘赌算法仅需每情境O(log(1/Δ))次即可恢复排名，并证明该对数依赖是最优的。俄罗斯轮盘赌算法在最坏情形之外，期望每情境仅用O(1)次比较。合成实验与基于Arena数据的半合成实验在不同偏好异质程度和情境分布下对比了四种算法。

Conclusion: 每情境重复比较次数存在对数级的最优下界，俄罗斯轮盘赌算法在理论上达到该最优性，且平均情形下开销极低，实验验证了不同异质性与情境分布下的表现差异。

Abstract: We study ranking models by population-average utility from pairwise comparisons when preferences vary across users and tasks. Prior work shows that a single comparison per user can be insufficient to identify the alternative with the highest average utility, even with arbitrarily many users (Golz et al., 2025). We investigate how many repeated comparisons within each user-task context are necessary and sufficient for ranking recovery. Under a heterogeneous Bradley-Terry model with fixed inverse temperature, we start with a naive MLE-based algorithm that requires $Ω(1/Δ^2)$ repeated comparisons per context to ensure ranking recovery. We then present two MLE-based variants and a randomized Russian Roulette-style algorithm that recover the ranking using $O(\log(1/Δ))$ repeated comparisons per context, and we prove that this logarithmic dependence is optimal. Despite this worst-case requirement, our Russian Roulette algorithm uses only $O(1)$ comparisons per context in expectation. Synthetic experiments and semi-synthetic experiments based on Arena data compare the four algorithms in settings with varying levels of preference heterogeneity and under varying context distributions.

</details>


### [45] [Symbolic Density Estimators for Unnormalized Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10807)
*Vikas Kanaujia,Riyansha Singh,Shashank Sharma,Vipul Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出深度生成模型与符号回归结合的框架，从样本中估计非归一化分布符号表达式，并在XY模型与φ⁴理论中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从观测样本估计概率密度函数（PDF）的符号或解析形式是统计与计算建模的基础难题，可得到刻画现象的可解释、可泛化关系。传统方法高度依赖领域专家与先验知识来选取函数形式或参数族，再通过参数估计确定系数，迁移性和自动化程度有限。

Method: 构建利用领域先验知识（如相互作用范围、预定义基元函数集合）的框架，从样本估计非归一化分布的符号表达式；将深度生成模型与符号回归（SR）结合，引入分解大分布等归纳偏置保持问题可解；所考察的深度生成模型包括基于似然的流模型与基于分数的模型。

Result: 实验验证了框架在多变量玩具分布以及计算物理中的格子模型（XY模型与φ⁴理论）上估计密度函数的有效性；在φ⁴理论重整化问题中，框架直接从样本估计不同尺度下哈密顿函数的紧凑符号近似。

Conclusion: 该框架能够从样本中自动获得紧凑的符号近似表达式，其形式在非微扰情形下用传统微扰或解析方法难以推导，显示深度生成模型与符号回归结合在符号密度估计与物理重整化中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Estimating the symbolic or analytical form of probability density functions (PDFs) from observed samples is a fundamental challenge in statistical and computational modelling. This process is critical for deriving interpretable and generalizable relationships characterizing the underlying phenomenon. Traditionally, this estimation depends strongly on domain expertise and prior field-specific knowledge, with experts selecting appropriate functional forms or parametric families based on empirical evidence and theoretical understanding. The coefficients of these forms are then typically determined through parameter estimation. In this paper, we develop a framework for estimating symbolic expressions of unnormalized distributions from observed samples using domain-specific prior knowledge, such as the range of interactions and a predefined set of primitive functions. We integrate deep generative models with symbolic regression (SR), incorporating inductive biases, such as factorizing large distributions, to keep the problem tractable. The deep generative models we examine include likelihood-based models, viz., flow models, and score-based models. Experiments show the effectiveness of the proposed framework for estimating density functions for multivariate toy distributions as well as lattices from computational physics, namely, XY model and $φ^4$ theory. When applied to the renormalization problem in $φ^4$ theory, the proposed framework estimates compact symbolic approximations of the hamiltonian function at different scales directly from samples, yielding expressions that may be challenging to derive using traditional perturbative or analytic approaches in nonperturbative settings.

</details>


### [46] [Controlled Acquisition and Abstention in Three-Channel Score Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10808)
*Mengzhe Geng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在受控三分数基准中研究多模态一致性决策：策略观察两个带符号分数，可按成本请求第三个分数或弃权。结果区分了全模态精度与请求前选择价值，表明选择价值取决于预算与已观察对的排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当音频、视频与文本彼此不一致时，仅有精度无法说明应获取额外来源还是弃权，需要研究在成本与弃权约束下的选择性决策问题。

Method: 构建受控三分数基准：策略观察两个带符号分数，可付费请求第三个分数并允许弃权；奖励机制特定，仅对指定歧义机制弃权才算正确，混合污染下弃权受罚。采用阈值策略、三分数多数参考、仅训练集价值选择器，并设置匹配控制与匹配预算测试（10%、25%、50%、63.7%），在83个随机种子上评估；另在留出演员的情绪片段上做八帧融合与请求路由分析。

Result: 部分留出合成划分上，阈值策略达到目标决策精度0.789±0.006、效用0.481±0.014；三分数多数参考为0.626±0.008与0.252±0.016但使用更多信息。匹配预算下，仅训练价值选择器在10%与25%预算优于无查询与匹配随机策略，而配对不确定性在所有预算下效用更高；在50%与63.7%预算下选择器效用下降，尽管非歧义精度略高。若弃权全部计为错误，多数法在效用上超过阈值策略。中心时间设置下全轨迹控制与神经模型相当，位置扰动可区分二者。留出演员情绪片段中，两对编码器的八帧融合精度差异符号相反，演员区间均含零，匹配请求路由增益小且不确定。

Conclusion: 全模态精度与请求前选择价值是不同概念；选择价值依赖预算与已观察分数对的排序，阈值策略在请求效率上占优，但其相对优势取决于歧义奖励设定。

Abstract: When audio, video, and text disagree, accuracy alone does not show whether to acquire another source or abstain. We study these choices in a controlled three-score benchmark: a policy observes two signed scores, may request the third at a cost, and can abstain. The primary reward is mechanism-specific: abstention is correct only for one designated ambiguity mechanism and is penalized under mixed corruption. Matched controls show that a threshold policy matches always-request decisions with fewer requests; its advantage over always-answer fusion depends on the reward assigned to that ambiguity. On a partially held-out synthetic split, the threshold policy reaches 0.789 +/- 0.006 targeted decision accuracy and 0.481 +/- 0.014 utility across 83 seeds. A three-score majority reference reaches 0.626 +/- 0.008 and 0.252 +/- 0.016, but uses more information. In a matched-budget test, a train-only value selector improves utility over no-query and matched-random policies at 10% and 25% budgets, while pair uncertainty has higher utility at every budget. At 50% and 63.7% budgets, the selector lowers utility despite slightly higher non-ambiguous accuracy. If all abstentions are scored incorrect, majority outranks the threshold policy in utility. At a central temporal setting, full-trace controls match the neural models while position perturbations separate them. On held-out-actor emotion clips, eight-frame fusion has opposite-signed accuracy differences for two encoder pairs, with both actor intervals containing zero; matched-request routing gains are small and uncertain. These results separate full-modality accuracy from pre-request selection value and show that selection value depends on budget and the observed-pair ranking.

</details>


### [47] [Evaluating Rubric Generation with Interventional Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10809)
*Erik Skalnes,Layne C. Price,Raviteja Anantha,Michael Oberst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Interventional Transfer (IT) 方法，用于大规模评估 LLM 生成的评分细则质量，并在 HealthBench 案例中发现生成细则与专家细则之间存在不对称迁移现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实例级评分细则需要专家知识，难以规模化，因此研究转向用 LLM 自动生成细则；但即便有专家细则作参照，如何规模化评估生成细则的质量仍不清楚。现有评估方法无法揭示细则间的一致性结构。

Method: 提出 Interventional Transfer (IT)：核心假设是两条细则若相似，则当对回答进行扰动使其通过/未通过其中一条时，两条细则的评分会协同变化。通过不同类型的干预迁移来评估生成细则在不同任务中的效用。

Result: 在 HealthBench 案例中，对 Qwen3.8-27B、Deepseek-V4-Flash、Opus-5 生成的细则进行评估，用于评价 GPT-5.6-Terra 的回答，发现存在不对称性：使回答变差的扰动可从生成/专家细则迁移到对应专家/生成细则上（分数下降），但使某条细则得分提升的扰动无法可靠迁移。

Conclusion: 该不对称性对使用 LLM 生成细则进行性能监控和爬山优化具有启示意义；与现有细则评估方法相比，IT 能揭示这些方法未能呈现的不对称现象。

Abstract: Instance-specific rubrics are common in AI benchmarks where reliable evaluation requires specific expert knowledge. This approach is difficult to scale, prompting research into the generation of rubrics with large language models (LLMs). However, even when expert rubrics are available as references, it is unclear how to productively evaluate the quality of generated rubrics at scale. In this paper, we introduce a method for the evaluation of rubric generation, which we call Interventional Transfer (IT), based on the idea that two rubrics are similar if they move together when a response is perturbed to pass/fail one of them. In contrast to existing approaches for evaluation of rubric generation, we argue that different forms of interventional transfer can be used to evaluate the utility of generated rubrics for different tasks. For instance, we apply this approach in a case study on HealthBench, where we demonstrate an asymmetry in rubrics generated by Qwen3.8-27B, Deepseek-V4-Flash, and Opus-5, used to evaluate responses from GPT-5.6-Terra. Perturbations that degrade responses according to the generated/expert rubric transfer into lower scores on the corresponding expert/generated rubric, but perturbations that improve on one rubric do not reliably transfer into higher scores on the other. We argue that this finding has implications for the usage of LLM-generated rubrics for performance monitoring and hill-climbing. We contrast our approach with existing approaches for rubric evaluation, which do not surface the same asymmetry that we observe.

</details>


### [48] [MotherTree: Meta-learning on synthetic data improves decision tree training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10832)
*Ziyuan Wang,Fredrik D. Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MotherTree 是一种表格 Transformer，通过元学习决策树归纳，在单次前向传播中为新任务输出可直接检视的硬轴对齐决策树，且无需参考树监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统决策树算法模型透明、可独立部署，但每个新任务都需从头训练；表格基础模型虽能通过合成先验元学习实现小样本强预测，却只产生无法单独检视的上下文预测结果。二者优势难以兼得。

Method: 提出 MotherTree：用表格 Transformer 元学习决策树归纳。给定新任务的训练集，模型一次前向传播即输出硬轴对齐决策树；在合成先验上用随机梯度下降预训练，不需要参考树作为监督信号。

Result: 在样本量受控的基准测试中，与递归划分、梯度树学习、全局最优树及表格基础模型蒸馏等常用方法生成的同尺寸树性能相当；持续优于从头梯度学习，且作为强初始化器，经任务微调后在所有基准与样本量上均优于对应的从头学习器。

Conclusion: 元学习可为学习独立、小型决策树分类器提供有效归纳偏置，兼顾透明可检视性与小样本学习效率。

Abstract: Conventional decision tree algorithms produce effective, transparent models that can be audited, communicated, and deployed independently of the training data, but require learning every new task from scratch. In contrast, tabular foundation models demonstrate that meta-learning from a synthetic prior distribution enables strong in-context prediction for previously unseen tasks, especially in small-sample regimes. However, this approach does not produce a standalone model that can be inspected in isolation. We introduce MotherTree, a tabular transformer that meta-learns decision tree induction: given a training set for a new task, it outputs a hard, axis-aligned decision tree, equivalent in form to classically trained trees, in a single forward pass. MotherTree is pre-trained on a synthetic prior using stochastic gradient descent without requiring reference trees for supervision. On established benchmarks with controlled sample size, the approach is competitive with size-matched trees from common algorithms: recursive partitioning, gradient-based tree learning, globally optimal trees, and distillation from tabular foundation models. Notably, MotherTree consistently improves over from-scratch gradient-based learning and acts as a strong initializer: task-specific tuning of the generated tree outperforms the corresponding from-scratch learner on all benchmarks and sample sizes. These results show that meta-learning can provide effective inductive biases for learning stand-alone, small decision tree classifiers.

</details>


### [49] [Amortized Off-Policy Evaluation for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10848)
*Younwoo Choi,Leo Feng,Vincent Liu,Haanvid Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PFN-OPE，一种先验数据拟合网络，将离策略评估（OPE）分摊到上下文赌博机任务分布上，单次前向传播即可估计价值，无需逐任务拟合，在奖励偏移场景下误差比最强基线降低2.0–9.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确评估是选择部署哪个LLM的核心，但在真实流量上测试候选模型会让真实用户暴露于未验证模型，因此团队只能在已部署模型产生的数据上离线评估，即离策略评估（OPE）。该场景面临两种分布偏移：后训练更新导致响应与日志数据分歧（策略偏移），以及业务需求变化导致奖励定义改变（奖励偏移）。经典OPE方法按任务定义、每遇新日志数据或新奖励定义都需重新拟合，不适合这种持续部署场景。

Method: PFN-OPE是一种先验数据拟合网络，将OPE跨上下文赌博机任务分布进行分摊（amortize）。它在一个由LLM响应池构成、并由多个奖励函数打分（同时包含策略偏移与奖励偏移）的任务集合上预训练一次；测试时，将一份日志数据集与每个提示对应的一个采样目标响应映射为价值估计，仅需单次前向传播，无需逐任务拟合。

Result: 在HelpSteer2与UltraFeedback数据集，配合Qwen、Llama和Gemma策略，PFN-OPE在所有测试配置的奖励偏移设置下，误差比最佳基线低2.0至9.3倍。

Conclusion: PFN-OPE通过元学习式分摊消除逐任务拟合需求，有效应对策略偏移与奖励偏移，为LLM持续部署场景提供了一种高效、准确的离线评估方案。

Abstract: Accurate evaluation is central to selecting which LLM to deploy, yet testing a candidate on live traffic exposes real users to an unvetted model. Teams therefore evaluate candidates offline, on data produced by already-deployed models. This is off-policy evaluation (OPE), and it faces two distribution shifts: as a model is updated in post-training, its responses diverge from the logged ones (policy shift), and the reward definition under which it is judged changes with business requirements (reward shift). Classical OPE methods are ill-suited to this continual-deployment setting because they are defined per task and require fitting from scratch on every new logged dataset or reward definition. To address this, we propose PFN-OPE, a prior-data fitted network that amortizes OPE across a distribution of contextual-bandit tasks. We pretrain it once on tasks constructed from a pool of LLM responses scored by several reward functions, in which both shifts occur. At test-time it maps a logged dataset and one sampled target response per prompt to a value estimate in a single forward pass, with no per-task fitting. On HelpSteer2 and UltraFeedback with Qwen, Llama, and Gemma policies, PFN-OPE achieves 2.0 to 9.3 times lower error than the best baselines across all tested configurations in the reward-shifted settings.

</details>


### [50] [KDFP: A first-principles approach to knowledge distillation in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10854)
*Ryan Swift,Konstantinos Psounis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KDFP方法，用于大语言模型的通用知识蒸馏，性能提升1.6%-4.9%，训练效率提升至多99.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型蒸馏工作多聚焦于后训练阶段的能力（如指令遵循、思维链推理、工具使用），而通用知识蒸馏研究存在明显空白，而该能力对边缘设备上高效、私密部署至关重要。

Method: 采用第一性原理方法，评估既有工作的经验教训并开展新探索，提出KDFP——一种面向大语言模型白盒通用知识蒸馏的新方法，并通过临时参数减少提升训练效率。

Result: 在9个基准上，KDFP相较现有方法性能提升1.6%-4.9%，同时通过临时参数减少将训练效率提升至多99.1%。

Conclusion: KDFP为现代大语言模型提供了有效的白盒通用知识蒸馏方案，兼顾性能与训练效率，为边缘部署等场景提供支持。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation is an established technique for improving the capabilities of small, efficient student models by training them with the representations of larger, more capable teacher models. Much of the recent work in the distillation of large language models (LLMs) has focused on distilling abilities learned during post-training, such as instruction following, chain-of-thought reasoning, and tool usage. This has left a large research gap in general knowledge distillation for LLMs, which is essential for developing efficient and private systems suitable for deployment on edge devices. We take a first-principles approach, evaluating previous lessons from prior works and conducting new explorations to develop a distillation methodology suitable for modern LLMs. We present KDFP, a novel methodology for white-box general knowledge distillation in LLMs. We demonstrate that KDFP outperforms existing methods by 1.6% $-$ 4.9% across 9 benchmarks while increasing training efficiency by up to 99.1% through ephemeral parameter reduction.

</details>


### [51] [Shape irregularity of Life-Like Network Automaton rules as an indicator of classification performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10867)
*Lucas C. S. Oliveira,Michiel Rollier,Jan Baetens,Odemir M. Bruno*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对基于生命游戏式网络自动机（LLNA）的复杂网络分类中转移规则选择的高成本组合优化瓶颈，提出以"锯齿度"（jaggedness）来刻画规则空间结构，并据此设计启发式规则搜索策略，在精度接近全局最优的同时大幅降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂网络分类需要兼具尺度不变性和计算高效性的高层结构表征。LLNA方法通过涌现的时间模式提取网络描述符，无需预先给定特征，但其效能受限于自动机转移规则选择这一高成本组合优化问题。现有文献依赖穷举搜索，在大规模应用中不可行，因此亟需揭示规则空间的结构并设计高效搜索策略。

Method: 作者提出"锯齿度"（jaggedness）这一性质，用于量化LLNA转移函数与锯齿形状的相似程度，揭示规则空间的内在结构。理论分析表明该指标可作为混沌性与敏感性的代理度量，而这两者是生成具有判别力的动态行为的关键。在此基础上，提出一种以锯齿度作为引导的启发式规则搜索策略，替代穷举搜索。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法在分类精度上达到全局最优值5%以内，同时相比穷举搜索减少了90%的计算开销。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了LLNA规则空间由"锯齿度"所刻画的内在结构，并提供了一种新颖高效的自动机方法优化框架，为基于自动机的模式识别任务中的规则选择问题提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Complex Network (CN) classification requires high-level structural characterizations that are both scale-invariant and computationally efficient. Methods based on Life-Like Network Automata (LLNA) offer an interesting way to extract network descriptors by leveraging emergent temporal patterns without requiring provided features, but their efficacy is bottlenecked by a high-cost combinatorial optimization problem: the selection of the automaton transition rule. While current literature relies on exhaustive searches that are unfeasible for large-scale applications, this work reveals that the rule space is fundamentally structured by a property we term ``jaggedness'', that quantifies the resemblance of a LLNA transition function with a sawtooth shape. We demonstrate that this metric acts as a theoretical proxy for chaoticity and sensitivity -- properties essential for generating discriminative dynamic behaviors among network categories. Moreover, we introduce a heuristic search strategy that uses jaggedness to guide the rule selection. Experimental results show that our approach achieves classification accuracies within 5% of the global optimum while reducing computational overhead by 90% compared to exhaustive approach. Our findings provide a novel, efficient, framework for optimizing automata-based methods for pattern recognition.

</details>


### [52] [Barron Optimal Transport I: Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10875)
*Evan Dogariu,Joan Bruna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出以Barron能量替代经典Brenier动能，构造新的最优测度输运度量，用以刻画神经网络表征复杂度，并用于分析生成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成建模与采样中，沿ODE/SDE将参考分布（如高斯）映射到目标分布，离散化后等价于深度残差网络，每层逼近瞬时速度。因此自然的问是：给定参考与目标测度，如何找到实现该输运的最经济神经网络表征。经典最优输运的平均动能L2未刻画网络复杂度与特征学习的自适应性。

Method: 以Benamou–Brenier的动能最优输运表述为起点，将平均动能L2能量替换为Barron能量——衡量用单个神经网络隐层表示给定向量的复杂度的自然范数，可捕捉特征学习的自适应性质，从而在概率测度空间上定义新度量，补充Wasserstein与Stein几何。

Result: 在该度量下量化了扩散生成建模的次优性：针对由神经网络pushforward高斯生成的数据，建立了超多项式的score逼近下界；并探讨了自适应性对替代生成模型的益处。配套论文利用Barron输运几何进行采样，通过特征自适应扩展Stein变分梯度方法的适用范围。

Conclusion: Barron输运几何提供了刻画神经网络复杂度与自适应性的新概率度量，既可证明扩散模型的score逼近下界，也可为特征自适应采样方法提供理论框架。

Abstract: Motivated by recent applications in generative modeling and sampling, we introduce a framework for optimal measure transport where cost captures the notion of neural network complexity. In transport-based generative models, samples from a reference distribution (e.g. Gaussian) are mapped to samples of a target distribution along ordinary or stochastic differential equations. These are implemented as deep residual networks when discretized in time, where each hidden layer approximates the associated instantaneous velocity. Thus, given a pair of target and reference measures, a natural question is to search for the most efficient neural representation that implements this transport.
  Our starting point is the kinetic formulation of OT, due to Benamou and Brenier. We replace the average kinetic $L^2$ energy by the \emph{Barron} energy \cite{bach2017breaking, ma2022barron}, a natural norm which measures the complexity of representing a given vector field with a neural hidden layer, and which captures the adaptive properties of feature learning. This defines a metric on the space of probability measures, complementing existing Wasserstein and Stein geometries.
  In this work we examine the properties of this metric in the context of generative modeling. As a first application, we quantify the suboptimality of diffusion generative modeling in the Barron geometry by establishing super-polynomial score approximation lower bounds for data generated by neural network pushforwards of the Gaussian. We then investigate the benefit of adaptivity as a way to study alternative generative models. In a companion paper \cite{companionpaper} we leverage the Barron transport geometry for sampling applications, extending the scope of Stein variational gradient methods via feature adaptation.

</details>


### [53] [Gen-PINNs: Generative Adversarial Physics Informed Neural Networks for solving partial differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10897)
*Muhammad M. Akmal,Kamy Sepehrnoori,Michael J. Pyrcz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出生成对抗物理信息神经网络（Gen-PINNs），一种无数据的确定性残差对抗框架，通过在生成器中引入动态加权物理信息损失、由判别器评估PDE残差特征，并结合傅里叶表示、正交谱诊断与改进梯度动态加权，有效缓解标准PINNs的谱偏差、损失失衡与优化停滞问题，在Burgers、Allen-Cahn和Kuramoto-Sivashinsky等方程上取得精度与收敛性的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PINNs在求解PDE时存在谱偏差、损失分量失衡以及优化器停滞等局限，难以处理具有尖锐或激波前沿的解；同时，尽管GAN在数据驱动建模中表现优异，其对抗学习在确定性物理信息PDE求解中的应用仍然有限。

Method: 提出Gen-PINNs统一确定性残差对抗框架：生成器通过动态加权的物理信息损失学习PDE解；多个判别器针对理想零残差状态评估互补的PDE残差特征；并引入傅里叶表示以捕捉高频空间内容、正交谱诊断以量化频率相关误差、以及改进的基于梯度的动态加权系统，用于平衡物理、初值、边值与对抗损失目标。

Result: 在非线性与高阶PDE（Burgers、Allen-Cahn、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程）上与标准PINNs对比，Gen-PINNs在尖锐前沿、刚性和高阶PDE求解中，精度与收敛性均取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 确定性残差对抗学习是求解困难非线性PDE的一种有效途径，Gen-PINNs展示了其在挑战性PDE问题上的潜力。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) are a widely used data-free method for solving Partial Differential Equations (PDEs) using machine learning. With recent advances in Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs), adversarial learning has shown strong capabilities for modeling complex data-driven problems; however, the use of GANs in deterministic physics-informed PDE solutions remains limited. In this work, we first identify limitations of standard PINNs for solving PDEs, including spectral bias, loss imbalance, and optimizer stagnation. We then propose Generative Adversarial Physics-Informed Neural Networks (Gen-PINNs), a unified deterministic residual-adversarial framework designed to improve data-free solutions of PDEs with sharp or shock-front behavior. The generator learns the underlying PDE solution using dynamically weighted physics-informed loss components, while separate discriminators evaluate complementary PDE residual features against ideal zero-residual states. The framework further develops and adapts several methodological components, including a Fourier representation for resolving high-frequency spatial content, an orthonormal spectral diagnostic for quantifying frequency-dependent solution errors, and a modified gradient-based dynamic weighting system for physics, initial-condition, boundary-condition, and adversarial loss objectives. Gen-PINNs is tested against standard PINNs on nonlinear and higher-order PDEs, including the Burgers, Allen-Cahn, and Kuramoto-Sivashinsky equations. The results demonstrate substantial improvements in accuracy and convergence across sharp-front, stiff, and higher-order PDE solutions, highlighting the potential of deterministic residual-adversarial learning as an effective approach for solving challenging nonlinear PDEs.

</details>


### [54] [How Hackable Is Your Speech Quality Metric? A Corrected Protocol, a Benchmark, and What Patching Buys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10899)
*Ali Alavi,Donald S. Williamson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出语音质量预测器可被攻击（hackability）的既有度量存在两大缺陷：未剥离处理链自身带来的分数偏移、以及单一攻击者不具代表性；提出新协议下重新评测并审计了封闭的"攻击-检测-修补"环路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音质量预测器越来越多被用作奖励信号，但其被"刷分"（hackability）的程度缺乏公认度量。现有测量方式存在偏差，导致防御效果的可比性与结论可靠性存疑，因此需要一个更严谨的度量协议。

Method: 1）以未扰动的往返（round trip）结果作为评测基线，剥离神经编解码器等处理链自身的分数漂移，而非以原始输入为基线；2）使用多个仅随机种子不同的攻击者进行评测，取最坏情况而非单一攻击者样本；3）在新协议下对比四个已发表预测器（NISQA、SSL-MOS、DNSMOS、UTMOS）的hackability；4）审计一个封闭的"攻击-检测-修补"循环，检验其加固效果、与随机扰动基线的对比、以及跨域代价，并测试针对修补后预测器后训练的增强器表现。

Result: 基线选择对测得的hackability影响可达四倍（某防御从0.31降至0.08）；五个仅随机种子不同的攻击者对同一预测器的成功率从0.00到0.38。新协议下四个预测器差异显著：NISQA在90%语句上被攻破，SSL-MOS 21%，DNSMOS 14%，UTMOS 6%。审计的修补环路仅在其自身攻击空间内加固预测器，幅度小于攻击者之间的离散度；随机扰动基线可与之持平；并造成跨域最多0.30系统SRCC的损失。针对修补后预测器后训练的增强器，其hackability大幅降低（PESQ -0.03 对比 -0.23）。

Conclusion: 语音质量预测器的hackability度量需明确定义基线（未扰动往返）并采用多攻击者最坏情况，否则防御结论不可靠；现有攻击-检测-修补方案存在过拟合于自身攻击空间、跨域性能下降等局限；作者公开代码、预注册与运行输出以支持可复现研究。

Abstract: Speech quality predictors are increasingly used as rewards, yet no agreed measure of their hackability exists. The usual measurement has two flaws. First, the perturbation reaches the predictor through a processing chain -- here a neural codec -- that shifts the score on its own, which scoring against the raw input charges to the attack. Referencing the unperturbed round trip instead changes measured hackability by up to a factor of four (0.31 to 0.08 for one defence). Second, one trained attacker is a sample, not a measurement: five attackers differing only in random seed reach success rates from 0.00 to 0.38 against one fixed predictor, so a defence claim needs the worst case over several. Under this protocol, four published predictors differ widely: NISQA is hacked on 90% of utterances, SSL-MOS on 21%, DNSMOS on 14% and UTMOS on 6%. We then audit a closed attack-detect-patch loop. It hardens the predictor only in its own attack space, by less than the spread between attackers; a random-perturbation baseline matches it; and it costs up to 0.30 system SRCC out of domain. Enhancers post-trained against patched predictors hack them far less (PESQ -0.03 versus -0.23). Code, preregistration and run outputs are released.

</details>


### [55] [Language Models for Page-Level Layout Decisions in E-commerce Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10920)
*Varun Joshi,Eva C. Song,ChengXiang Zhai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究电商搜索页布局（二级堆叠插入位置）的离线评估方法，发现基于表示的语言模型方法在预测用户参与度上优于提示词方法，可作为离线布局评估的可靠基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商搜索页中，二级堆叠等推荐模块的插入位置会影响用户体验：位置合适可提升参与度，位置不当则会打断浏览流程并削弱主结果。传统搜索排序已有交错实验等成熟评估手段，但页面级布局决策的评估仍依赖昂贵的在线A/B测试，因此需要可扩展的离线评估方法。

Method: 将语言模型作为可扩展的评估器，针对特定位置是否插入二级堆叠这一布局决策，系统比较三类方法：直接基于提示词的判断、基于提示词派生特征的判断，以及基于表示的（representation-based）方法。

Result: 在预测用户参与度方面，基于表示的方法持续优于基于提示词的判断方法。 

Conclusion: 基于表示的语言模型方法为电商搜索的离线页面级布局评估提供了可靠基础，有望减少对在线A/B测试的依赖。

Abstract: E-commerce search pages are critical touchpoints for millions of online shoppers. While traditional search engines return a ranked list of results, modern E-commerce search pages increasingly incorporate recommender system modules -- for example, secondary stacks that surface alternative product groupings at specific positions. When introduced appropriately, secondary stacks can improve user engagement; however, suboptimal placement may disrupt browsing flow and degrade the primary results. Unlike traditional search ranking, where evaluation techniques such as interleaving are well established, evaluating page-level layout changes e.g., when and where to insert a secondary stack remains challenging without costly online A/B testing. To address this, we study offline methods for evaluating whether a given layout decision -- specifically, the inclusion of a secondary stack at a particular position -- is beneficial to users. We investigate language models as scalable evaluators by comparing direct prompt-based, prompt-derived feature, and representation-based methods. Our results show that representation-based approaches consistently outperform prompt-based judging in predicting user engagement, suggesting they provide a reliable foundation for offline layout evaluation in E-commerce search.

</details>


### [56] [Adaptive Multi-Discriminator WGAN Framework for Resource-Constrained Internet of Vehicles Using Reinforcement Learning and Game Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10926)
*Farhoud Jafari Kaleibar,Amr M. Zaki,Marin Litoiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应多判别器Wasserstein GAN（MD-WGAN）框架，用强化学习与博弈论协同调度IoV中GAN训练资源，实现精度与资源效率兼顾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将机器学习负载作为网络服务管理带来新的资源编排难题：节点分配、通信与计算预算划分、以及在移动性导致连通性与节点可用性变化时维持服务质量。在车联网（IoV）中部署GAN是此类难题的严苛实例：资源受限、拓扑动态、多目标互斥，传统GAN架构难以同时实现高精度、高效资源利用、低时延与低通信开销。

Method: 提出自适应多判别器Wasserstein GAN（MD-WGAN）框架，将强化学习与博弈论协同相结合。路侧单元承载生成器，并配对DQN智能体，用于选择判别器子集并在移动车载节点上管理分布式训练；随后通过博弈论协同步骤在生成器与判别器之间分配训练轮次。统一优化目标将对抗学习质量与资源效率、通信开销和车辆约束下的时延联系起来，使框架能随网络条件变化持续调整训练行为。

Result: 在真实NGSIM轨迹数据上评估：预测精度与最先进GAN基线相当，取得所有方法中最低的RMSE（1.029）与MAE（0.894）；平均CPU利用率降低约28%，平均内存使用降低约6%，通信开销与时延具有竞争力。

Conclusion: MD-WGAN通过强化学习驱动的判别器子集选择与博弈论轮次分配，在IoV动态约束下联合优化对抗学习质量与资源、通信、时延开销，可在不牺牲预测精度的前提下显著提升资源效率。

Abstract: Managing machine learning workloads as a network service introduces a resource-orchestration problem distinct from conventional model training; which nodes should be allocated to a task, how communication and computation budgets should be divided among them, and how service quality should be sustained as connectivity and node availability change with mobility. Deploying Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) in Internet of Vehicles (IoV) environments is a demanding instance of this problem; resource constraints, dynamic network topologies, and competing optimization objectives mean that traditional GAN architectures cannot simultaneously achieve high accuracy, efficient resource use, low delay, and low communication overhead. This paper introduces an adaptive multi-discriminator Wasserstein GAN (MD-WGAN) framework that integrates reinforcement learning with game-theoretic coordination to address these challenges jointly. In our framework, roadside units host generators paired with Deep Q-Network (DQN) agents that select discriminator subsets and manage distributed training across mobile vehicular nodes, while a game-theoretic coordination step allocates training epochs between generators and discriminators. A unified optimization objective ties adversarial learning quality to resource efficiency, communication overhead, and latency under vehicular constraints, allowing the framework to continuously adapt its training behavior as network conditions change. Evaluation on real-world NGSIM trajectory data shows that the framework attains prediction accuracy comparable to state-of-the-art GAN baselines - the lowest RMSE (1.029) and MAE (0.894) among all evaluated methods - while markedly improving resource efficiency: average CPU utilization is reduced by roughly 28% and mean memory usage by roughly 6%, at competitive communication overhead and latency.

</details>


### [57] [Coefficient Calibration as Selection Pressure in Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10931)
*Mattia Billa,Veronica Guidetti,Federica Mandreoli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DSCA校准策略，将优化数据按协变量分布划分等量子集，对每个候选结构在各子集独立校准并取参数均值作为评估点，从而在符号回归搜索中引入对协变量偏移稳健性的选择压力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模因符号回归中候选结构在系数校准后进行比较，校准协议本身参与进化选择。标准集中式校准在混合样本的联合最优处评估每个结构，忽略该校准在协变量偏移下的稳定性，可能偏向于依赖样本特定系数的结构。

Method: DSCA（Dirichlet-Sinkhorn Constant Averaging）：将优化数据划分为协变量分布不同的等尺寸子集；对每个候选结构在每个子集上独立校准；在所得参数均值处评估该候选，以此作为选择依据。

Result: 理论上，对正确设定且可识别的表达式，DSCA相对集中式校准的额外损失在总体层面趋于消失；在模型误设定下，当各子集校准无法聚合到联合最优时，该额外损失持续存在。在合成基准和十个真实数据集上，无论以负对数似然还是AIC、BIC进行选择，DSCA在函数恢复和精度-复杂度权衡上均优于集中式BFGS与Levenberg-Marquardt校准。机制分析表明DSCA额外损失与泛化差距相关，且重复集中式拟合无法复现该效应。

Conclusion: 受控的异质校准为符号回归搜索提供了互补的选择压力来源，可改善函数恢复与精度-复杂度权衡。

Abstract: In memetic symbolic regression, candidate structures are compared after coefficient calibration, so the calibration protocol itself contributes to evolutionary selection. Standard centralized calibration evaluates each structure at its pooled-sample optimum, ignoring how stable this calibration is under covariate shifts, and can thus favor structures whose fit relies on sample-specific coefficients.
  We propose Dirichlet-Sinkhorn Constant Averaging (DSCA), a calibration strategy that partitions the optimization data into equally sized subsets with different covariate distributions, calibrates each candidate independently on every partition, and evaluates it at the mean of the resulting parameters. We show that the excess loss of DSCA relative to centralized calibration vanishes at the population level for correctly specified, identifiable expressions, whereas under misspecification it persists when partition-specific calibrations do not aggregate to the pooled optimum.
  On synthetic benchmarks and ten real-world datasets, DSCA improves functional recovery and the accuracy-complexity trade-off over centralized Broyden-Fletcher-Goldfarb-Shanno and Levenberg-Marquardt calibration, under selection by negative log-likelihood and by the Akaike and Bayesian information criteria. Mechanism analyses associate the DSCA excess loss with the generalization gap and show that the effect is not reproduced by repeated centralized fitting. These results indicate that controlled heterogeneous calibration provides a complementary source of selection pressure in symbolic-regression search.

</details>


### [58] [World-Model Policy Arbiter for Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10932)
*Junwei Quan,Evgenii Opryshko,Nicholas Rhinehart,Igor Gilitschenski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 WMPA：在测试时用世界模型想象各冻结策略的未来，以共享目标条件价值函数评分，按短承诺区间选策略执行，宏平均成功率从44%提升至58%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线目标条件强化学习（GCRL）算法多样，但没有单一算法能在所有环境、目标乃至同一任务的不同阶段保持最优，因此仅部署单一最佳策略并非最优选择。作者转而探索将一组冻结的目标条件策略组成"策略组合"，在每个状态决定由哪个策略行动，以获取组合收益。

Method: 在每状态选策略面临三个困难：策略自带的价值函数尺度不同甚至缺失，无法直接比较；只能一次执行一个策略，却需评估各策略可能到达的状态；频繁切换会导致控制不稳定。为此提出 World-Model Policy Arbiter（WMPA）：给定一组冻结策略，在学习到的状态空间世界模型中分别 rollout 每个策略，用共享的目标条件价值函数评估这些"想象未来"，选出得分最高者为当前策略，并执行一个短承诺区间后再进行下一轮仲裁。方法仅需一个冻结目标条件策略库，无需重训练策略，也不需要任务特定的先验知识。

Result: 在 OGBench 官方评测协议下，覆盖迷宫导航以及立方体、场景与拼图操作共18个基于状态的（state-based）数据集：WMPA 将宏平均成功率从每个数据集上最佳单策略的44%提升至58%，并在12个数据集上取得统计显著增益，其中包括 cube-double-play 提升33个百分点、scene-play 提升36个百分点。

Conclusion: 在测试时对冻结目标条件策略进行组合仲裁，可显著优于逐数据集挑选的最佳单策略，且无需重训练与特权知识，说明策略组合（portfolio）范式在离线GCRL中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) has produced a diverse set of goal-reaching algorithms, yet no single algorithm performs best across environments, goals, and even different phases of the same task. Rather than deploying only the best-performing policy, we ask whether a set of frozen goal-conditioned policies can be used collectively as a portfolio, deciding at every state which policy should act. Choosing a policy at each state is not straightforward. The policies' own value functions cannot be compared directly: they may use different scales, and some policies have no value function. We need to judge each policy by the states it is likely to reach, even though we can execute only one policy at a time. We also need to avoid switching so often that control becomes unstable. To address these challenges, we introduce World-Model Policy Arbiter (WMPA), a test-time framework that, given a set of frozen policies as input, rolls out each frozen policy in a learned state-space world model, evaluates the imagined futures with a shared goal-conditioned value function, and executes the highest-scoring policy for a short commitment interval before the next round of arbitration (policy selection). WMPA assumes access to a bank of frozen goal-conditioned policies and requires neither policy retraining nor privileged task-specific knowledge. Under the official OGBench evaluation protocol on 18 state-based datasets spanning maze navigation as well as cube, scene, and puzzle manipulation, WMPA improves the macro-average success rate from the 44% achieved by the best policy selected per dataset to 58%, with statistically significant gains on 12 datasets. These gains include +33 percentage points on cube-double-play and +36 percentage points on scene-play.

</details>


### [59] [Rethinking the Tradeoff Between Temporal Encoding and Nonlinear Computation in Spiking Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10933)
*Hanfei Liu,Shuchang Feng,Yanxia Chen,Changzeng Fu,Shiqi Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Spora，通过联合设计脉冲编码与注意力算子，在短时间窗内以二值时间权重实现最高 T 比特的组合值表示，突破传统脉冲计数读出仅 O(log2 T) 比特的容量瓶颈，在四个时间步下达到 76.6 平均 GLUE 分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲语言模型面临两难：既要通过短时间窗表征连续语义特征，又要保留代价高昂的非线性注意力运算。核心瓶颈在于传统 spike-count 读出的表示容量随步数仅对数增长。

Method: 提出 Spora，联合设计脉冲编码与注意力算子。用二值时间权重使 T 个脉冲可承载最多 T 比特组合容量。给出两种编码：UBS 通过阈值与脉冲触发的残差衰减产生非负整数码；BBS 分离符号与幅值并为有符号激活学习尺度。这些表示支撑累积-移位点积与注意力中的整数指数映射。

Result: 四个时间步下 Spora 取得 76.6 平均 GLUE 分数与 44.1 CoLA MCC，较 SpikeLM 分别提升 1.2 与 6.2 分。将 BBS 扩展至六步后，两项分数升至 78.2 与 47.4。通过条件衰减分析、同预算激活量化对比、事件负载统计与定点评估进一步刻画编码保真度与计算成本的关联。

Conclusion: 联合优化脉冲编码与注意力算子可有效提升脉冲语言模型在有限时间步下的表达容量与下游性能，且编码保真度与计算开销之间存在可分析的权衡关系。

Abstract: Spiking language models face a tradeoff between representing continuous semantic features over short temporal windows and retaining costly nonlinear attention operations. We introduce Spora, which jointly designs spike encodings and attention operators. Binary temporal weights let $T$ spikes represent compositional values with up to $T$ bits of capacity, compared with $O(\log_2 T)$ bits for spike-count readout. Unipolar Binary Spiking (UBS) uses thresholds and spike-triggered residual decay to produce non-negative integer codes; Bipolar Binary Spiking (BBS) separates sign and magnitude and learns a scale for signed activations. These representations support accumulation-and-shift dot products and integer-exponent mappings in attention. With four time steps, Spora achieves 76.6 average GLUE score and 44.1 CoLA MCC, improving over SpikeLM by 1.2 and 6.2 points, respectively. Extending BBS to six steps raises these scores to 78.2 and 47.4. Conditional-decay analysis, matched-budget activation-quantization comparisons, event-workload statistics, and fixed-point evaluation further characterize the connection between encoding fidelity and computational cost.

</details>


### [60] [RH-Detect: A Unified Benchmark for Reward Hacking Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10947)
*Junwei Quan,Evgenii Opryshko,Rohan Subramani,Igor Gilitschenski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RH-Detect统一基准，整合11个公开数据集的奖励黑客相关子集（92,761行、6类行为），在5,021个开放式评测单元上以零训练方式评估6个现成语言模型作为检测器，最佳模型池化AUROC达0.962、准确率超93%，但多轮工具使用场景存在明显性能落差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励黑客（reward hacking）指模型利用评估信号而未完成既定任务，威胁已部署语言模型系统的可靠性。现有数据集在标签、响应格式和元数据约定上各不相同，导致检测器结果难以横向比较，缺乏统一评测基准。

Method: 构建RH-Detect基准，将11个公开数据集中与奖励黑客相关的子集统一到同一schema，共92,761行、6类行为；在5,021个开放式评测单元（含任务提示与自由形式模型输出，并包含多轮工具使用轨迹）上，以零额外训练方式评估来自5个模型族的6个现成语言模型作为检测器；同时考察不同输入格式（如是否保留思考过程）的影响，并将该基准作为检测器训练数据集，采用单token SFT与GRPO留一法评估。

Result: 最佳模型池化AUROC为0.962，准确率超93%；但对四个最强模型，在两个多轮工具使用数据集MALT和TRACE上的准确率比其它来源低10.7–15.9个百分点（同一决策阈值下）。输入格式影响因模型而异：移除thinking使Qwen3.5-4B的AUROC从0.779升至0.849，却使Qwen Flash从0.977降至0.950。作为训练数据时，留一法单token SFT在6个留出来源中的5个上提升平均AUROC；针对失败案例的GRPO后续训练带来略微的性能提升。

Conclusion: 单一池化分数会掩盖数据来源、检测器输入与训练流程之间的差异；RH-Detect揭示了多轮工具使用场景的检测短板，为部署时监控提供统一可比基准。

Abstract: Reward hacking, where a model exploits an evaluation signal without completing the intended task, threatens the reliability of deployed language model systems. Existing datasets use different labels, response formats, and metadata conventions, making detector results difficult to compare. We present RH-Detect, a benchmark that combines reward-hacking-relevant subsets from eleven public datasets, comprising 92,761 rows and six behavior categories, into a common schema. On 5,021 open-ended evaluation units, each comprising a task prompt and a free-form model continuation, including multi-turn tool-use trajectories, we evaluate six off-the-shelf language models from five families as reward hacking detectors without additional training. The best model achieves a pooled AUROC of 0.962, with accuracy above 93%. For the four strongest models, however, accuracy on the two multi-turn tool-use datasets, MALT and TRACE, is 10.7-15.9 percentage points lower than on the other sources at a common decision threshold, highlighting a key gap for deployment-time monitoring. We find that different input formats have different effects across models. Removing thinking raises Qwen3.5-4B AUROC from 0.779 to 0.849, but lowers Qwen Flash from 0.977 to 0.950. We also evaluate the benchmark as a training dataset for detectors. Holding out each source in turn, single-token SFT improves average AUROC on five of six held-out sources. A GRPO follow-up on that failure case yields a slight improvement in detection performance. Our results show that a single pooled score can conceal variation across data sources, detector inputs, and training procedures.

</details>


### [61] [SPD-MetaFormer is what you need for small-data brain decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10952)
*Zhida Wang,Wei Lyu,Guo Yu,Sui Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文发现SPD流形注意力模型（MAtt、GBWAtt）学习到的注意力权重接近均匀，替换为均匀权重后性能几乎不变，据此提出无需注意力的SPD-MetaFormer架构，在三个EEG基准上取得有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑信号解码面临神经记录噪声大、个体差异大且标注数据有限的问题。近期基于对称正定（SPD）流形的注意力模型虽表现优异，但所学token加权机制的实际贡献尚不明确，作者希望厘清注意力权重是否真正起作用。

Method: 作者考察两种代表性架构：基于对数欧氏几何的MAtt和基于广义Bures–Wasserstein几何的GBWAtt。分析其注意力权重分布，并将其与有界相似度参数化及softmax缩放导致注意力对比度受限相联系。随后用均匀权重替换学习权重进行训练与评估。基于此提出SPD-MetaFormer：一种无注意力架构，采用对数欧氏几何下均匀加权的Fréchet聚合，骨干网络使用测地残差更新摘要token、共享谱前馈映射变换所有token，最后经学习加权读出，token状态在切空间分类前始终保持SPD值。

Result: MAtt与GBWAtt学到的注意力权重在训练后仍接近均匀；用均匀权重替换学习权重对平均预测性能影响很小，同时保留原聚合几何。在三个EEG基准上，SPD-MetaFormer相对已发表的欧氏与流形基线取得有竞争力的结果；匹配复现实验对比了MAtt与GBWAtt内部学习权重与均匀权重的效果。

Conclusion: 在短序列和有限数据场景下，精心设计的SPD架构（如无注意力的SPD-MetaFormer）可作为自适应流形注意力的更简单且有效的替代方案。

Abstract: Brain signal decoding is challenging because neural recordings are noisy and vary across individuals, while labeled data are often limited. Recent attention-based models on the symmetric positive definite (SPD) manifold have nevertheless achieved strong performance using covariance and connectivity representations, yet the contribution of learned token weighting remains unclear. We examine two representative architectures, MAtt (based on log-Euclidean geometry) and GBWAtt (based on generalized Bures--Wasserstein geometry), and find that their learned attention weights remain close to uniform after training. We relate this behavior to bounded similarity parameterizations that, under the original softmax scaling, limit attention-weight contrast. Moreover, replacing learned weights with uniform weights, throughout training and evaluation, has little effect on mean predictive performance while preserving each model's original aggregation geometry. Motivated by these findings, we introduce SPD-MetaFormer, an attention-free architecture built on uniformly weighted Fréchet aggregation under log-Euclidean geometry. Its backbone uses a geodesic residual to update a summary token and a shared spectral feedforward map to transform all tokens, followed by a learned weighted readout. Token states remain SPD-valued until tangent-space classification. Across three EEG benchmarks, SPD-MetaFormer achieves competitive results relative to published Euclidean and manifold baselines. Separate matched reproductions test learned versus uniform weighting within MAtt and GBWAtt. These results suggest that, in the short-sequence and limited-data regimes studied, carefully designed SPD architectures can provide a simpler and effective alternative to adaptive manifold attention.

</details>


### [62] [TRACE: A Governance Framework for Measuring Explainability Debt in Production AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10957)
*Harish Kant Pathak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出TRACE框架，用于量化、追踪和修复生产AI系统中的“可解释性债务”，以应对监管问责需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产AI系统的现有监控框架无法检测逐渐累积的治理责任，即在监管机构、审计方或受影响个体要求问责时，系统逐渐无法解释个体决策，这构成“可解释性债务”。

Method: 提出TRACE七工具治理框架，核心为可解释性债务分数（EDS），辅以DART、SHIV、FDE、HVE、AIDE、ZERO等工具；通过对一个日处理5万笔金融交易的生产欺诈检测系统进行12个月纵向案例研究，验证框架有效性。

Result: 审计日EDS为0.23，可通过DART轨迹分析提前六个月统计预测（beta=0.008/周，R²=0.94，95% CI: [0.006, 0.010]）；78%的可解释性债务集中在最高监管风险决策类别（超过1万美元的交易），而系统级指标无法显示这种风险不对称。

Conclusion: TRACE是首个面向生产AI部署中EU AI Act第13条合规的定量操作架构，确立了独立于解释生成的“解释治理”新子学科。

Abstract: Production AI systems deployed in high-stakes domains accumulate a governance liability that existing monitoring frameworks fail to detect: the progressive inability to explain individual decisions when regulators, auditors, or affected individuals demand accountability. We introduce TRACE (Transparency, Risk, Accountability, Compliance, and Explainability), a seven-instrument governance framework for measuring, tracking, and remediating Explainability Debt in production AI systems. The foundational instrument, the Explainability Debt Score (EDS), quantifies the proportion of production decisions falling below a governance-defined explainability confidence threshold at any point in time. Complementary instruments include DART (Debt Accumulation Rate Tracker for breach forecasting), SHIV (Scenario Health and Integrity Validator for daily governance), FDE (Feature Drift Evaluator for causal attribution), HVE (Human Validation Engine), AIDE (Audit Intervention Decision Engine), and ZERO (Zero Explainability Risk Optimiser for remediation). Through a twelve-month longitudinal case study of a production fraud detection system processing 50,000 daily financial transactions, achieving 98.46% accuracy and ROC-AUC of 0.9990, we demonstrate that an EDS of 0.23 on audit day was statistically predictable six months in advance using DART trajectory analysis (beta = 0.008/week, R-squared = 0.94, 95% CI: [0.006, 0.010]), and that 78% of Explainability Debt was concentrated in the highest-regulatory-risk decision category (transactions above $10,000), a risk asymmetry completely invisible to system-level metrics. TRACE provides the first quantitative operational architecture for EU AI Act Article 13 compliance in production AI deployment, establishing a new subdiscipline of explanation governance distinct from explanation generation.

</details>


### [63] [Spectrally Targeted Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10965)
*Vishrut Goyal,Rohan Ramkumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Spectrally Targeted Muon，通过阈值 τ 只对动量矩阵中大于或小于 τ 的奇异值做正交化，在 Muon（全正交化）与 normalized SGD 之间连续插值。实验揭示 Muon 的增益主要来自放大原本过小、无法有效训练的方向，而非压制最大奇异方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon 对更新矩阵做完全正交化（所有奇异值置一），在训练大语言模型上表现优异，但其成功机制尚不明确：究竟是放大了梯度下降所忽略的小奇异方向，还是压制了破坏训练的大退化方向，二者未被解耦验证。

Method: 提出 Spectrally Targeted Muon，仅对高于或低于阈值 τ 的奇异值做正交化，使 τ 变化时在 normalized SGD 与 Muon 间插值。借助在移位 Gram 矩阵上运行 Newton–Schulz 迭代计算投影，分离出相关奇异子空间，从而避免显式 SVD。在 CIFAR-10 与 NanoGPT speedrun 上评测，跟踪梯度、更新与权重矩阵的有效秩，以及新指标——更新与权重矩阵等谱流形切空间的 alignment。

Result: 三点发现：(1) 动量的小奇异值并非噪声，仅正交化除最大的少数几个奇异值之外的全部方向即可接近 Muon，而只正交化最大方向则明显不足；(2) 在语言模型上动量奇异值均远小于一，针对性正交化只能起到放大作用，Muon 的收益来自把原始尺度下过小、无法训练的方向变为可训练；(3) 缩小最大奇异值是保持参数谱平坦的原因，这一操作代价低廉但并非驱动 loss 下降的主因；AdamW 与 Muon 的主要差异在于其构建结构的速度较慢，这解释了 AdamW 起步更慢、warmup 对 AdamW 有益却对 Muon 有害的现象。

Conclusion: Muon 的有效性源于对动量的放大而非修剪，其核心作用是把小奇异方向提升到可训练的尺度；谱平坦性由压缩最大奇异值实现，与 loss 下降机制相互独立；不同优化器的本质差异可归结为构建结构的速度差异。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer orthogonalizes each update matrix, setting all of its singular values to one, and has proven highly effective for training large language models. It remains unclear, however, whether this success comes from amplifying small singular directions that gradient descent neglects or from suppressing large, degenerate directions that disrupt training. We introduce Spectrally Targeted Muon, which orthogonalizes only the singular values above or below a threshold $τ$, so that varying $τ$ interpolates between normalized SGD and Muon. It isolates the relevant singular subspaces with projections computed by Newton-Schulz iteration on a shifted Gram matrix, so no SVD is needed. We evaluate these variants on the CIFAR-10 and NanoGPT speedruns, tracking the effective rank of gradient, update, and weight matrices and a new metric, the alignment of updates with the tangent space of the weight matrix's isospectral manifold. We find three things. First, the small singular values of the momentum are not noise. Orthogonalizing everything except the few largest singular values of each matrix nearly matches Muon while touching only a small fraction of the momentum, whereas orthogonalizing only the top falls well short even though it holds almost all of it. On language models every momentum singular value is far below one, so targeted orthogonalization can only amplify, and Muon wins by making directions that are too small to train on at their raw scale trainable. Second, shrinking the largest singular values is what keeps the parameter spectrum flat; this is cheap, and it is not what drives the loss. Third, AdamW differs from Muon mainly in how slowly it builds structure, which explains its slower start and why warmup helps AdamW but only hurts Muon.

</details>


### [64] [ASPIRE: Saddle-Point Discovery through Set Prediction and Physical Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10969)
*Yucheng Zhao,Quanyou Zhang,Shaoxiang Qin,Haixuan Xu,Xiongye Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ASPIRE框架通过等变集合预测器Ev-Quiformer从单个初始原子环境预测多个鞍点候选，再用Dimer搜索精修，提升缺陷扩散事件发现效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件驱动模型预测热激活扩散与缺陷演化需识别原子重排机制及其激活势垒，但鞍点搜索是主要计算瓶颈：一个亚稳态可产生多种重排、局部搜索昂贵且易失败或重复收敛到同一鞍点。

Method: 提出ASPIRE框架：从单个初始原子环境预测一组鞍点候选，并在原始势函数上通过Dimer搜索精修。其等变集合预测器Ev-Quiformer包含（i）几何条件化的标量-向量事件槽以生成多个鞍点提议；（ii）解码器将每个槽映射为完整原子位移场，结合原子、槽与锚点相对向量及不变系数。

Result: 在BCCFE4VACAV-4000基准上参考事件覆盖率达77.20%，优于传统Dimer基线的75.73%，且Dimer力评估次数约减半；50个构型计时中，单构型墙钟时间由478.8 s降至176.3 s。

Conclusion: ASPIRE通过集合式鞍点预测与精修，在保持更高事件覆盖的同时显著降低计算成本，并提供了两个BCC铁空位数据集及提议等变性的理论条件。

Abstract: Predicting thermally activated diffusion and defect evolution with event-driven models requires identifying atomic rearrangement mechanisms and their activation barriers. Discovering the associated saddle points is a major computational bottleneck: multiple rearrangements may originate from one metastable state, while costly local searches can fail or repeatedly converge to the same saddle. To address this challenge, we introduce ASPIRE (Atomistic Saddle-Point Inference with Refinement for Events), a framework that predicts a set of saddle candidates from a single initial atomic environment and refines them through Dimer searches on the original interatomic potential. The framework's equivariant set predictor, Ev-Quiformer, integrates (i) geometry-conditioned scalar-vector event slots for generating multiple saddle-point proposals and (ii) a decoder that maps each slot to a full atomic displacement field by combining atom, slot, and anchor-relative vectors with invariant coefficients. We also contribute two datasets: (i) BCCFE4VACAV-4000, comprising 4,000 four-vacancy body-centered cubic iron configurations and 65,450 reference events grouped by initial state for set supervision and post-refinement evaluation; and (ii) BCCFE-1TO4VAC, comprising 5,372 configurations with one to four vacancies each. Theoretically, we establish conditions for proposal equivariance. Experimentally, ASPIRE achieves 77.20% reference-event coverage on this benchmark, compared with 75.73% for a conventional Dimer baseline, while requiring approximately half as many Dimer force evaluations. In a timing evaluation on 50 configurations, ASPIRE reduces wall time per configuration from 478.8 s to 176.3 s under the stated hardware settings.

</details>


### [65] [Transferability of Learned States in Neural PDE Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10972)
*Shunye Wang,Haochen Wen,Shuo Li Liu,Xuanyi Wang,Lihao Liu,Zhongying Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对神经PDE求解器中的“有效复用”评估难题，提出了一套复用契约框架，通过配对状态比较、匹配目标信息与预算、成本核算，将解的精度、学习贡献与数值效用分离，并用文献审计、线性系统实验与多任务轨迹验证了该框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在神经PDE求解器中，最终精度往往同时反映源任务学习和目标时刻的计算量，难以判断复用是否真正有效。因此需要一个能够区分解精度、学习贡献和数值效用的评估框架，以避免把目标阶段的额外计算误判为复用收益。

Method: 作者提出“复用契约”：通过配对状态比较、匹配目标信息与预算、成本核算来分离三类因素。对12篇论文进行文献审计，提取18条版本特定协议记录，记录保留状态、目标时刻资源和已报告控制项；针对固定线性系统与残差容差，构造两个具有相同解误差、能量误差和残差范数的初始猜测，但用不同共轭梯度（CG）迭代次数达到同一解；并在240条源训练轨迹、两类线性PDE族、傅里叶神经算子和卷积网络上测试固定预测器的收益是否随校正算法反转。

Result: 在64个分布内任务（63×63内部网格）上，相对预测误差均不超过5%的配对中，三种范数的降低都伴随更多CG迭代，在两个库中任务级平均速率分别为23.5%和23.9%。基于工作量选择在留出分布内任务上节省2.50–3.33次CG迭代；匹配自适应表明有限预算预训练具有价值。独立批次确认，一个物理训练的傅里叶神经算子相对于零初始化Poisson预条件CG，可带来0.73%的完整在线节省。

Conclusion: 复用评估必须依赖匹配的状态比较和下游计算证据，而不能仅看最终精度。该复用契约能够分离学习贡献与目标时刻计算，揭示固定预测器收益在不同校正算法下可能反转，并为判断神经PDE求解器中的有效复用提供可操作标准。

Abstract: Assessing useful reuse in neural PDE solvers is challenging: final accuracy can reflect source learning and target-time computation. Our reuse contract separates solution accuracy, learning contribution, and numerical utility through paired state comparisons, matched target information and budgets, and cost accounting. A literature audit extracts 18 version-specific protocol records from 12 papers, documenting retained states, target-time resources, and reported controls. For a fixed linear system and residual tolerance, we construct two initial guesses with identical solution-error, energy-error, and residual norms, reaching the same solution with different conjugate-gradient (CG) iteration counts. Across 240 source-training trajectories, two linear PDE families, Fourier neural operators and convolutional networks, a fixed predictor's benefit reverses across correction algorithms. Among pairs with both relative prediction errors less than or equal to 5 percent on 64 in-distribution tasks (63 by 63 interior grids), reductions in all three norms accompany more CG iterations, at mean taskwise rates of 23.5 percent and 23.9 percent in two libraries. Work-based selection saves 2.50-3.33 CG iterations on held-out in-distribution tasks; matched adaptation demonstrates finite-budget pretraining value. Independent batches confirm a 0.73 percent complete online saving for one physics-trained Fourier neural operator against zero-initialized Poisson-preconditioned CG. Reuse requires matched state comparisons and downstream computational evidence.

</details>


### [66] [Budgeted Multi-Source Counterfactual Annotation for Off-Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10974)
*Biao Xiang,Ali Eshragh,Yuexing Li,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究预算约束下为离策略评估（OPE）获取反事实标注的分配问题，提出整数规划建模与主要化-最小化算法，实验显示MSE分别降低20.58%和10.77%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离策略评估中行为策略覆盖不足会导致高方差重加权或奖励模型外推；反事实标注可补充未观测动作的证据，但专家与LLM等标注源成本高、有偏且含噪，因此需在预算内合理分配标注资源。

Method: 将问题建模为上下文-动作对与标注源上的整数分配问题，在给定源特定成本与误差分布下最小化依赖标注方案的估计器方差分量；通过首次标注阈值与局部标注价值区间刻画标注的有用性；针对多源耦合问题，提出结合动态规划子程序的主要化-最小化（MM）算法，保证目标函数单调改进。

Result: 在合成临床数据和LLM标注的教育数据bandit实验中，相对无标注基线，所提分配方法在固定误差分布下将均方误差（MSE）分别降低20.58%与10.77%。

Conclusion: 预算化反事实标注分配可有效降低上下文bandit离策略评估的估计方差，所提框架为多源、异质误差与成本下的标注获取提供了可优化且单调收敛的求解方案。

Abstract: Off-policy evaluation (OPE) estimates the value of a target policy from logged data, but limited behavior-policy coverage can force high-variance reweighting or reward-model extrapolation. Counterfactual annotations can add evidence about unobserved actions, yet practical sources, including domain experts and large language models (LLMs), may be costly, biased, or noisy. We study budgeted acquisition of such annotations for contextual-bandit OPE. Given source-specific costs and error profiles, we formulate an integer allocation problem over context-action pairs and annotation sources to minimize the component of estimator variance that depends on the annotation plan. We characterize when annotations are valuable through a first-annotation threshold and local annotation-value regimes. For the coupled multi-source problem, we develop a majorization-minimization algorithm with dynamic-programming subroutines that monotonically improves the objective. Experiments in synthetic clinical and LLM-annotated education bandits show that our allocation method reduces fixed-profile mean squared error (MSE) by 20.58% and 10.77%, respectively, relative to no annotation.

</details>


### [67] [Optimizing Large Language Models with Chained LMOs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10975)
*Sungyoon Kim,Kaan Ozkara,Youngsuk Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出链式线性最小化预言机（chained LMOs）统一框架，解释矩阵归一化优化器为何有效，并据此提出 TensorChain 优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon 及其衍生优化器通过组合多种矩阵归一化取得经验成功，但这些方法碎片化、缺乏统一视角；且许多组合策略超出标准 LMO 框架，理论上在光滑凸目标上可能发散，因此需要统一理论解释其有效性来源。

Method: 将此类优化器形式化为 LMO 的复合（chained LMOs）；借助线性联想记忆（linear associative memory）分析，证明在各向异性嵌入下链式组合可优于 Muon。基于该框架提出 TensorChain：将不同层的兼容权重矩阵堆叠为三维张量，并沿各轴进行归一化。

Result: 在 Qwen3 0.6B 与 1.7B 预训练中，TensorChain 在平均 token 效率上优于所有链式基线，在匹配验证损失时相比 Muon 平均节省 9.6% token。

Conclusion: 链式 LMO 框架为矩阵归一化优化器提供了统一解释，并指导设计出更高效的 TensorChain 优化器。

Abstract: Muon has motivated a growing family of optimizers that compose multiple matrix normalizations, but these methods remain fragmented and lack a unified perspective. We introduce chained linear minimization oracles (chained LMOs), which cast these methods as compositions of LMOs. Despite their empirical success, many chains fall outside the standard LMO framework and can diverge on smooth convex objectives. To explain why composition can nevertheless help, we turn to linear associative memory and show that chaining can improve over Muon under anisotropic embeddings. Empirically, we propose TensorChain, a novel optimizer within the framework that stacks compatible weight matrices across different layers and normalizes the 3d tensor across its axes. In Qwen3 0.6B and 1.7B pretraining, TensorChain outperforms all chained baselines in average token efficiency, with average token savings of 9.6% over Muon at matched validation loss.

</details>


### [68] [Multi-Bandwidth Distribution Matching Distillation: On the Equivalence of Distribution Matching Distillation and Drifting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10989)
*Jialin Zhu,Xing Liu,Feixiang He,He Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了Drifting Model与分布匹配蒸馏(DMD/DMD2)之间的精确等价关系：将预训练DFSGMs的速度场/噪声场转化为Drifting Model中的吸引力场，并从生成分布估计排斥力场，即可证明训练Drifting Model等价于DMD。基于此等价视角，提出改进方法MBDMD（多带宽分布匹配蒸馏）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一步生成模型是当前研究热点，Drifting Models展现出巨大潜力，且已有工作揭示其与扩散/流式生成模型(DFSGMs)的联系。尽管Drifting Model与广泛使用的DMD/DMD2优化目标公式几乎相同，但目前尚无工作建立二者的精确对应关系，这一理论空白亟待填补。

Method: 通过理论推导，将预训练DFSGMs的速度场/噪声场转化为Drifting Models中的吸引力场(attraction force field)，同时从生成分布中估计排斥力场(repulsion force field)，从而证明训练Drifting Model与分布匹配蒸馏在数学上自然等价。基于该等价概念，从Drifting Model的视角出发，提出改进方法——多带宽分布匹配蒸馏(MBDMD)。

Result: 证明了Drifting Model与DMD/DMD2之间的精确等价关系，即二者优化目标在特定场转换下完全对应；并据此提出MBDMD方法以改进原有DMD。

Conclusion: 该工作首次建立了Drifting Model与DMD/DMD2的精确理论对应，统一了两类方法的理论框架，并从Drifting Model视角为分布匹配蒸馏的改进提供了新思路，所提出的MBDMD方法展现了该等价视角的实用价值。

Abstract: Researchers are exploring effective one-step generative model continuously, and, Drifting Models (Deng et al., 2026), demonstrate great potential in one-step generation recently. There are works that reveal the connection between Diffusion & Flow Style Generative Models (DFSGMs) (Ho et al., 2020; Song et al., 2020a;b; Lipman et al., 2022; Liu et al., 2022) and Drifting Models (Li & Zhu, 2026; Lai et al., 2026; Turan et al., 2026). But no one has yet established a precise correspondence between the Drifting Model and the widely used distillation method- Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD/DMD2) (Yin et al., 2024b;a) to the best of our knowledge, even though their optimization objective formulas are virtually identical. In this paper, we prove that by converting the velocity-field / noise-field from the pre-trained DFSGMs into the attraction force field in Drifting Models and estimating the repulsion force field from the generative distribution, training the Drifting Model is naturally equivalent to the Distribution Matching Distillation. With this equivalent concept, we propose an improved method based on DMD from the Drifting Model's perspective- Multi-Bandwidth Distribution Matching Distillation (MBDMD).

</details>


### [69] [Invariant-Measure Reasoners: Stable Representations for Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10996)
*Yuto Inui,Takuya Konishi,Yoshinobu Kawahara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出不变测度推理器（ImR），用循环块更新的潜状态长期分布（不变测度）下的预测头期望作为稳定表征，缓解潜推理模型随循环深度增加导致的预测不稳定，在迷宫和数独任务上提升准确率并降低预测波动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜推理模型反复用同一循环块更新潜状态。随循环深度增加，潜状态序列可能收敛到状态空间的一个紧致子集，但不一定收敛到不动点。现有模型通常只对单个潜状态施加预测头进行预测，而潜状态在多次更新后仍可能继续变化，导致跨循环深度的预测不稳定。因此需要一种更稳定的表征方式来消除这种深度依赖的预测波动。

Method: 提出不变测度推理器（ImR）框架，以不变测度作为稳定表征。该测度刻画潜状态在紧致子集上的长期分布，并且在循环块更新下保持不变。ImR 从预测头输出在该测度下的期望进行预测。ImR 有两种使用方式：其一，仅微调现有模型的预测头；其二，从零开始训练模型。两种方式均旨在降低预测不稳定并提升准确率。

Result: 在迷宫和数独任务的大量设置中，两种使用方式均降低了预测不稳定并提升了准确率。在某些设置下，用 ImR 训练的模型相较现有模型更频繁地表现出非不动点行为，但即便存在这种行为仍能达到高准确率，而现有模型则不能。

Conclusion: 结果表明 ImR 能够利用原本会造成不稳定的动力学来服务于潜推理，说明不变测度可作为一种有效且稳定的潜推理表征，并为利用非收敛/非不动点动态提供了新思路。

Abstract: Latent reasoning models repeatedly update a latent state using the same recurrent block. As the recurrent depth increases, the sequence of latent states may converge to a compact subset of the state space without necessarily converging to a fixed point. Existing models typically predict by applying a prediction head to a single latent state. However, the latent state can continue to change even after many updates, potentially making predictions unstable across recurrent depths. To address this instability, we introduce invariant-measure reasoners (ImR), a framework that uses an invariant measure as a stable representation. This measure describes the long-run distribution of latent states on the compact subset and is invariant under updates by the recurrent block. ImR predicts from the expectation of the prediction head's output under this measure. We use ImR in two ways: fine-tuning only the prediction head of existing models and training models from scratch. Both approaches reduce prediction instability and improve accuracy in many settings on maze and Sudoku tasks. In some settings, models trained with ImR exhibit non-fixed-point behavior more frequently than existing models yet achieve high accuracy even with such behavior, unlike existing models. These results suggest that ImR can leverage otherwise destabilizing dynamics for latent reasoning.

</details>


### [70] [F$^3$NO: Frequency-Decomposed Finite-Time Flow-map Neural Operators with Cross-Scale Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10998)
*Fan Wu,Cheng Jing,Kookjin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出频率分解有限时间流映射神经算子 F³NO，用低频特征引导高频细节的非线性精细化，直接预测指定未来时刻状态，结合短时间段内并行预测与段间递归传播提升长时预报精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子可实现快速 PDE 预报，但自回归式重复预测会累积误差，且细尺度结构难以解析。

Method: F³NO 进行频率分解，每层内通过跨尺度条件连接全局谱处理与局部细节精化；模型直接预测指定未来时刻状态，并按预测时间间隔调整两分支贡献；对长轨迹采用短时间段内并行预测加段间递归传播。

Result: 在五个 PDE 基准上，预报精度优于自回归与直接预测基线；消融实验表明频率分解精化能以更少参数提升精度，而分段策略的收益取决于空间分辨率与动力学状态。

Conclusion: 频率分解与跨尺度条件可有效缓解误差累积与细尺度解析问题，直接多时刻预测配合分段递归传播为长时 PDE 预报提供有效方案。

Abstract: Neural operators enable fast PDE forecasting, but repeated predictions accumulate errors and fine-scale structures remain difficult to resolve.
