We introduce a frequency-decomposed finite-time flow-map neural operator (F$^3$NO) that leverages updated low-frequency features to guide nonlinear refinement of high-frequency information. Within each layer, this cross-scale conditioning connects global spectral processing with local detail refinement. The model directly predicts states at specified future times and adjusts the contributions of the two branches according to the prediction interval. For longer trajectories, it combines parallel predictions within short temporal segments with recursive propagation between segments. Experiments on five PDE benchmarks demonstrate improved forecasting accuracy over autoregressive and direct-prediction baselines. Ablations show that frequency-decomposed refinement can improve accuracy with fewer parameters, while the benefits of segmentation depend on spatial resolution and dynamical regime.

</details>


### [71] [Low-rank tensor structure of precipitation and its application to satellite-reference merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11000)
*Ryan Solgi,Rohan Shankar,Hugo A. Loaiciga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将美国本土日降水表示为时空张量，采用CP分解证明保留原生时空模态的重建更准确，并据此提出TMerge框架融合卫星降水与稀疏参考观测，显著提升IMERG产品精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降水具有间歇性和变异性，在广域范围上难以准确估计；但其时空结构暗示可能存在低秩表示，为利用有限参考观测改进卫星降水估计提供了潜在途径。

Method: 将CONUS日降水构建为时空张量，应用CANDECOMP/PARAFAC（CP）分解，与逐日独立场分解及展开的时空矩阵分解对比；进而提出TMerge，通过共享的低秩空间与时间因子融合卫星降水与稀疏参考观测，用于校正IMERG Final Run产品，并与线性偏差校正、分位数映射和神经网络对比。

Result: 2019—2022年间，TMerge将相关系数从0.53提升至0.85，RMSE和MAE分别降低48.2%和29.3%；在各季节、降水强度区间和区域上均优于线性偏差校正、分位数映射和神经网络，改进具有空间连贯性，且在IMERG误差最大的沿海地区提升最显著。

Conclusion: 低秩张量结构能以简约方式近似降水的主导时空变异性，为在广域、参考观测有限的条件下改进卫星降水估计提供了实用机制。

Abstract: The intermittent and variable nature of precipitation makes its accurate estimation over extended domains difficult, yet its spatiotemporal structure suggests that a low-rank representation may be possible. This work represents daily precipitation over the contiguous United States (CONUS) as spatiotemporal tensors and applies CANDECOMP/PARAFAC factorization, showing that preserving the native spatial and temporal modes yields more accurate reconstruction than factorizing independent daily fields or unfolded space--time matrices. Building on this finding, this work presents TMerge, a tensor-based framework that integrates satellite precipitation with sparse reference observations through shared low-rank spatial and temporal factors. TMerge was applied to correct the IMERG Final Run product with climate prediction center reference observations over CONUS. During 2019-2022, TMerge increased correlation from 0.53 to 0.85 and reduced root-mean-square error and mean absolute error by 48.2% and 29.3%, respectively. TMerge consistently outperformed linear bias correction, quantile mapping, and neural networks across seasons, precipitation-intensity regimes, and regions. Improvements were spatially coherent and largest in coastal regions where IMERG errors were greatest. These results demonstrate that low-rank tensor structure parsimoniously approximates the dominant spatiotemporal variability of precipitation and provides a practical mechanism for improving satellite estimates under limited reference observations over extended domains.

</details>


### [72] [Emergent Inverse-Depth Scaling From Nonlinearity In Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11063)
*Zirui Peng,Yizhou Liu,Ziming Liu,Jeff Gore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究注意力机制非线性如何影响大语言模型的深度缩放规律，发现非线性注意力产生损失随深度呈反比衰减，而非线性模型中的幂律缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论用模型深度的幂律缩放来解释参数量缩放规律，但这一理论基于线性注意力模型，假设数据谱为幂律分布、模型按全局谱强度顺序学习。然而大语言模型具有强非线性，其缩放机制尚不清楚。

Method: 作者测试了不同数据谱下非线性注意力对损失随深度变化的影响，分析非线性注意力如何选择性聚焦相关token，并借助中心极限定理建立跨层误差共享与层间差异聚合的理论联系。

Result: 在所有测试的数据谱下，非线性注意力均产生损失随深度的反比衰减，而非幂律衰减。非线性使注意力能选择性聚焦相关token，强、弱谱方向并行学习；跨层共享误差决定损失平台，层间差异的聚合带来随深度持续的增益。

Conclusion: 深度缩放可能源于注意力中的非线性，这使大语言模型能够局部聚焦，并可能使全局协方差结构变得不那么重要。

Abstract: Scaling laws describe power-law improvements in model performance with dataset size and parameter count, yet their underlying mechanisms are not fully understood. To explain the parameter count scaling, existing theory posits power-law scaling with model depth. In linear-attention models, this scaling is tied to a power-law data spectrum: unable to selectively attend to relevant tokens, these models learn according to global spectral strength, with stronger directions learned before weaker ones. Large language models, however, can be strongly nonlinear. Here, we show that nonlinear attention yields inverse-depth decay of loss across all tested data spectra. Nonlinearity enables attention to focus selectively on relevant tokens, allowing strong and weak spectral directions to be learned in parallel. Similar focusing across layers motivates a connection to the central limit theorem: shared error across layers sets the loss plateau, while aggregation turns layer-specific differences into continued gains with depth. Our findings suggest that depth scaling may arise from nonlinearity in attention, which allows large language models to focus locally and may make the global covariance structure less relevant.

</details>


### [73] [Measuring and Mitigating Solution Mode Collapse in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11064)
*Liv G. d'Aliberti,Marwa Abdulhai,Sofiia Druchyna,Peter Henderson,Manoel Horta Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ModeBench 基准与 Re:Max 方法，揭示 RLVR 后训练会使模型正确解法的多样性（mode）收敛，并通过按模式均匀回放来同时提升成功率与解法多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 的奖励只关注答案是否正确，对模型产生的是哪种正确解法完全不敏感——无论是第一千次重复的熟悉解法，还是从未产生过的新解法，获得的奖励相同。但保留多种正确解法对模型有潜在价值：可给用户提供选择，也可能提供提升整体性能的解题策略。因此需要先量化 RLVR 对解法多样性的影响，再加以改善。

Method: 1) 构建 ModeBench：一个多解任务基准，其验证器同时返回正确性与所发现的 mode（解法模式）；2) 用 ModeBench 测量 RLVR 后训练过程中解法多样性的变化；3) 提出 Re:Max：将每个已发现 mode 的一个已验证样例存入 replay buffer，并对存储的 mode 均匀采样训练，使"只出现过一次的解"与"反复出现的解"获得同等练习机会。

Result: ModeBench 测量显示：RLVR 后训练在准确率保持或提升的同时，将概率质量集中到更少的正确 mode 上；且前沿模型本身已高度集中。Re:Max 在三种模型规模、两种 RL 目标以及更困难的任务构造下，均同时提升了策略成功频率与成功解法的多样性。

Conclusion: RLVR 存在"多样性坍缩"问题，奖励对解法模式不敏感导致概率集中；通过按发现模式均匀回放（Re:Max）可在不牺牲准确率的前提下恢复并提升解法多样性，说明训练目标的样本组织方式而非仅奖励信号本身决定多样性保留程度。

Abstract: A language model (LM) can usually answer the same question in more than one way, but reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is indifferent to which correct answer a model produces. A solution will earn the same reward whether it is the thousandth copy of a familiar answer or one the model has never produced before. Yet, there is potential value in having the model retain multiple correct solutions as it is trained. For instance, multiple modes may give users a choice and provide problem-solving strategies that improve overall model performance. Here, we introduce ModeBench, a benchmark of multi-solution tasks in which the verifier returns both correctness and mode discovered. We then use ModeBench to measure how solution diversity changes under RLVR post-training. We find that RLVR post-training concentrates probability onto fewer correct modes even as accuracy holds or improves, and moreover, that frontier models are already highly concentrated. We then introduce our solution, Re:Max, which stores one verified example per discovered mode in a replay buffer and trains on those stored modes uniformly. A solution found once is, therefore, practiced as often as one found repeatedly. Across three model scales, two RL objectives, and harder task constructions, replay improves both how often a policy succeeds and how many different ways it can succeed.

</details>


### [74] [CityDeploy-Bench: Benchmarking Physics-Grounded Spatial Set Planning for Multi-Transmitter Network Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11065)
*Chenyang Yuan,Xiaoyuan Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CityDeploy-Bench基准，将多发射机部署重构为统一射线追踪验证器下的物理基础空间集合规划问题，揭示物理耦合增强时规划行为从独立决策向集合协调的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市级无线部署自动化在复杂城市传播与全网干扰下仍具挑战性，现有方法难以刻画多发射机间的物理耦合与协同关系，缺乏可复现的受控对比测试环境。

Method: 构建CityDeploy-Bench基准，将多发射机部署形式化为物理基础的空间集合规划，采用统一射线追踪验证器；将效用表示与规划动态解耦，在多样规划器间对直接标量奖励、关系模型与高阶交互结构进行受控比较。

Result: 随物理耦合增强，规划行为出现明显转变：部署质量越来越取决于所学效用能否捕获集体发射机交互，而单纯增强搜索无法弥补关系结构的缺失。

Conclusion: 多发射机部署本质上是物理交互集合上的协调问题，而非一组独立空间决策的叠加。发布CityDeploy-Data与基准框架作为可复现测试平台，连接决策学习与物理基础的无线网络设计研究。

Abstract: Automating city-scale wireless deployment remains challenging under complex urban propagation and network-wide interference. We introduce \textbf{CityDeploy-Bench}, a benchmark that reframes multi-transmitter deployment as \emph{physics-grounded spatial set planning} under a unified ray-tracing verifier. The benchmark separates utility representation from planning dynamics, enabling controlled comparison between direct scalar rewards, relational models, and higher-order interaction structures across diverse planners. Our experiments reveal a clear transition in planning behavior as physical coupling grows. Deployment quality becomes increasingly dependent on whether the learned utility captures collective transmitter interactions, whereas stronger search alone cannot compensate for missing relational structure. This establishes multi-transmitter deployment as a coordination problem over physically interacting sets rather than a collection of independent spatial decisions. We release CityDeploy-Data and the benchmark framework as a reproducible testbed for research linking decision learning with physically grounded wireless network design.

</details>


### [75] [Optimally Pacing Budget Spending and Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11074)
*Mark Braverman,Jingyi Liu,Jieming Mao,Jon Schneider,Eric Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对对抗性设置下带预算约束的在线学习问题，给出了接近最优的遗憾界：在全信息下可与任意预算配速专家类比较，并在在线资源分配中获得首个 $o(\sqrt{T})$ 的保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带预算约束的在线学习需在有限预算下竞争任意专家类，已有下界（Braverman et al., 2025）表明遗憾不可避免，但缺乏与之匹配的上界算法；同时在线资源分配任务希望突破 $\sqrt{T}$ 级遗憾。

Method: 提出全信息算法，可与任意 $F$ 个专家的类别及候选预算配速计划竞争，针对累计支出与计划距离在 $D$ 以内的专家取得 $O(D\sqrt{\log F}+\sqrt{T\log F})$ 遗憾；并将该技术推广到在线资源分配，在允许分数分配时给出 $O(D\sqrt{\log F})$ 遗憾算法。

Result: 所获遗憾界与 Braverman et al. (2025) 的下界匹配，达到近最优；在允许分数分配的在线资源分配中实现 $O(D\sqrt{\log F})$ 遗憾，据作者所知是首个达到 $o(\sqrt{T})$ 保证的算法。

Conclusion: 该工作为带预算约束的对抗性在线学习提供了近最优遗憾算法，并将方法成功拓展至在线资源分配，首次实现亚 $\sqrt{T}$ 遗憾。

Abstract: We establish near-optimal regret bounds for budget-constrained online learning against arbitrary classes of budget-pacing experts in the adversarial setting. In particular, given any class of $F$ experts and a candidate budget pacing schedule, we provide a full-information algorithm which obtains regret $O(D \sqrt{\log F}+ \sqrt{T\log F})$ against all experts whose cumulative spending stays within distance $D$ of this schedule, matching lower bounds established by Braverman et al. (2025).
  We additionally show that our technique extends to various problems in online resource allocation, where the learner gets to see the rewards and costs of the current options available to them, and establish $O(D\sqrt{\log F})$ regret bounds when fractional allocation is allowed. This is the first algorithm we are aware of which can achieve $o(\sqrt{T})$ guarantees for such tasks.

</details>


### [76] [Stability-Plasticity Balance via Singular-Vector Selection in LLM Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11076)
*Lingxiang Wang,Hainan Zhang,Liang Pang,Hongwei Zheng,Zhiming Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SVC（奇异向量通道）方法，将LLM领域持续适配中的可塑性-稳定性权衡建模为对奇异向量通道的选择性更新，通过估计各通道的适配收益与遗忘代价，自适应筛选可训练通道，在保留预训练能力的同时提升下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在领域特定持续适配中面临灾难性遗忘，需在获取新能力与保留预训练能力之间取得平衡。现有PEFT方法虽通过限制可训练参数量缓解该问题，但缺乏一个有原则的"单元"来决定可塑性应分配到何处、稳定性应在何处保留。

Method: 作者将奇异向量通道识别为管理该权衡的自然单元：每个通道对应一个输入-输出变换，可被更新以获取新知识，或被固定以保留预训练能力。基于此提出SVC——一种选择性更新奇异向量通道的参数高效持续学习方法。微调前，SVC使用领域特定数据估计每个通道的适配收益，并仅将固定的公共通用领域语料作为历史激活代理来估计遗忘代价；随后通过基于拐点的代价筛选、Pareto前沿过滤和Otsu阈值化自适应选择可训练通道。

Result: 在四个LLM家族和八个下游任务上的实验表明，相较于现有PEFT基线，SVC在取得强劲下游性能的同时更好地保留了预训练能力。对通道打分与选择的进一步分析验证了在奇异向量通道层面进行选择性可塑性的有效性。

Conclusion: 以奇异向量通道为单元的选择性可塑性可有效实现LLM的持续适配，为PEFT中可塑性与稳定性的分配提供了有原则的决策单元。

Abstract: Domain-specific continual adaptation of LLMs risks catastrophic forgetting, creating a fundamental tension between acquiring new capabilities and preserving those learned during pretraining. PEFT mitigates this problem by restricting the number of trainable parameters, but existing methods lack a principled unit for deciding where plasticity should be allocated and stability should be preserved. We identify the singular-vector channel as a natural unit for managing this trade-off. Each channel represents an input-output transformation, which can be updated to acquire new knowledge or fixed to preserve pretrained capabilities. Based on this perspective, we introduce SVC, a parameter-efficient continual-learning method that selectively updates Singular-Vector Channels. Before fine-tuning, SVC uses domain-specific data to estimate each channel's adaptation benefit and a fixed public general-domain corpus only as a history activation proxy for estimating forgetting cost. It then adaptively selects trainable channels based on these scores via knee-based cost screening, Pareto-front filtering, and Otsu thresholding. Experimental results across four LLM families and eight downstream tasks show that SVC better preserves pretrained capabilities while achieving strong downstream performance relative to existing PEFT baselines. Further analysis of channel scoring and selection demonstrates that selective plasticity at the singular-vector-channel level enables effective continual LLM adaptation.

</details>


### [77] [DaCe-DT: Data-Centric Offline Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning via Adaptive Prompts and Trajectory Correction for Heterogeneous Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11085)
*Xinfei Wang,Shanchen Pang,Chenhao Zhang,Shudong Wang,Wenhao Ji,Haiyuan Gui,Meng Han,Xiaojian Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 DaCe-DT 离线多任务强化学习框架，从数据视角解决三个瓶颈，在 Meta-World 上平均提升 11.73%（最优数据）与 13.34%（次优数据）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线多任务强化学习（Offline MTRL）研究主要聚焦算法层面的优化，较少从数据层面提升学习能力与泛化性能。作者从数据角度指出三个瓶颈：(i) 不同任务复杂度下提示长度未被有效利用；(ii) 随机采样的提示片段存在语义不相关性；(iii) 碎片化、不连续轨迹带来误导性监督信号。

Method: 提出 DaCe-DT 框架，对异构任务复杂度与数据质量不敏感，包含三个机制：长度门控提示掩码（LGPM）、检索增强提示构建（RAPC）、值自适应回报校准（VARC），协同实现以数据为中心的提示自适应与轨迹精炼。

Result: 在 Meta-World 基准上持续优于现有最优方法：最优数据集平均提升 11.73%，次优数据集平均提升 13.34%。

Conclusion: DaCe-DT 能在异构离线数据与任务下实现稳健的多任务泛化与稳定策略学习，证明了从数据层面改进可有效从不完美数据中稳定学习并提升整体多任务性能。

Abstract: Offline multi-task reinforcement learning (Offline MTRL) heavily depends on the quality and distribution of pre-collected data. However, existing methods mainly focus on algorithmic optimization, with less emphasis on data-level improvements to enhance learning ability and generalization performance. This paper, from a data perspective, reveals three key bottlenecks that limit Offline MTRL performance:(i) ineffective utilization of prompts length under diverse task complexities, and (ii) semantic irrelevance of randomly sampled prompt segments, (iii) misleading supervision induced by fragmented and discontinuous trajectories. To address these challenges, we propose DaCe-DT, a robust offline MTRL framework designed to be insensitive to heterogeneous task complexities and data quality, featuring length-gated prompt masking (LGPM), retrieval-augmented prompt construction (RAPC), and value-adaptive return calibration (VARC). Together, these mechanisms enable DaCe-DT to deliver data-centric prompt adaptation and trajectory refinement, resulting in robust multi-task generalization and stable policy learning amid heterogeneous offline data and tasks. Experimental results on Meta-World show that DaCe-DT consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving an average improvement of 11.73% on optimal datasets and an improvement of 13.34% on suboptimal datasets, demonstrating its effectiveness in learning stably from imperfect data and improving overall multi-task performance.

</details>


### [78] [Cova-PINN: Cross-Domain Conservation Physics-Informed Neural Network for Fluid-Solid Conjugate Heat Transfer in Complex Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11108)
*Weizheng Zhang,Xunjie Xie,Hao Pan,Lin Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Cova-PINN 多域 PINN 框架，将守恒支撑与复杂几何中的热交互路径对齐，在局部尺度联合优化跨域复合控制容积平衡，并在全局换热器尺度施加配对壁面闭合条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准多域 PINN 在独立采样的域支撑上分别施加控制方程与界面条件，虽能得到看似合理的温度场，却导致端到端能量传递与出口温度不准确。多域 PINN 需在复杂几何下灵活建模介质特定表征以求解流体-固体共轭传热（CHT），因此需要一种能保证守恒性精度的框架。

Method: Cova-PINN 框架：1）局部尺度上联合优化跨域复合控制容积平衡；2）全局换热器尺度上施加配对壁面闭合条件。在四个三周期极小曲面（TPMS）换热器及一个几何上不同的 DualMS 设计上进行评估，与面向 CHT、面向优化及面向复杂几何的 PINN 基线在统一协议下对比。

Result: 相较于最接近的基线 MUSA-PINN-CHT，Cova-PINN 在四种 TPMS 拓扑上分别将平均出口温度误差与器件级闭合误差降低 37.7% 与 60.2%，同时提升全场与热负荷精度，并在 DualMS 上取得一致性增益。

Conclusion: 将守恒支撑与热交互路径对齐可有效提升多域 PINN 在复杂几何共轭传热问题中的端到端能量传递精度与出口温度预测能力。

Abstract: Multi-domain physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) flexibly model medium-specific representations to solve fluid--solid conjugate heat transfer (CHT). However, standard multi-domain PINNs enforce governing equations and interface conditions on separately sampled domain supports, which can yield plausible temperature fields but inaccurate end-to-end energy transfer and outlet temperatures. We propose Cova-PINN, a multi-domain PINN framework that aligns conservation support with thermal interaction paths in complex geometries. Cova-PINN jointly optimizes cross-domain composite control-volume balances at the local scale and paired-wall closure at the global exchanger scale. We evaluate Cova-PINN on four triply periodic minimal surface (TPMS) heat exchangers and a geometrically distinct DualMS design against CHT-specific, optimization-oriented, and complex-geometry PINN baselines under a common protocol. Relative to the closest baseline, MUSA-PINN-CHT, Cova-PINN reduces average outlet-temperature and device-level closure errors across the four TPMS topologies by $37.7\%$ and $60.2\%$, respectively, while also improving full-field and heat-duty accuracy, with consistent gains on DualMS.

</details>


### [79] [Dynamics as Code: On Model Compression via Dynamic System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11115)
*Fan Gao,Wei Su,Juntong Fan,Renfeng Peng,Hongyu Liu,Jinqiao Duan,Feng-Lei Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将动态系统压缩范式推广至四类DS家族，证明无理缠绕轨迹在Diophantine条件下构成ε-网，并借助KD-tree与坐标模板加速及离群点识别，在ResNet-18与Qwen系列上实现无需后训练的可控压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练神经网络规模持续增长，内存与算力约束使模型压缩成为部署前提；已有剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、低秩分解等方法，而将压缩重新表述为紧凑权重表示（用动态系统轨迹索引编码高维参数）这一范式尚需理论刻画与通用化扩展。

Method: 以无理缠绕为例，在Diophantine条件下证明有限轨迹M = O(ε^{-(d+ν)})个状态构成d维权重空间的ε-网，从而关联状态分辨率、解压误差与压缩比；提出统一的DS压缩框架，统合空间填充曲线（Hilbert、Peano、Morton/Z-order、Snake）、混沌系统（Lorenz）、同余与伪随机生成器（LCG、PCG）以及低差异序列（Halton）四类家族；引入KD-tree与坐标模板加速以扩展至大模型，并用离群点识别控制误差。

Result: 在ResNet-18与Qwen2.5-1.5B/Qwen1.5-7B上的实验表明，基于DS的压缩在无需事后重训练的情况下取得有竞争力的压缩比，解压误差可控且状态空间设计灵活。

Conclusion: DS-based压缩是一种具有原理性且实用的压缩方法，其理论保证与统一框架为模型压缩提供了新的可预测路径。

Abstract: The escalating size of pretrained neural networks has rendered model compression a prerequisite for deployment under stringent memory and compute constraints. With the irrational winding as an example, earlier work introduced a dynamic system (DS) paradigm that reconceptualizes compression as compact weight representation: high-dimensional parameters are encoded by the index of a trajectory produced by a dynamic system, from which the vector is recovered during decompression. This mechanism is fundamentally distinct from pruning, quantization, knowledge distillation, and low-rank decomposition. Along this direction, we prove that under a Diophantine condition, a finite trajectory of \(M = O(ε^{-(d+ν)})\) states in the irrational winding constitutes an \(ε\)-net over the \(d\)-dimensional weight space, thereby linking state resolution, decompression error, and compression ratio in a predictable manner. Furthermore, we propose a generalized DS-based model compression framework by unifying four DS families---space-filling curves (Hilbert, Peano, Morton/Z-order, Snake), chaotic systems (Lorenz), congruential and pseudo-random generators (LCG, PCG), and low-discrepancy sequences (Halton). Also, we introduce the KD-tree and coordinate-template acceleration to scale to large models as well as outlier identification to control the error. Experiments on ResNet-18 and Qwen2.5-1.5B/Qwen1.5-7B validate that DS-based compression achieves competitive compression ratios without post-hoc retraining, with controllable decompression error and flexible state-space design, establishing it as a principled and practical compression approach.

</details>


### [80] [Machine Learning Optimization for Enhanced OS Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11133)
*Jae Sung Kim,Spencer Ekeroth,Jeremy Neale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究被动操作系统指纹识别，提出OsirisML工具，在CIC-IDS2017数据集上利用nPrint预处理与XGBoost建模，随机划分下子集准确率达97.66%，整段捕获84.69%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作系统指纹识别可通过分析TCP/IP流量识别网络中的操作系统。作者希望验证被动识别方法在CIC-IDS2017这一大规模真实数据集上的有效性，并提供可复用的工具支持模型生成、重训练与测试。

Method: 使用CIC-IDS2017数据集（超过47GB的pcap文件及其对应操作系统标签）；提出命令行工具OsirisML，采用nPrint将流量预处理为表格数据，再以XGBoost进行机器学习建模。

Result: 随机划分训练/测试时，OsirisML在Friday捕获的下采样子集上准确率为97.66%，在整个Friday捕获上为84.69%；在无攻击的整个Monday捕获上准确率为73.83%，F-1分数为79.38%。

Conclusion: 被动操作系统指纹识别在真实大规模流量数据上具有可行性，OsirisML结合nPrint与XGBoost可在不同数据规模与场景下取得可用的识别性能，但在整体捕获及无攻击流量场景下性能仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Operating System (OS) Fingerprinting is a technique that can be used to identify a network's operating systems by evaluating network traffic in the form of TCP/IP packets. This research will explore the effectiveness of passively identifying operating systems on the CIC-IDS2017 dataset, a collection of over 47 gigabytes of pcap files with their corresponding operating systems. This research also proposes a new command line interface, OsirisML, which uses nPrint to preprocess the data into tabular data and XGBoost to apply ML to the data to generate, retrain, and test ML models. When packets are split randomly between training and testing, OsirisML models reach an accuracy of 97.66% on a down-sampled subset of the Friday capture and 84.69% on the entire capture. On the entire Monday capture, which contains no attacks, OsirisML reaches an accuracy of 73.83% and an F-1 score of 79.38%.

</details>


### [81] [QUILT: Rethinking Sparse-Attention Prefill through Shared Query Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11134)
*Zhenduo Zhao,Qihui Zhou,Mingcong Song,Zhiyi Chen,Chuangguan Ye,Fengfan Hou,Zequn Gong,Jing Li,Hongjie Si,Guoping Long*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QUILT是一种工作负载感知的稀疏注意力执行机制，通过联合处理相邻查询并复用共享KV条目，减少冗余访存与计算，在长上下文推理中实现显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏注意力内核独立处理每个查询，导致跨查询共享的KV条目被反复加载与反量化。作者观察到相邻查询所选择的KV条目存在大量重叠，因此存在跨查询复用的机会。

Method: 提出QUILT：联合处理相邻查询并复用共享KV条目。核心包括Shift-and-Compare Set Decomposition（SCSD），将不规则集合操作转化为适合现代加速器的规则数据并行原语，并与注意力计算流水线化以隐藏开销；级联共享（Cascaded sharing）在多个粒度上分层捕获复用；瓦片感知执行策略平衡共享粒度与硬件瓦片利用率，并选择性剔除低重要性的查询特定尾部以消除利用不足的瓦片。

Result: 在LongBench上使用GLM-5.3与DeepSeek-3.2，在张量并行与序列并行下评估。相比最先进的稀疏注意力内核，内核平均延迟最多降低55.1%，处理的KV数据最多减少55.9%，首token时延（TTFT）最多降低36.8%，精度退化可忽略。

Conclusion: 跨查询KV复用可有效缓解稀疏注意力的冗余访存与计算，QUILT在不损失精度的前提下显著降低延迟、KV数据量与TTFT，验证了工作负载感知执行机制在长上下文场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Sparse attention reduces the cost of long-context attention, but existing kernels typically process queries independently, repeatedly loading and dequantizing KV entries shared across queries. We observe substantial overlap in the KV entries selected by neighboring queries, creating opportunities for cross-query reuse. We present QUILT, a workload-aware sparse-attention execution mechanism that jointly processes neighboring queries and reuses shared KV entries to reduce redundant memory traffic and computation. QUILT introduces Shift-and-Compare Set Decomposition (SCSD), which transforms irregular set operations into regular data-parallel primitives suitable for modern accelerators, and pipelines SCSD with attention computation to hide its overhead. Cascaded sharing captures reuse hierarchically at multiple granularities. A tile-aware execution strategy balances sharing granularity with hardware tile utilization and selectively removes low-importance query-specific tails to eliminate underutilized tiles. We evaluate QUILT on LongBench using GLM-5.3 and DeepSeek-3.2 under both tensor and sequence parallelism. Compared with the state-of-the-art sparse-attention kernel, QUILT reduces average kernel latency by up to 55.1% and processed KV data by up to 55.9%, while reducing time-to-first-token (TTFT) latency by up to 36.8% with negligible accuracy degradation.

</details>


### [82] [ActiveMedAgent: Cost-Aware Trajectory Learning for Multimodal Medical Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11140)
*Weiwei Ma,Xiaobing Yu,Peijie Qiu,Jin Yang,Zhaoqi An,Xuanzhao Dong,Xiaoqi Zhao,Xiaofeng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ActiveMedAgent 将临床“由廉价到昂贵”的序贯检查逻辑引入多模态医学 AI：在冻结的视觉-语言模型上追踪诊断概率分布，用“每步诊断收益减成本”对证据获取打分，并离线训练轻量 MLP 控制器决定何时继续获取、何时终止。三个基准上优于无引导获取与全模态基线，并发现信息过载效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床诊断本质上是序贯的：只有在预期新证据能消解诊断不确定性时，医生才会从廉价检查升级到昂贵检查。现有多模态医学 AI 多采用“一次输入全部模态”的范式，忽略了检查的成本与顺序，导致不必要的开销与信息过载。

Method: 在冻结的、通过 API 访问的视觉-语言模型之上构建 ActiveMedAgent：维护候选诊断上的概率分布，对每次证据获取按“单步诊断效用减去成本”进行打分；基于这些带分数的轨迹离线训练一个轻量 MLP 控制器，学习何时请求更多证据、何时做出诊断承诺。

Result: 在三个常用基准上，基于轨迹的策略学习一致优于无引导获取基线和全模态基线。此外发现信息过载效应：在 175 个案例中，智能体使用更少通道即得出正确诊断，而全模态基线反而失败。

Conclusion: 成本感知的序贯证据获取策略优于全模态一次性输入；学会“省略什么”与学会“获取什么”同等重要，说明选择性获取与主动终止是多模态医学诊断的关键能力。

Abstract: Clinical diagnosis is inherently sequential: clinicians escalate from cheap to costly tests only when additional evidence is expected to resolve diagnostic uncertainty. We present ActiveMedAgent, a framework that brings this cost-aware sequential logic to multimodal medical AI. Given a frozen, API-accessed vision-language model, ActiveMedAgent tracks probability distributions over candidate diagnoses and scores each acquisition by its per-step diagnostic utility minus cost. A lightweight MLP controller is then trained offline on these scored trajectories, learning when to request additional evidence and when to commit. Across three commonly used benchmarks, trajectory-based policy learning consistently outperforms both unguided acquisition and full-modality baselines. Notably, we identify an information overload effect. In 175 cases, the agent produces a correct diagnosis with fewer channels while the full-modality baseline fails, showing that learning what to omit can be as important as learning what to acquire.

</details>


### [83] [Ranking Prior Alignment for Credit Risk Modeling: When Do External Priors Matter?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11146)
*Qiye Lu,Jiang Ji,Liang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Ranking Prior Alignment 框架，将外部排序先验（专家/教师模型/LLM）通过温度缩放 KL 散度损失蒸馏进任意评分模型，在冷启动信用评分场景下显著提升效果，且收益随数据量、模型容量、特征质量下降而增大（逆缩放规律）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融机器学习中的冷启动信用评分问题：新产品上线时标注违约数据稀缺、特征管道不成熟，模型需以小容量部署以避免过拟合。标准防御手段均在同一稀缺数据上运作，因此亟需一种基于领域知识的外部正则化来源。

Method: 提出 Ranking Prior Alignment，一种模型无关框架，通过温度缩放 KL 散度损失将外部排序先验蒸馏进评分模型。统一损失形式为 L = L_task + gamma(t)·KL(P_agent || P_model)，其中 gamma(t) 采用指数衰减调度。该框架统一了神经（MIL 注意力）与树模型（XGBoost 自定义目标）两类架构。推理阶段无需外部模型，树模型实现可容忍至 eta = 0.5 的标注噪声。

Result: 在超150万商户的工业数据集上，MIL 对齐在 3K bags 时取得 7/7 正评估单元（1 个 ID + 3 个 OOT + 3 个退化指标；OOT-1 峰值 ΔAUC = +0.020）；XGBoost 消融在 300 bags 时取得 9/9 正向指标。公开 Amex 数据集交叉验证 5/5 正折（平均 ΔAUC = +0.041）。四类模型族（MIL、XGBoost、LightGBM、逻辑回归）与四种教师架构验证了框架的模型无关性与先验来源无关性。

Conclusion: 对齐收益呈现逆缩放规律：数据丰度 N、模型容量 C、特征质量 Q 越低，收益越大，可帮助实践者判断何时值得投入先验标注。框架模型无关、先验来源无关，适用于冷启动信用评分。

Abstract: Cold-start credit scoring -- deploying models with scarce labeled data, weak features, or minimal capacity -- is a recurring problem in financial machine learning. When a new lending product launches, labeled default data is scarce, feature pipelines are immature, and models must be deployed with minimal capacity to avoid overfitting. Standard defenses operate on the same limited data; what is needed is a source of external regularization grounded in domain knowledge.
  We propose Ranking Prior Alignment, a model-agnostic framework that distills external ranking priors (from domain experts, teacher models, or LLMs) into any scoring model via a temperature-scaled KL divergence loss. The framework unifies neural (MIL attention) and tree-based (XGBoost custom objective) architectures through a single formulation: L = L_task + gamma(t) * KL(P_agent || P_model), where gamma(t) follows an exponential decay schedule. The method requires no external model at inference, and its tree-based instantiation tolerates annotation noise up to eta = 0.5.
  On an industrial dataset of over 1.5M merchants, MIL alignment achieves 7/7 positive evaluation cells at 3K bags (1 ID + 3 OOT + 3 degradation metrics; peak Delta AUC = +0.020 on OOT-1), and XGBoost ablation achieves 9/9 positive metrics at 300 bags. Cross-dataset validation on public Amex shows 5/5 positive folds (avg Delta AUC = +0.041). Four model families (MIL, XGBoost, LightGBM, Logistic Regression) and four teacher architectures show that the framework is both model-agnostic and prior-source-independent.
  We further observe that alignment gains exhibit an inverse-scaling pattern: benefits grow as data abundance N, model capacity C, and feature quality Q decrease, helping practitioners decide when to invest in prior annotation.

</details>


### [84] [Do LLMs Learn from Rewards in Context? : Rethinking the role of reward in In-Context Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11152)
*Minchan Kwon,Seunghee Koh,Sunghyun Baek,Minsung Bae,Junmo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过受控实验检验"上下文强化学习(ICRL)"是否真的实现了RL，发现奖励信号几乎不起作用，轨迹的提升效果也不依赖其语义内容，表明直接ICRL实质上是ICL的特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在推理时越来越多通过积累上下文经验（而非更新参数）来提升表现，这一过程常被称为上下文强化学习(ICRL)。但ICL能否真正扮演RL的角色此前从未被系统检验，本文旨在回答这一问题。

Method: 研究采用最简单的直接ICRL形式，即模型直接以原始"轨迹-奖励"对作为条件，检验奖励是否充当学习信号。在四个基准、六个模型上进行受控实验，通过翻转、随机化、移除奖励，以及使用显式指示探索、利用或推理奖励的元提示，考察奖励的作用；同时对轨迹进行打乱和破坏，检验其语义内容是否关键。

Result: 奖励确实被模型读取，但效应很小：翻转、随机化或移除奖励后，改进曲线几乎不变，即便在明确指示探索、利用或推理奖励的元提示下也依然如此。轨迹确实驱动了性能提升，但与语义内容无关——打乱或破坏的轨迹与真实轨迹效果相当。

Conclusion: 这些模式与ICL中的已知现象高度一致，说明直接ICRL更应被理解为ICL的一种特例，而非推理时RL。这一重新定位对智能体记忆设计有启示：输入分布和示范等ICL因素可能比奖励塑形、探索等RL要素更重要。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly improve at inference time by accumulating experience in context rather than by updating parameters. This process is often described as in-context reinforcement learning (ICRL). Whether in-context learning (ICL) can actually play the role of RL, however, has not been tested. We study this question in its simplest form, direct ICRL, where the model conditions directly on raw trajectory-reward pairs, and ask whether the reward acts as a learning signal. Through controlled experiments on four benchmarks across six models, we find that the reward is read, but its effect is small: flipping, randomizing, or removing the reward leaves the improvement curve almost unchanged, and this holds even under meta-prompts that explicitly instruct the model to explore, exploit, or reason over rewards. Trajectories drive improvement, but not through their semantic content: shuffled or corrupted trajectories work as well as real ones. These patterns closely mirror those known in ICL, suggesting that direct ICRL is better understood as a special case of ICL than as inference-time RL. This reframing has implications for agent memory design: ICL factors such as input distribution and demonstrations may matter more than RL elements such as reward shaping and exploration.

</details>


### [85] [RideBench: A Large-Scale Exogenous-Aware Benchmark for Ride-Hailing Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11164)
*Shengsheng Lin,Jing Hu,Zhengyang Hu,Jiazheng Sun,Zichun Cao,Siwei Sun,Zhichao Zou,Enyun Yu,Dongdong Li,Xinyi Hu,Weiwei Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发布大规模网约车时序数据集Ride-Hailing，并基于此构建外生感知预测基准RideBench，评测30余种方法，揭示现有模型与真实网约车预测需求之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实网约车预测需要应对天气扰动、节假日效应、大型活动冲击等外生情景，并支持跨异构区域与长时域规划；但现有模型与真实需求存在明显错配，缺乏统一的数据与基准来系统评估外生感知预测能力。

Method: 构建Ride-Hailing数据集：源自滴滴市场数据，覆盖200个空间区域、连续四年、半小时粒度，包含天气扰动、节假日效应、大型活动影响三类外生情景。基于该数据集提出RideBench基准，涵盖常规提前一周预测与最长提前8周、预测步长达2,688步的长时域预测，并评测30余种方法，包括仅内生模型、外生感知模型与时间序列基础模型。

Result: 未来已知外生变量在常规提前一周预测中带来明显收益，尤其在天气、节假日和大型活动情景下；但现有外生感知模型在复杂外部情境下仍难以充分捕捉扰动引发的模式变化。长时域预测中，现有模型无法同时实现低逐点误差、准确的宏观趋势与可靠的近期预测。

Conclusion: 现有预测模型与真实网约车需求存在明显错配，亟需能更好利用未来已知外生信息、跨异构区域扩展并支持长时域规划的模型。发布Ride-Hailing与RideBench旨在推动社区研究这些实际挑战。

Abstract: We release Ride-Hailing, a large-scale ride-hailing time series dataset synthesized from DiDi's marketplace data across 200 spatial areas. Ride-Hailing spans four consecutive years at half-hourly granularity and covers three representative exogenous scenarios: Weather Disturbance, Holiday Effect, and Large-scale Event Impact. Built upon Ride-Hailing, we introduce RideBench, a comprehensive benchmark for exogenous-aware ride-hailing forecasting, covering both regular week-ahead forecasting and long-horizon 8-week-ahead forecasting with up to 2,688 prediction steps. RideBench evaluates over 30 representative forecasting methods, including endogenous-only models, exogenous-aware models, and time series foundation models. Our results show that future-known exogenous variables provide clear benefits in regular week-ahead forecasting, especially under weather, holiday, and large-scale event (e.g., major sporting events and concerts) scenarios. However, current exogenous-aware models still struggle to fully capture disturbance-induced pattern changes under complex external contexts. For long-horizon forecasting, existing models cannot simultaneously achieve low pointwise errors, accurate broad trends, and reliable near-term forecasts. These findings reveal a clear mismatch between existing forecasting models and real-world ride-hailing requirements, highlighting the need for models that can better exploit future-known exogenous information, scale across heterogeneous areas, and support long-horizon planning. By introducing Ride-Hailing and RideBench, we aim to encourage the community to study these practical challenges in real-world ride-hailing forecasting.

</details>


### [86] [CARE: A Lightweight Plug-in Gated Correction and Uncertainty-aware Module for Long-term Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11165)
*Guo Cheng,Changlong Lv,Jingyi Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CARE，一种轻量级即插即用模块，可与任意确定性长时程预测骨干并行运行，通过历史上下文重采样对齐、残差修正和风险门控提升多变量长时程预测精度，并输出可解释的逐坐标可靠性信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量长时程预测在电力负荷调度、交通流管理和金融风控中至关重要，但现有确定性骨干模型仅输出单条轨迹，掩盖了不同时程与通道间异质的预测难度，且不提供局部化的可靠性信号。

Method: CARE与基础模型并行工作：重采样历史上下文以匹配预测时程，从对齐历史中学习残差修正模式，并施加由逐坐标sigmoid风险门调制的尺度感知有界更新；采用多目标损失联合优化预测精度、残差跟踪、风险对齐和基础模型锚定，无需重新设计架构。

Result: 在八个基准数据集和三种代表性骨干上，CARE以极小的参数量和延迟开销提升精度。其风险门能可靠识别高误差区域：在Weather数据集上，最高门控三分位组的误差约为最低三分位组的四倍，为规划者提供可解释的逐步信任信号。

Conclusion: CARE作为一种轻量、通用的修正分支，可在不改变原架构的情况下增强任意确定性预测器，兼顾精度提升与逐坐标可解释的风险估计，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multivariate long-horizon forecasting is critical to electricity load scheduling and traffic flow management, and to financial risk control. Existing deterministic backbones output a single trajectory, masking heterogeneous prediction difficulty across horizons and channels and providing no localized reliability signal. We present CARE (Corrective branch with Aligned context and Relative-error Estimation), a lightweight plug-in that enhances any deterministic forecaster without architectural redesign. Operating in parallel with the base model, CARE resamples historical context to match the forecast horizon, learns residual correction patterns from this aligned history, and applies scale-aware bounded updates modulated by per-coordinate sigmoid risk gates. A multi-objective loss jointly optimizes forecast accuracy, residual tracking, risk alignment, and base-model anchoring. Across eight benchmarks with three representative backbones, CARE improves accuracy with marginal parameter and latency overhead. Its risk gates reliably identify high-error regions: on Weather, the highest-gate tertile exhibits nearly four times the error of the lowest-gate tertile, offering planners an interpretable per-step trust signal. Code is available at https://github.com/CG-BNYC/CARE.

</details>


### [87] [PIVOT: Perplexity-Informed KD-to-RL Transition Scheduling for Vertical-Domain Few-Shot Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11167)
*Heng Li,Yong Zhang,Ning Cheng,Zhigen Li,Yun Zhu,Yanmeng Wang,Shaojun Wang,Jing Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 PIVOT 框架，依据教师评估的序列困惑度在 OPD 与 GRPO 间动态路由样本，在垂直领域小模型少样本分类上优于继续 OPD 与全局同步 OPD→GRPO 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 垂直领域少样本分类中，小语言模型因监督信号有限难以获取领域决策知识。现有 KD-to-RL 流程（OPD 到 GRPO）采用全局固定转换计划，忽略了不同样本对教师引导知识获取与奖励驱动精炼的需求差异。

Method: PIVOT（Perplexity-Informed Transition Optimization）：以教师评估的序列困惑度为路由信号，低困惑度样本转入 GRPO 进行奖励驱动精炼，高困惑度样本保留在 OPD 中继续领域知识获取。

Result: 在 Banking77 和 HWU64 上，相同 post-warm-up 学生优化步数下，PIVOT 稳定优于 continued OPD 与全局同步 OPD→GRPO 基线，取得更强下游性能与更稳定训练动态。

Conclusion: 以困惑度为依据的样本级动态转换调度，优于统一的全局转换策略，是 KD 与 RL 结合训练小模型的有效改进方向。

Abstract: Vertical-domain few-shot classification remains challenging for small language models, as limited supervision makes it difficult to acquire domain-specific decision knowledge. On-Policy Distillation (OPD) can improve teacher-guided adaptation by supervising student-generated rollouts, while GRPO-based reinforcement learning can further refine downstream predictions. However, existing KD-to-RL pipelines typically rely on globally fixed transition schedules, ignoring that different samples may require different amounts of teacher-guided acquisition before reward-driven refinement. We propose PIVOT (Perplexity-Informed Transition Optimization), a dynamic transition framework that routes samples between OPD and GRPO according to teacher-evaluated sequence perplexity. PIVOT moves low-perplexity samples to GRPO for reward-driven refinement while keeping high-perplexity samples under OPD for continued domain knowledge acquisition. Experiments on Banking77 and HWU64 show that PIVOT consistently outperforms continued OPD and globally synchronized OPD$\rightarrow$GRPO baselines under the same number of post-warm-up student optimization steps, achieving stronger downstream performance and more stable training dynamics.

</details>


### [88] [SACQ: Structured Decoding with Memory-Conditioned Refinement for Long-Horizon Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11170)
*Guo Cheng,Zhengzhuo Xu,Chenchen Jing,Jingyi Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SACQ结构化预测头，用两阶段解码替代扁平读出，并配合批自适应scaled log-cosh损失，提升长期时间序列预测精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期时间序列预测模型多以patch编码器加扁平读出（flatten readout）结尾，通过单一共享投影把全部历史记忆映射到所有未来步。这种对未来位置的隐式耦合模糊了位置特定的历史—未来对齐关系，并放大了对输入损坏与极端监督噪声的敏感性。

Method: SACQ是一种即插即用结构化预测头，保持编码器不变、仅替换扁平读出，采用两阶段解码：先构建粗粒度patch网格预测骨架，再通过跨注意力在历史记忆上细化每个未来位置，并用可学习的逐patch门控将注意力修正与粗骨架融合。同时提出批自适应scaled log-cosh损失，按当前残差尺度自动校准鲁棒性，抑制离群梯度同时保留对常见误差的类MSE敏感性。

Result: 在PatchTST、DLinear与patch-Mamba骨干上取得顶尖测试MSE/MAE，参数与延迟开销仅小幅增加；在推理期输入损坏与训练集标签噪声压力测试下显著优于扁平读出；消融实验验证了各架构组件的有效性。

Conclusion: SACQ以即插即用方式解决了扁平读出的位置耦合与鲁棒性不足问题，兼顾精度、鲁棒性与较低开销，具有良好的通用性与可移植性。

Abstract: Long-term time series forecasting (LTSF) models predominantly employ patch-based encoders terminated by a flatten readout head that maps the entire encoded historical memory to all future steps through a single shared projection. This implicit coupling of future positions obscures position-specific historical-to-future alignment and amplifies sensitivity to corrupted inputs and extreme supervision noise. We present SACQ, a plug-in structured prediction head that replaces flatten readout while keeping the encoder unchanged. SACQ adopts a two-stage decoding pipeline: it first establishes a coarse patch-grid forecast scaffold, then refines each future position through cross-attention over historical memory and merges the attention-derived correction with the coarse scaffold via a learned per-patch gate. To stabilize optimization under long horizons and noisy labels, we further propose a batch-adaptive scaled log-cosh loss that automatically calibrates robustness to the current residual scale, suppressing outlier gradients while preserving MSE-like sensitivity for typical errors. SACQ attains top-tier test MSE/MAE across PatchTST, DLinear, and patch-Mamba backbones with only modest incremental overhead in parameters and latency. Under inference-time input corruption and training-set label-noise stress tests, SACQ substantially outperforms flatten readouts, with ablation studies validating each architectural component.

</details>


### [89] [Predictive Multiplicity in Cell-Fate Assignment: Label-Free Rashomon Sets and the Limits of Per-Cell Certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11185)
*Arjun Bhupatiraju,Abhiram Bhupatiraju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FateMultiplicity框架在无谱系标签下构建Rashomon集，发现轨迹推断模型多重性普遍存在且由模型空间多样性主导，但基于该集合的逐细胞命运认证并不能带来更可靠的命运判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞轨迹推断将转录组测量映射到发育连续谱上，但拟合效果相当的多种模型配置可能给出相互冲突的细胞命运分配。作者希望量化这种模型多重性，并检验能否从无标签的模型集合中导出更可靠的逐细胞命运认证。

Method: 提出FateMultiplicity——无标签框架，在交叉拟合的留出基因上评估模型不一致性，通过以随机种子变异校准的非劣性检验，构建统计可容许的模型集合（Rashomon集）。以逐细胞认证命运裕度FM为判据，并与基线配置自身决策裕度、种子离散度基线在模拟真值上对比；同时考察将下确界放宽为q分位数、上确界等变体构造。

Result: 多重性较大且更依赖模型空间多样性而非规模：第二算法12种配置暴露20.0%细胞，第一算法24种仅暴露3.8%。FM并不能优于模型自身的拟合裕度：模拟真值上FM判别误分配AUC 0.682，基线决策裕度0.965（p=0.003），种子离散度基线0.854。信息量由容许集宽度而非基数决定：基数4时，种子重拟合0.933、超参数扰动集0.701。将下确界放宽到q分位数可恢复判别力但趋近单模型自信度；上确界达0.973，因theta*的归属从下方约束、而下确界无锚。两项构造保留：裕度侵蚀比在模拟中区分真假分支点（AUC 0.890，未在真实数据验证）；对克隆观测命运，未认证细胞与克隆结果不一致比认证细胞高16.4个百分点（p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 轨迹推断中的多重性值得测量和报告，但基于无标签Rashomon集的逐细胞认证并非获得更可靠命运判断的途径。裕度侵蚀比与未认证细胞的克隆不一致性两项构造显示出潜在价值。

Abstract: Single-cell trajectory inference maps transcriptomic measurements onto developmental continua, yet configurations that fit the data equally well can assign conflicting cell fates. FateMultiplicity is a label-free framework that constructs a statistically admissible model set, or Rashomon set, without lineage labels, by evaluating model discrepancy on cross-fitted held-out genes under non-inferiority testing calibrated against random-seed variation. Multiplicity is large and depends more on the diversity of the model space than its size: twelve configurations of a second algorithm expose 20.0% of cells where twenty-four of the first expose 3.8%. Whether the per-cell certified fate margin FM yields more reliable assignments than the fitted model already provides is then tested, and it does not. On simulation ground truth, on the same cells, FM discriminates misassignment at AUC 0.682, against 0.965 for the baseline configuration's own decision margin (p = 0.003) and 0.854 for a seed-dispersion baseline. Informativeness is governed by the breadth of the admitted set, not its cardinality: at cardinality four, seed refits give 0.933 and hyperparameter-perturbed sets 0.701. Relaxing the infimum to a q-quantile recovers discrimination but converges toward the single model's own confidence; the supremum reaches 0.973 because theta*'s membership bounds it from below, while the infimum is unanchored. Multiplicity in trajectory inference is worth measuring and reporting, but per-cell certification over a label-free Rashomon set is not a route to more reliable fate calls. Two constructions survive: a margin-erosion ratio separates real from spurious branch points in simulation (AUC 0.890, untested on real data), and against clonally observed fate, uncertified cells disagree with their clone's outcome 16.4 percentage points more often than certified cells (p < 0.001).

</details>


### [90] [Do Flatter Minima Drive Better Generalization? An Algorithmic Separation in Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11206)
*Mohnish Harwani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究平坦损失景观是否是驱动泛化转变（grokking）的因果机制，发现单独使用SAM不足以可靠诱发转变，但与权重衰减结合可加速泛化转变达4倍，并通过理论分析揭示了其机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平坦损失景观长期被认为与更好的泛化相关，但其作为泛化因果机制的地位尚不明确。Grokking现象提供了一个独特测试平台：模型倾向用非泛化结构拟合数据并长时间停留，仅在特定条件下才转向泛化。近期工作主张平坦性是这一转变的必要几何条件，本文旨在检验平坦性是否能作为驱动该转变的机制。

Method: 使用锐度感知最小化（SAM）偏向更平坦解，并将其与权重衰减等驱动泛化的机制结合进行实验；同时构建一个最小化的两层ReLU插值模型（包含记忆解与泛化解）进行理论分析，开展局部稳定性分析。

Result: 单独使用SAM尽管产生更平坦的解，仍不足以可靠诱发泛化转变；但SAM与权重衰减结合时，可将向泛化解的转变在epoch级别加速最多4倍。理论分析表明，即使在简单设置下，平坦性本身也无法区分记忆解与泛化解，而权重衰减偏向泛化解；在低范数区域存在一个局部稳定性窗口，其中记忆插值解在梯度下降下局部稳定但在SAM下不稳定，这解释了SAM加速转变的能力。

Conclusion: 平坦性并非泛化的充分因果机制；其作用需与权重衰减等机制结合才能有效驱动和加速泛化转变。本文提供了关于平坦性在驱动泛化中作用的更可解释说明，尤其适用于模型容易通过学习非泛化结构来最小化损失的场景。

Abstract: Flat loss landscapes have long been linked to better generalization in neural networks. However, its role as a causal mechanism for generalization is less established. Grokking provides an unique testbed to understand this distinction: models are prone to fit observed data using non-generalizing structure and remain in that regime for prolonged periods, transitioning to generalization only under particular training conditions. In this work, we study whether flat loss landscapes can act as a driving mechanism in this transition. While recent work has argued for flatness as a necessary geometric condition for this transition, we find that biasing training toward flatter solutions using sharpness-aware minimization (SAM) is insufficient to reliably induce this transition, despite producing flatter solutions. However, when SAM is paired with mechanisms that drive generalization such as weight decay, an interesting property emerges: SAM can accelerate the transition to generalizing solutions by up to 4x at the epoch-level. We theoretically untangle this relationship between SAM and weight decay using a minimal interpolating two-layer ReLU model with both memorizing and generalizing solutions. We show that even in this simple setup, flatness alone cannot distinguish a memorizing solution from a generalizing one, while weight decay favors generalizing solutions. However, under a local stability analysis, there exists a window where a memorizing interpolant is locally stable under gradient descent but unstable under SAM in the low-norm regime, which can explain SAM's ability to accelerate this transition. Overall, our results provide a more interpretable account of the role of flatness in driving generalization, especially in settings where models are vulnerable to minimizing loss through learning non-generalizing structure.

</details>


### [91] [Bridging KV-Cache Quantization and Linear Attention: From Theory to Pretrained Weight Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11214)
*Kaicheng Xiao,Liran Dong,Haotian Li,Guoliang Xing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出RAM-Net，通过离散地址空间上的软分配机制，桥接KV缓存量化与线性注意力，实现高效注意力并支持Transformer到RAM-Net的权重迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存量化将单个KV条目压缩为离散码但保留所有条目，线性注意力则将多个历史KV贡献递归聚合为固定大小连续状态但会引入干扰。二者的对比引出核心问题：能否在单一机制中同时实现逐KV压缩与多KV聚合。

Method: 将RAM-Net识别为上述桥接机制：通过离散地址空间上的软分配，决定对每个地址关联的连续槽状态进行递归更新。在受限RAM-Net构造下，证明软地址分配将硬量化匹配扩展为可分离的读写重叠，局部近似全注意力相似度并支持递归聚合。基于软量化中间构造，进一步实现Transformer到RAM-Net的权重迁移。

Result: 在0.3B至7B参数的九个预训练Transformer模型上，每个模型仅用500M token预算，在六项常识与知识任务中，RAM-Net平均恢复了教师模型相对随机猜测准确率增益的87.1%。

Conclusion: 软分配机制可统一逐KV压缩与多KV聚合，RAM-Net为高效注意力提供理论与实证支持，并通过权重迁移有效继承预训练Transformer能力。

Abstract: KV-cache quantization and linear attention are two representative approaches to tackling the storage and computational costs of Transformers. KV-cache quantization compresses individual KV entries into discrete codes but retains all entries, whereas linear attention recurrently aggregates multiple historical KV contributions into a fixed-size continuous state but can introduce interference. This contrast raises the question of whether per-KV compression and multi-KV aggregation can be bridged within a single mechanism for efficient attention. We identify RAM-Net as such a bridge through soft assignments over a discrete address space. These assignments determine recurrent updates to the continuous slot state associated with each address. Under a restricted RAM-Net construction, we prove that soft address assignments extend hard quantized matching to a separable read-write overlap that locally approximates full-attention similarity and supports recurrent aggregation. These connections further enable Transformer-to-RAM-Net weight migration through a new path based on a soft-quantized intermediate construction. Across nine pretrained Transformer models from 0.3B to 7B parameters, RAM-Net recovers an average of 87.1% of the teachers' accuracy gains over random guessing across six commonsense and knowledge tasks using only a 500M-token budget per model.

</details>


### [92] [The Lattice of Transition Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11216)
*T. Y. Tsui,Jiatao Gu,Lingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将扩散模型、自回归模型及介于两者之间的模型统一描述为“腐蚀格”（corruption lattice）上的路径，把并行解码调度的代价定义为其并行步骤所丢弃的依赖关系，进而提出可在解码前预测不同调度性能的设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型擅长连续场生成，自回归模型擅长离散token生成，近期混合模型试图结合二者优势，但每种混合模型的解码调度均按设计固定，缺乏对调度性能的事前预测依据。论文希望回答：在固定步数下，能否在解码前预测某一模型解码调度的性能。

Method: 作者将扩散、自回归及中间模型统一表示为一条腐蚀格上的路径，并将调度代价定义为并行步骤所丢弃的依赖关系。基于该代价，论文从数据几何角度分析零代价调度的最少步数：对于在图上马尔可夫且沿图路径依赖的数据，最少步数等于图的 treedepth，该量与序列长度呈对数关系、与网格边长呈线性关系。当步数少于 treedepth 时，任何调度都需付出正代价，作者利用从预训练权重估计得到的成对依赖核，在解码前预测不同调度的代价排序。

Result: 在文本生成、图像生成和视频生成任务中，论文验证了关于不同调度在不同指标与基准下排序的大部分预测结果，表明所提出的代价与依赖核能够有效预测解码调度性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为未来自回归模型、扩散模型及其混合模型的解码提供了设计原则，即通过腐蚀格上的路径与调度代价分析，可在解码前预测并选择更优的解码调度；代码已开源。

Abstract: Diffusion and autoregression (AR) have long been seen as different categories of generative models, with diffusion specialising in continuous fields and AR specialising in discrete tokens. Recent work seeks to combine the advantages of the two models, and each hybrid fixes its decoding schedule by design. In this paper, we ask whether the performance of decoding schedules of one model can be predicted before decoding at a fixed number of steps. We describe diffusion, AR, and models in between as paths on one corruption lattice, and define the cost of a schedule as the dependence its parallel steps discard. The cost shows that the fewest steps of a zero-cost schedule are set by the geometry of the data, in the same way for tokens and for continuous fields. In particular, for data that are Markov on a graph and dependent along its paths, the fewest steps equal the graph's treedepth, which is logarithmic in the length of a sequence and linear in the side length of a grid. With fewer steps than the treedepth, every schedule pays a positive cost, whose ranking we predict before decoding with a kernel of pairwise dependence estimated from pretrained weights. Across text generation, image generation, and video generation, we verify most of the predictions about the rankings of different schedules under different metrics and benchmarks. This work therefore provides a design principle for decoding for future AR models, diffusion models, and anything in between. Our code is available at https://github.com/TSUITUENYUE/The-Lattice-of-Transition-Laws.

</details>


### [93] [Multimodal Graph Retrieval-Augmented Sequential Recommendation via Collaborative Filtering Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11228)
*Jason Marcell Setiadi,Xin Cao,Lina Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MGRASRec：一种多模态图检索增强的序列推荐框架，直接将协同过滤信号注入MLLM提示，单次前向推理即可完成候选物品评分，在三个公开数据集上取得最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型（MLLM）序列推荐方法存在两方面不足：一是仅依赖目标用户自身交互历史，忽略了邻居用户的协同信号；二是需在长交互历史上反复进行MLLM推理，计算开销巨大。

Method: 提出MGRASRec，通过从用户-物品交互图中检索结构化路径，并经多模态相似度扩展以提升覆盖范围；将候选物品条件下的协同过滤信号直接注入MLLM提示。该检索过程同时以零额外成本定位与候选最相关的历史物品，从而免除循环式摘要，使每个候选仅需一次前向传播。所有组件统一为增强提示，用于MLLM的参数高效微调。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上的广泛评估验证了MGRASRec的有效性，在所有指标上取得最佳性能，尤其在排序质量方面提升显著。

Conclusion: MGRASRec通过图检索增强与协同信号注入，兼顾协同信息利用与推理效率，为MLLM序列推荐提供了一种有效且高效的方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated strong potential for sequential recommendation through their ability to reason over complex multimodal data. However, existing approaches either rely solely on the target user's own interaction history, neglecting collaborative signals from neighboring users, or incur substantial computational overhead through repeated MLLM inference over long interaction histories. To address these challenges, we propose MGRASRec, a multimodal graph retrieval-augmented framework for sequential recommendation. MGRASRec injects collaborative filtering signals conditioned on the candidate item directly into the MLLM prompt by retrieving structured paths from a user-item interaction graph, extended via multimodal similarity to increase coverage beyond exact co-interaction overlap. This retrieval also surfaces the history items most relevant to the candidate at no additional cost, removing the need for recurrent summarization and keeping inference to a single forward pass per candidate. All components are unified into an augmented prompt for parameter-efficient fine-tuning of an MLLM. Extensive evaluations across three publicly available datasets validate the effectiveness of MGRASRec, achieving the best performance on all metrics with particularly strong gains in ranking quality.

</details>


### [94] [SteerCast: Retrieval-Based Latent Steering for Decoder-Only Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11229)
*Van Dai Do,Huu Hiep Nguyen,Minh Hoang Nguyen,Hung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 SteerCast，一种基于检索的隐空间引导方法，在推理阶段提升解码器式预测器性能，无需更新参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测需从历史观测与辅助特征预测未来值；现有解码器式预测器已微调固定，希望在不重训练的前提下进一步提升其推理精度。

Method: 构建检索库：将每个历史窗口的表示与其“引导向量”（真实续段表示与模型自身预测表示之差）存入数据库。测试时对查询历史检索最近邻，聚合其引导向量，并在自回归生成每一步注入预测器隐藏状态。

Result: 在多变量基准与多个预测步长上，SteerCast 稳定优于微调主干模型和检索类基线，且除原微调外无需额外训练，仅用训练集作检索语料。

Conclusion: 通过检索训练集中的隐空间引导信号并在推理期注入，可在零额外训练成本下有效提升解码器式时间序列预测精度。

Abstract: Time series forecasting aims to predict future values from historical observations and auxiliary features. We propose \textbf{SteerCast}, a retrieval-based latent steering method that improves decoder-only forecaster at inference time, without updating its parameters. SteerCast constructs a database from the training set by storing a representation of each history window together with a \emph{steering vector} computed in the forecaster's latent space, defined as the difference between representations induced by the ground-truth continuation and by the model's own prediction. At test time, SteerCast retrieves nearest neighbors for a query history, aggregates their steering vectors, and injects the resulting signal into the forecaster's hidden states at every step of autoregressive generation, guiding predictions toward trajectories consistent with similar training cases. Experiments across diverse multivariate benchmarks and multiple horizons show that SteerCast consistently improves forecasting accuracy over the fine-tuned backbone and retrieval-based baselines, while requiring no additional training beyond the original fine-tuning and using only the training set as a retrieval corpus.

</details>


### [95] [Low-Cost Sensor Calibration for Indoor Air Quality Monitoring: A Dataset, Evaluation Scenarios, and a Lightweight Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11236)
*Jinyong Yun,Seokho Ahn,Hyungjin Kim,Sungbok Shin,Young-Duk Seo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了为期六个月、涵盖五个地点、包含低成本与参考传感器及上下文元数据多元室内空气质量测量数据集，定义四种评估场景，并提出结合输入窗口压缩与残差时序及特征融合的轻量级时序校准模型，在四种场景下均取得良好校准性能且边缘推理成本低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本传感器可实现可扩展的室内空气质量监测，但存在非线性失真、噪声与时间漂移问题，需要校准。传统严格成对校准设置要求在每个部署地点配置共址参考传感器，且未考虑时空异质性，限制了可扩展性。

Method: 构建六个月、五地点、多元室内空气质量测量数据集（低成本与参考传感器加上下文元数据）；基于该数据集定义四种评估场景：参考高效场景与位置迁移场景评估空间泛化，长期漂移场景与事件条件场景评估对渐变与突变分布偏移的鲁棒性；据此推导设计需求，并提出轻量级时序模型，结合输入窗口压缩与残差时序及特征融合。

Result: 实验表明，所提模型在四种评估场景下均取得较强的校准性能，同时边缘推理成本较低。

Conclusion: 该数据集与场景体系为低成本室内空气质量传感器的时空泛化与漂移鲁棒性校准提供了系统评估基准；所提轻量时序模型在低边缘推理开销下满足多场景校准需求，缓解了传统严格成对校准对共址参考传感器的依赖。

Abstract: Low-cost sensors enable scalable indoor air quality monitoring but require calibration because of nonlinear distortions, noise, and temporal drift. The conventional strict pairwise calibration setting requires a co-located reference sensor at each deployment location and does not account for spatial and temporal heterogeneity. To address these limitations, we introduce a six-month dataset comprising multivariate indoor air-quality measurements from low-cost and reference sensors with contextual metadata collected at five locations. Using this dataset, we define four evaluation scenarios. The reference-efficient and location-transfer scenarios evaluate spatial generalization, whereas the long-term drift and event-conditioned scenarios assess robustness to gradual and abrupt distribution shifts. Based on these scenarios, we derive design requirements and propose a lightweight temporal model that combines input-window compression with residual temporal and feature fusion. Experiments show strong calibration performance across all four scenarios with low edge-inference cost.

</details>


### [96] [Read What Matters: Query-Adaptive Quantization for KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11245)
*Siddharth Bhandari,Lucas Gretta,Krishna Balasubramanian,Shiva Kasiviswanathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReadKV 通过"渐进式编码 + 查询相关前缀分配"解耦 KV-cache 的保留位数与每次读取位数，在平均读 4 bit 的场景下将 C4 困惑度增幅控制在 0.66% 以内，并证明查询相关访问严格优于任何查询无关读取器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV-cache 条目在存储时其未来查询尚不可知，而每次解码查询对精度的需求位置又各不相同，二者存在结构性错配。现有方法通常以统一位宽存储并整体读取，无法在该错配下同时兼顾存储容量与每步读取带宽，尤其长上下文解码中每步搬运的 cache 字节（而非权重）成为主要开销。

Method: 将预算拆分为"保留位预算"与"每次查询读取位预算"两个独立维度。对每个 key 与 value 采用渐进式编码，其前缀可支持不同重建精度。查询到来时：先依据查询分配 key 通道前缀，用重建后的 key 计算注意力，再依据该注意力分配 value token 前缀；已存条目保持不变。每阶段在固定预算下优化经校准的失真目标，并在细化增益递减条件下证明分配的最优性，同时建立这些目标与注意力输出误差的联系。

Result: 在六个基座模型上，从 8-bit cache 平均读取 4 bit 使 C4 困惑度最多上升 0.66%，逻辑读取量约为 16-bit cache 的四分之一，保留容量约为其一半；相同读写预算下一致优于"存 4 bit 并整读 4 bit"。在两个指令微调模型的长上下文问答与检索任务上提供额外质量证据。在 NVIDIA A10G 的 8K token、batch-one、单层负载下，受限 8-bit ReadKV 读取器在 2 bit 平均读取预算下延迟较被测 TurboQuant 编解码器低 39%。理论上给出有限维注意力族，证明同读取预算下查询相关访问严格优于任何查询无关读取器，即使竞争编解码器不受限制。

Conclusion: 保留位数多于每查询读取位数的设计契合长上下文解码场景，可在显著降低每步 cache 搬运量与延迟的同时保持精度。查询相关、分阶段的渐进式读取在下游质量与效率上均有优势，并具备理论上的严格分离保证。

Abstract: KV-cache entries are stored before their future queries are known, but each decoding query needs precision in different places. We study this mismatch using separate budgets for retained bits and bits fetched per query. ReadKV stores each key and value in a progressive code whose prefixes support different reconstruction precisions. For each query, it allocates key-channel prefixes using the query, computes attention from the reconstructed keys, and then allocates value-token prefixes using that attention. Stored entries remain unchanged. Each stage optimizes a calibrated distortion objective under a fixed budget; we prove exact allocation under diminishing refinement gains and relate these objectives to attention-output error. We also exhibit a finite-dimensional attention family where query-dependent access strictly outperforms every query-independent reader at the same read budget, even with unrestricted competing encoders and decoders.
  Across six base models, reading four bits on average from an eight-bit cache increases C4 perplexity by at most 0.66%, using about one quarter of the logical reads and half the retained capacity of a 16-bit cache. It is consistently more accurate than storing and fully reading four bits at the same payload-read budget. Retaining more bits than each query fetches is aimed at long-context decoding, where the cache bytes moved per step, rather than the weights, dominate cost. Long-context question answering and retrieval on two instruction-tuned models provide additional quality evidence. On the tested 8K-token, batch-one, single-layer workload on an NVIDIA A10G, a restricted eight-bit ReadKV reader with a two-bit mean payload-read budget has 39% lower latency than the tested TurboQuant codec.

</details>


### [97] [Why On-Policy Distillation Sometimes Fails: Vanishing Learning Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11247)
*Lei Zhao,Qichao Zhao,Bowen Zuo,Qishi Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究on-policy distillation (OPD)的失败机制，发现大规模教师模型导致早熟损失平台，而自强化学习教师则避免此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管OPD能实现语言模型间的能力迁移，但其失败机制尚不明确，尤其在不同教师模型规模下表现出显著差异。

Method: 通过代码生成和数学推理实验比较不同教师（大规模教师 vs 自RL教师）的OPD效果；将OPD建模为小学习率极限下的理想化连续时间动力系统；使用训练日志诊断梯度学习信号代理；证明局部恢复保证；测量参数相对变化和线性CKA相似度。

Result: 大规模教师OPD在200次更新后平均最终损失仅降低25.1%，而自RL教师降低96.2%；观察到损失平台与梯度学习信号代理的早期下降相关；学生参数相对变化极小（0.025-0.098%），层间线性CKA>0.98。

Conclusion: 有限表征适应可能导致学习信号崩溃，但该假设仍需进一步验证；自RL教师的成功可通过局部恢复保证条件性解释。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) enables effective capability transfer between language models, yet the mechanisms underlying its failures are not fully understood. Across code generation and mathematical reasoning, OPD with larger-scale teachers exhibits early loss plateaus, with an average final loss reduction of 25.1% after 200 updates, compared with 96.2% for self-RL teachers, obtained by further reinforcement learning (RL) training of the initial student. To understand this difference, we analyze OPD as an idealized continuous-time dynamical system in the small-learning-rate limit. Our training-log diagnostics associate these plateaus with an early decline in a gradient-based learning-signal proxy while substantial loss remains; these measurements do not establish why the underlying gradient weakens. We further prove a local recovery guarantee for teachers sufficiently close to the initial student in a shared parameterization under regularity conditions, offering a conditional explanation for the success of self-RL teachers in our experiments. Across runs with and without loss plateaus, we observe small relative parameter changes (0.025-0.098%) and high similarity between the student's representations before and after OPD (linear CKA $>0.98$ across layers). These observations suggest that limited representation adaptation may contribute to learning-signal collapse, a hypothesis that remains to be tested. Code is available at https://github.com/leizhao7/opd-learning-signals.

</details>


### [98] [Neuro-Memory Fuzzy Inference System for Mimicking Human-like Car Following Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11252)
*Nazmul Haque,Md Asif Raihan. Md. Hadiuzzaman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: This study presents the Neuro-Memory Fuzzy Inference System (NeMeFIS), a hierarchical machine learning architecture that asymmetrically models acceleration and deceleration in car following behavior by integrating five human memory types procedural, working, episodic, semantic, and declarative. By linking external variables to memory functions via metaheuristics and validating them through factor and p-value analyses, NeMeFIS uncovers latent cognitive influences across Arterial, Collector, and Rural Highway corridors for different types of vehicles. Results from 54 different trained models emphasize cognitive thresholds shaped by driver perception limits and cognitive load. The trained NeMeFIS models outperform traditional statistical and conventional machine learning models in replicating realistic driving behavior, including comparisons with Linear Regression, ANFIS, and LSTM architectures. Fuzzy rule analysis reveals that declarative memory demands the highest rule, especially during deceleration, indicating complex braking decisions. Procedural memory drives acceleration, while semantic and declarative memory guide deceleration. Risk perception also emerges as a key factor, particularly on urban roads. Validated on both heterogeneous and homogeneous datasets, NeMeFIS offers a robust framework for modeling driver cognition. The findings support psychotherapeutic applications and the development of adaptive, human-like decision systems in Connected and Autonomous Vehicles (CAVs) to enhance traffic safety.

</details>


### [99] [Residual spectral instabilities in representation learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11257)
*Zhen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将变分自编码器中的维度级后验坍缩表述为围绕部分坍缩态的涨落理论，用负ELBO的有效自由能Hessian定义潜变量涨落的"质量矩阵"，推导坍缩方向的不变涨落扇区及其精确质量谱，给出再激活判据，并在数值延拓中验证谱边缘处的逐级维度坍缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习到的表示会逐级丢失潜在自由度，呈现一连串相变式坍缩，但其背后的稳定性原理尚不清楚；作者希望为VAE中维度级后验坍缩提供一个统一的稳定性/临界判据。

Method: 把负ELBO视作有效自由能，对其做二次展开得到高斯涨落理论；以Hessian作为潜变量涨落的质量矩阵，证明坍缩方向构成不变涨落扇区，并通过条件残差算子导出其精确质量谱，得到再激活方向降低自由能的判据（解码方差低于残差谱上边缘，取等号为边缘临界）。

Result: 在线性高斯VAE极限下，该判据复现主成分阈值；沿连续连通分支反向看，再激活边界给出逐次坍缩的局部判据；数值延拓实验显示潜维度在这些谱边缘附近依次丢失。

Conclusion: 结果支持一种"谱级联"解释：后验坍缩序列由幸存表示未能解释的残差信息所支配。

Abstract: Learned representations can lose latent degrees of freedom successively, suggesting a cascade of transitions whose underlying stability principle remains unclear. Here we formulate dimension-wise posterior collapse in variational autoencoder (VAE) as a fluctuation theory around partially collapsed states. Interpreting the negative evidence lower bound as an effective free energy, its quadratic expansion defines a Gaussian theory whose Hessian acts as a mass matrix for latent fluctuations. We show that the collapsed directions form an invariant fluctuation sector and derive its exact mass spectrum in terms of a conditional residual operator. A local reactivation direction lowers the free energy when the decoder variance falls below the residual spectral upper edge, with equality marking marginality. The criterion recovers principal component thresholds in the linear Gaussian VAE limit. Viewed in reverse along continuously connected branches, the reactivation boundary provides a local criterion for successive collapse. Numerical continuation experiments show successive loss of latent dimensions near these spectral marginalities. These results support a spectral cascade interpretation governed by residual information left unexplained by the surviving representation.

</details>


### [100] [How to post-train on a surrogate: Envelope sampling mitigates reward hacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11281)
*Sanjit Dandapanthula,Shuvom Sadhuka,Samir Khan,Michael Oberst,Aaditya Ramdas,Alexandra Chouldechova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"包络采样"(envelope sampling)，一种用于LLM裁判重校准的理论方法，通过最小化后训练模型后悔值的上界，缓解奖励劫持问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM后训练通常依赖LLM裁判等廉价代理奖励，因真实奖励(如人类偏好)查询成本过高。这常导致奖励劫持：针对失校准的代理进行强化学习会产生不良副作用。现有裁判重校准方法昂贵或依赖启发式，且当代理在稀有输出集上失校准时，on-policy采样会失效。

Method: 提出包络采样：一种有理论依据的裁判重校准方法，在假设人类奖励与重校准奖励位于裁判周围$L^2$球内的条件下，最小化后训练模型后悔值的上界。给出实用算法：通过拒绝采样或针对修改后的奖励进行微调来从包络中采样。

Result: 在临床笔记生成任务和受控谄媚(sycophancy)任务上的实验表明，基于包络样本的重校准能缓解奖励劫持，而基于基模型样本的重校准则无法做到。

Conclusion: 利用少量(n个)带真值标注的模型输出对LLM裁判重校准，包络采样在理论上和实证上均优于现有方法，有效缓解奖励劫持。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are commonly post-trained against LLM judges and other cheap surrogates because the true reward, such as human preference, is too expensive to query at scale. This practice often leads to reward hacking, where reinforcement learning against a miscalibrated surrogate leads to undesirable side effects. In this work, we study a setting in which a small number $n$ of model outputs are annotated with ground-truth labels (e.g., from expert review) and used to recalibrate the LLM judge before optimizing against it. Prior approaches to judge recalibration are costly or heuristic, and it is known that on-policy sampling fails when the surrogate is miscalibrated on a rare set of outputs. In this work, we propose envelope sampling, a theoretically-grounded method for judge recalibration that seeks to minimize an upper bound on the regret of the post-trained model under the assumption that the human reward and re-calibrated reward lie in an $L^2$ ball around the judge. We give practical algorithms to sample from the envelope by rejection or by fine-tuning against a modified reward, and experiments on clinical note generation and on a controlled sycophancy task show that recalibrating on envelope samples mitigates reward hacking where recalibrating on base-model samples does not.

</details>


### [101] [Memorization and Malign Generalization in Conditional Diffusion Models with Random Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11288)
*Gwangho Kim,Sungyoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在高维比例极限下分析随机特征条件得分模型，推导训练与测试损失的渐近表达式，揭示过参数化情形下的"恶性泛化"现象及条件信息量与记忆化的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件扩散模型能生成多样、新颖且高质量的样本，但其记忆化与泛化的理论理解仍然有限，现有工作主要在无条件设定下刻画这些行为。作者希望填补条件设定下理论分析的空白。

Method: 在高维比例极限下分析随机特征条件得分模型，推导训练损失和测试损失的渐近表达式，并对测试损失进行分解，以分别考察条件相关均值的预测与条件内预测方差。

Result: 通过测试损失分解表明，在过参数化情形下增加模型宽度会改善条件相关均值的预测，同时降低条件内预测方差，作者将这一现象命名为"恶性泛化"（malign generalization）。对训练损失的分析还表明，信息量更丰富的条件会在更小的宽度下导致对训练样本的记忆化。这些理论结论得到了在真实数据上使用U-Net架构的实验支持。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了条件扩散模型在过参数化条件下的泛化与记忆化机制：模型宽度增加可改善条件均值预测但减少条件内方差，更强的条件信息则促进更早的记忆化，为条件扩散模型的泛化理论提供了新的理解。

Abstract: Conditional diffusion models generate diverse, novel, and high-quality samples under prescribed conditions. However, theoretical understanding of their memorization and generalization remains limited, while recent works have characterized these behaviors primarily in unconditional settings. In this work, we analyze a random-feature conditional score model in the high-dimensional proportional limit, deriving asymptotic expressions for training and test losses. By decomposing the test loss, we show that in the overparameterized regime, increasing model width improves prediction of the condition-dependent mean while reducing within-condition prediction variance, a phenomenon we term "malign generalization." Furthermore, analyzing the training loss reveals that more informative conditions lead to memorization of training samples at smaller widths. These theoretical findings are supported by experiments with U-Net architectures on realistic data.

</details>


### [102] [From Geometry to Generalization: Why Row Normalization Can Beat Adam and Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11309)
*Jihwan Kim,Dogyoon Song,Chulhee Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明在高维多分类中,行归一化可取得比全批量Adam、随机重排Adam代理及精确SVD Muon更高的总体精度,并从几何角度解释其成因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同优化器在拟合同一训练数据时会选出几何结构差异显著的分类器,但这种差异是否可证明地影响总体(population)性能此前尚不清楚。

Method: 在各向同性高斯云数据模型下进行理论分析,比较行归一化的类间欧氏几何与Adam的逐坐标几何、Muon的谱几何;并将分析推广到类均值上全批量训练、类均值与测试噪声协方差独立取向的情形,以及类均值指数小于1的幂律谱;同时开展合成实验和末层语言模型实验。

Result: 行归一化严格优于全批量Adam、随机重排Adam代理和精确SVD Muon;优势源于其类间欧氏几何渐近保持总体决策边界方向,而Adam与Muon分别引入不消失的几何畸变;在重各向异性测试噪声下优势依然成立;当两类协方差均为对角且足够接近时,对Adam的优势可逆转,而对二者施加相同随机旋转可恢复该优势;合成与末层语言模型实验支持上述预测。

Conclusion: 优化器的隐式几何偏置可证明地影响高维分类的总体精度,行归一化因保持决策边界方向而具有优势,且该优势依赖于协方差结构与坐标轴对齐方式。

Abstract: Different optimizers can fit the same training data while selecting classifiers with substantially different geometries, but whether this difference provably affects population performance remains unclear. We show that row-wise normalization can achieve strictly higher population accuracy than full-batch Adam, a proxy for random-reshuffling Adam, and exact-SVD Muon in high-dimensional multiclass classification. Under an isotropic Gaussian-cloud data model, this advantage arises because row normalization's class-wise Euclidean geometry asymptotically preserves the population decision-boundary directions, whereas Adam's coordinate-wise geometry and Muon's spectral geometry introduce nonvanishing distortions. Beyond isotropy, the advantage persists for full-batch training on class means with independently oriented class-mean and test-noise covariances. It holds for power-law spectra with class-mean exponent below one, even under heavily anisotropic test noise. When both covariances are diagonal and sufficiently close, the advantage over Adam can reverse, while applying the same random rotation to both restores it by changing only their alignment with Adam's coordinate axes. Synthetic and last-layer language-model experiments support the predicted advantage.

</details>


### [103] [NP-Hardness of Minimizing Neurons in Two-Hidden-Layer ReLU Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11313)
*Sangrock Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明：对于两层隐藏层ReLU网络，在给定 L^p 逼近容差下，计算所需的最小隐藏神经元数目是 NP-hard 的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络架构优化的核心问题之一是：能否在多项式时间内计算出为逼近目标函数（在给定容差内）所需的最小隐藏神经元数量。本文针对两层ReLU网络回答该问题。

Method: 通过从 3-SAT 出发的多项式时间归约进行证明，构造由两层ReLU网络实现的紧支撑多面体锥台（frustum）函数，并建立盒-锥台函数有限线性组合在 L^p 下的稠密性。

Result: 对任意固定 d≥1、m≥1、1≤p<∞，精确计算最优隐藏神经元数是 NP-hard 的；即使目标由满足非零、逐分量非负、紧支撑、全局Lipschitz、连续分段仿射的有理ReLU网络表示，结论依然成立。归约产生架构间隙：不可满足公式的最优值为0，可满足公式的最优值至少为 d+2。

Conclusion: 在多项式时间内达到最小ReLU网络配置在计算上不可行，这为在ReLU网络设计中使用启发式逼近方法提供了理论依据。

Abstract: A fundamental question in neural network architecture optimization is whether the minimum hidden-neuron count required to approximate a target function within a prescribed tolerance can be computed efficiently. This paper resolves this question for two-hidden-layer ReLU networks under an $L^p(\mathbb{R}^d,\mathbb{R}^m)$ approximation constraint. For every fixed $d \ge 1$, $m \ge 1$, and $1 \le p < \infty$, we prove that computing the optimum exactly is NP-hard. The result holds even when the target is represented by a rational ReLU network whose realization is nonzero, componentwise nonnegative, compactly supported, globally Lipschitz, and continuous piecewise affine. The polynomial-time reduction from 3-SAT produces an architecture gap in which unsatisfiable formulas yield an optimum of zero, whereas satisfiable formulas yield an optimum of at least $d+2$. The proof constructs compactly supported polyhedral frustum functions realized by two-hidden-layer ReLU networks and establishes the $L^p$-density of finite linear combinations of box-frustum functions. The results offer theoretical justification for employing heuristic approximation methods in the design of ReLU neural networks, illustrating that attaining a minimal configuration within polynomial time is computationally unachievable.

</details>


### [104] [Deflating the Hessian: Rank-4 W4A4 Quantization for Multimodal Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11315)
*Shiwen Wang,Pengxiang Zhao,Xiaoming Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统一的低秩辅助 W4A4 PTQ 框架，将低秩补偿与残差量化耦合建模，用"去膨胀 Hessian"和激活噪声代理同时压制残差误差与激活量化误差，以低秩实现优于 SVDQuant 的量化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散 Transformer 的 4-bit 权重-激活（W4A4）训练后量化损失较大，低秩分支可缓解该问题：将权重分解为低位残差与高精度低秩分量。但现有低秩 PTQ 方法存在两类缺陷：一是将低秩补偿与残差量化分开优化，往往需要更高的秩；二是依赖二阶权重更新，却未显式建模激活量化误差，而该误差在 4-bit 下尤为突出。因此需要一种统一建模两者耦合关系的量化方法。

Method: 将低秩辅助的 W4A4 PTQ 建模为耦合标定问题，从联合目标推导基于优化的求解器。消去输出侧低秩因子后得到"去膨胀 Hessian"（deflated Hessian），使已被低秩分量捕获的残差误差被折减；同时引入激活噪声代理项以抑制激活量化误差。

Result: 在五个扩散骨干网络上，rank-4 方法在 PSNR 与 LPIPS 上稳定优于 rank-4 SVDQuant；在 SANA-1.6B、FLUX.1-schnell 与 FLUX.1-dev 上超越 rank-32 SVDQuant，且秩小 8 倍、量化速度最高快 6.25 倍；在 Qwen3-8B 上，rank-4 方法将 MMLU 准确率从 61.50% 提升至 68.17%（对比 rank-32 SVDQuant）。

Conclusion: 以"耦合标定"统一建模低秩补偿与残差量化，并显式引入激活量化误差抑制，可在显著更低秩与更低量化成本下取得更优的 W4A4 性能，验证了联合优化对低秩 PTQ 的有效性。

Abstract: In diffusion transformers, low-rank branches can mitigate 4-bit weight--activation (W4A4) post-training quantization (PTQ) loss by decomposing each weight into a low-bit residual and a high-precision low-rank component. Existing low-rank PTQ approaches, however, either optimize low-rank compensation and residual quantization separately, often requiring higher ranks, or rely on second-order weight updates without explicitly modeling activation quantization error, which becomes particularly pronounced under 4-bit quantization. To address these limitations, we present \method{}, a unified framework modeling low-rank-assisted W4A4 PTQ as a coupled calibration problem and deriving optimization-based solvers from the joint objective. Eliminating the output-side low-rank factor yields a \emph{deflated Hessian} that discounts residual errors already captured by the low-rank component, while an activation-noise surrogate is incorporated to suppress activation quantization error. Across five diffusion backbones, rank-4 \method{} consistently outperforms rank-4 SVDQuant in PSNR and LPIPS. It further surpasses rank-32 SVDQuant on SANA-1.6B, FLUX.1-schnell, and FLUX.1-dev with an $8\times$ smaller rank and up to $6.25\times$ faster quantization. Furthermore, on the Qwen3-8B LLM, rank-4 \method{} improves MMLU accuracy from 61.50\% to 68.17\% over rank-32 SVDQuant. Overall, \method{} achieves better W4A4 performance with substantially lower rank and quantization cost.

</details>


### [105] [Sample-Efficient Generative Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11349)
*Minxing Zheng,Shixiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CASA（Conformal Adaptive Sample Allocation），通过刻画额外样本的边际价值，在边际覆盖与平均采样预算约束下跨输入自适应分配采样，显著缩小生成式保形预测的不确定集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式保形预测依赖条件生成器采样的样本构建不确定集，采样预算需高效利用（如大扩散模型、科学模拟器采样昂贵）。现有方法对所有输入使用相同采样数，在响应分布简单处浪费样本、复杂处欠采样，导致集合膨胀且部分输入覆盖不足。

Method: 将问题形式化为在边际覆盖和平均采样预算约束下最小化期望集合大小，并通过刻画"额外样本的边际价值"来指导跨输入的自适应样本分配。

Result: 理论分析表明：在相同预算下，自适应分配比固定采样数产生更小的集合——遗漏一个模态会强制半径跨越模态间间隙，即使使用oracle半径也无法弥补。在合成与真实任务上，CASA在相同预算下产生显著更小的集合，常改善条件覆盖，且与现有半径自适应方法互补。

Conclusion: 自适应样本分配是提升生成式保形预测采样效率的有效策略，CASA在理论与实验上均优于固定采样数方案，并能与现有半径自适应方法结合使用。

Abstract: Generative conformal prediction builds uncertainty sets from samples of a conditional generator, which are efficient only when the samples represent the response distribution well. This can require many samples, each of which can be costly, as in large diffusion models and scientific simulators, so the sampling budget must be used efficiently. Existing methods draw the same number of samples at every input, wasting samples where the response distribution is simple and undersampling where it is complex, which inflates sets and leaves those inputs under-covered. We propose CASA (Conformal Adaptive Sample Allocation), which characterizes the marginal value of an additional sample and allocates samples across inputs to minimize the expected set size subject to marginal coverage and an average sampling budget. Theoretical analysis shows that adaptive allocation yields smaller sets than a fixed count at the same budget: a missed mode forces a radius that spans the gap between modes, and even oracle radius cannot compensate for it. On synthetic and real tasks, CASA produces substantially smaller sets at the same budget, often improves conditional coverage, and complements existing radius-adaptive methods.

</details>


### [106] [Bernoulli Flow Models: Self-Consistent Generative Modeling for Binary Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11362)
*Hao Mo,Liying Yang,Shumin Yao,Xinxing Yu,Ajian Liu,Xudong Mao,Yanyan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Bernoulli Flow Models (BFM)，用统一连续全局 Bernoulli 概率流替代离散一步马尔可夫链，可解析地在任意时间区间上求后验转移，从而在低 NFE 下保持高质量生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二值扩散模型生成高质量样本需大量函数评估（NFE），推理代价高。现有方法用单步似然近似多步似然，在低 NFE 跳步采样时严重降低样本质量，且离散链存在训练-推理不匹配。

Method: 不依赖顺序一步马尔可夫扩散链，定义数据分布与纯噪声之间的统一连续全局 Bernoulli 概率流路径，并推导任意时间区间上的解析闭式后验转移。降低 NFE 只需在新时间网格上重新评估解析后验，而非跳过离散步的近似。

Result: BFM 对激进 NFE 缩减高度稳健：LSUN Churches 256x256 上，256 步训练的 BFM 仅用 16 步采样达到 FID 9.22，而最先进离散基线退化至 204.10；在标准全步推理下也与连续和离散生成基线具竞争力。

Conclusion: BFM 是一种理论严谨、自洽且实用的快速二值数据生成框架，消除了离散链固有的训练-推理不匹配，实现自洽的低 NFE 采样。

Abstract: Binary diffusion models typically require a large number of function evaluations (NFEs) to generate high-quality samples, making practical inference computationally expensive. Reducing NFEs while preserving sample quality without distillation or additional training remains a significant challenge. Existing binary diffusion models define a discrete one-step forward path and then derive the reverse posterior. In low-NFE settings requiring cross-step sampling, they approximate the true multi-step likelihood with a single-step likelihood transition, which severely degrades sample quality. To address this fundamental limitation and decouple the generative dynamics from fixed discrete time steps, we propose Bernoulli Flow Models (BFM).
